政策法规国土安全部 (DHS)重要#职业移民与工卡#难民·庇护·TPS#执法·遣返·欺诈

DHS拟收紧对假释及监管释放外国人的自由裁量工作许可资格

发布日期 2026年6月5日

美国国土安全部(DHS)提议限制并明确对因人道主义获准假释入境者、获得暂缓执行(deferred action)者,以及在最终驱逐令下处于监管释放(order of supervision)状态者的自由裁量工作许可(EAD)资格。该提案尚未定稿,也未生效。公众可在2026年8月4日前提交书面意见。

要点

  • 该规则目前仅为拟议规则,尚未最终确定或生效。
  • 公众意见提交截止日期为2026年8月4日。
  • 除极少数例外情形外,取消最终驱逐令下处于监管释放状态的外国人申请工作许可的资格。
  • 收紧假释人员和暂缓执行对象的资格条件,要求其证明经济必要性。
  • 要求所有申请自由裁量工作许可者提交生物识别信息并通过身份核实。
  • 对曾被逮捕、起诉或定罪,或承认实施暴力犯罪,或有证据显示与帮派或恐怖组织有关联者,除非存在重大公共利益(如协助执法),否则不予批准工作许可。
  • 续签申请人必须受雇于或正在寻求受雇于参与E-Verify系统的雇主。

影响对象

因人道主义原因获准假释入境者、获得暂缓执行身份者,以及最终驱逐令下处于监管释放状态并申请或持有自由裁量工作许可的外国人。

建议行动

受影响的个人和雇主可在2026年8月4日前通过regulations.gov网站,引用DHS案卷编号USCIS-2026-0067提交书面意见。

全文翻译

美国国土安全部
Department of Homeland Security

机构:
AGENCY:
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS),国土安全部(DHS)。
U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services, DHS.
行动:
ACTION:
拟议规则制定通知。
Notice of proposed rulemaking.
摘要:
SUMMARY:
美国国土安全部拟限制并明确以下外国人享有酌情就业授权的资格:因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被临时假释进入美国的外国人、已获准延缓执行的外国人,以及存在最终驱逐令但因监管令而被临时释放的外国人。国土安全部进一步提议明确规定,申请就业授权的外国人若承认实施、已被逮捕或已被定罪于某些犯罪行为,则不应获得有利的自由裁量处理,除非存在重大的相抵触的公共利益,其中可能包括协助美国境内的执法活动。
The Department of Homeland Security proposes to limit and clarify eligibility for discretionary employment authorization for aliens paroled into the United States temporarily for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit, who have been granted deferred action, or against whom a final order of removal exists and who are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision. DHS further proposes to specify that aliens applying for employment authorization who admit to committing, have been arrested for, or have been convicted of certain criminal acts do not warrant a favorable exercise of discretion unless there are significant countervailing public interests, which may include assisting law enforcement activity in the United States.

DATES:

Comments on the information collection described in the “Paperwork Reduction Act” section of this proposed rule must be received on or before August 4, 2026. The electronic Federal Docket Management System will accept comments prior to midnight eastern time at the end of that day.

Comments submitted in a manner other than the one listed above, including emails or letters sent to DHS or USCIS officials, will not be considered comments on the proposed rule and may not receive a response from DHS. Please note that DHS and USCIS will not accept any comments that are hand-delivered, couriered, or sent by mail. 一、公众参与
I. Public Participation
美国国土安全部(以下简称“国土安全部”或“DHS”)邀请所有利益相关方通过提交书面数据、意见、评论和论据的方式参与本规则制定,内容可涉及本拟议规则的所有方面。国土安全部也欢迎就本拟议规则可能产生的经济、环境或联邦制影响提交评论意见。评论意见必须以英文提交,如非英文,须附英文译本。对美国公民及移民服务局(以下简称“USCIS”)实施本次修改最有帮助的评论意见,应引用拟议规则的具体部分,说明建议修改的理由,并提供支持该建议修改的数据、信息或依据。以上述方式之外的方式提交的评论意见,包括发送给国土安全部或USCIS官员的电子邮件或信函,将不被视为对本拟议规则的评论意见,国土安全部可能不会予以答复。
The Department of Homeland Security (DHS) invites all interested parties to participate in this rulemaking by submitting written data, views, comments and arguments on all aspects of this proposed rule. DHS also invites comments that relate to the economic, environmental, or federalism effects that might result from this proposed rule. Comments must be submitted in English, or an English translation must be provided. Comments that will provide the most assistance to U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services (USCIS) in implementing these changes will reference a specific portion of the proposed rule, explain the reason for any recommended change, and include data, information, or authority that support such recommended change. Comments submitted in a manner other than the one listed above, including emails or letters sent to DHS or USCIS officials, will not be considered comments on the proposed rule and may not receive a response from DHS.
提交说明:如果您提交评论意见,必须注明机构名称(美国公民及移民服务局)以及本次规则制定的国土安全部案卷编号USCIS-2026-0067。无论采用何种方式提交评论意见或材料,所有提交内容均将原样发布于联邦电子规则制定门户网站(http://www.regulations.gov),并将包含您提供的任何个人信息。因此,提交此类信息即表示将其公开。您在向国土安全部提交任何自愿性公开评论意见时,可考虑限制所提供个人信息的数量。国土安全部可自行决定对其认定可能影响个人隐私或具有冒犯性的评论内容不予公开展示。欲了解更多信息,请阅读http://www.regulations.gov上提供的《隐私与安全声明》。
Instructions: If you submit a comment, you must include the agency name (U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services) and the DHS Docket No. USCIS-2026-0067 for this rulemaking. Regardless of the method used for submitting comments or material, all submissions will be posted, without change, to the Federal eRulemaking Portal at http://www.regulations.gov, and will include any personal information you provide. Therefore, submitting this information makes it public. You may wish to consider limiting the amount of personal information that you provide in any voluntary public comment submission you make to DHS. DHS may withhold information provided in comments from public viewing that it determines may impact the privacy of an individual or is offensive. For additional information, please read the Privacy and Security Notice available at http://www.regulations.gov.
案卷:如需查阅案卷、阅读背景文件或已收到的评论意见,请访问http://www.regulations.gov,并注明国土安全部案卷编号USCIS-2026-0067。您也可以在在线案卷中注册接收电子邮件提醒,以便在有评论意见发布或最终规则发布时获得通知。
Docket: For access to the docket and to read background documents or comments received, go to http://www.regulations.gov, referencing DHS Docket No. USCIS-2026-0067. You may also sign up for email alerts on the online docket to be notified when comments are posted or a final rule is published.
二、执行摘要
II. Executive Summary
(一)监管行动的目的
A. Purpose of the Regulatory Action
国土安全部(DHS)拟根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11)(“(c)(11)”)对因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被暂准入境美国的外国人[1]限定并明确酌情就业授权的资格,并根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)(“(c)(14)”)对获得暂缓遣返的外国人[2]限定并明确酌情就业授权的资格。国土安全部还拟取消8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)(“(c)(18)”)项下的酌情就业授权资格,该条款适用于已有最终驱逐或遣返令但因监管令而暂时获释的外国人[3],但保留一项狭窄的例外情形。此外,国土安全部拟为就业授权增加自动终止条件及触发事件。拟议规则还将要求上述类别的外国人证明其就业的经济必要性,并证明其符合酌情给予有利裁量的条件。国土安全部还拟要求申请更新或后续就业授权的此类外国人必须受雇于参与E-Verify(由美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)管理的电子就业资格核验计划)的雇主,或正在寻求由该类雇主雇用。国土安全部还拟明确,所有根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)申请就业授权的外国人均须提交生物特征信息,在签发任何就业授权之前必须核实外国人的身份,并且一般而言,外国人曾被逮捕[4]、起诉或被判犯有任何刑事罪行,或曾承认实施暴力或危险犯罪,或有证据表明该外国人为帮派或恐怖组织成员的情形,不符合酌情给予有利裁量的条件,除非存在重大的相反公共利益考量,例如该外国人在美国协助执法活动。
DHS proposes to limit and clarify eligibility for discretionary employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11)(“(c)(11)”), for aliens paroled into the United States temporarily for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit,[1] and for discretionary employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)(“(c)(14)”), for aliens granted deferred action.[2] DHS also proposes to eliminate, with one narrow exception, discretionary employment authorization eligibility under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)(“(c)(18)”), for aliens against whom a final order of deportation or removal exists and who are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision.[3] Additionally, DHS proposes to add automatic termination conditions for employment authorization with triggering events. The proposed rule will also require that aliens in these categories establish their economic necessity for employment and establish they warrant a favorable exercise of discretion. DHS is also proposing to require aliens applying for renewal or subsequent requests of employment authorization in these categories be employed by or seeking employment with an employer who participates in E-Verify, the electronic employment eligibility verification program administered by USCIS. DHS also proposes to clarify that all aliens applying for employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c) must submit biometrics, that an alien's identity must be validated before issuing any employment authorization, and that, generally, situations where aliens have been arrested,[4] indicted, or convicted of any criminal act, or who have admitted to committing a violent or dangerous crime, or for whom evidence exists that the alien is a member of a gang or terrorist organization, do not warrant a favorable exercise of discretion, unless there are significant countervailing public interests, which may include the presence of the alien in the United States to assist law enforcement activity in the United States.
此外,国土安全部正在明确,根据实施美国在《禁止酷刑和其他残忍、不人道或有辱人格的待遇或处罚公约》(CAT)第三条项下义务的法规而获得暂缓遣返的外国人,有资格依照8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)申请酌情就业授权。根据CAT获得暂缓遣返并不会自动获得就业授权。此类外国人可以申请就业授权,但美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)保留根据8 CFR(印刷页第34354页)274a.12(c)(18)认定其资格的权力和裁量权,前提是该外国人符合酌情给予有利裁量的条件。
Additionally, DHS is clarifying that aliens granted deferral of removal based on regulations implementing the United States' obligations under Article 3 of the Convention Against Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment (CAT) are eligible to apply for discretionary employment authorization in accordance with 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18). Employment authorization will not be automatic upon the grant of deferral of removal under CAT. Such aliens may apply for employment authorization, but USCIS retains the authority and discretion to determine their eligibility under 8 CFR ( printed page 34354) 274a.12(c)(18) if the alien warrants a favorable exercise of discretion.
这些拟议的修改和澄清是对第14159号行政命令(E.O.)“保护美国人民免受入侵”[5]的回应,旨在确保美国人民的持续安全与保障以及我国移民制度的完整性。国土安全部力求确保对外国人酌情给予就业授权的任何决定均符合国土安全部根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)所承担的义务,即逮捕、拘留并及时将任何刑事外国人、对国家安全或公共安全构成威胁的外国人,以及依据《移民与国籍法》不可入境、可被驱逐或不符合救济资格的外国人驱逐出美国。国土安全部还力求确保其规则与本届政府减少非法移民以及减少外国人试图在国会为外国人合法移民美国所提供的全面制度之外获取移民福利的激励措施的努力保持一致。执法对于移民制度的完整性至关重要。它保护美国国家安全,并确保只有那些依法具备资格且合法居留在美国的人才能享有《移民与国籍法》项下的任何福利和特权。
These proposed changes and clarifications are responsive to Executive Order (E.O.) 14159, “Protecting the American People Against Invasion” [5] to ensure the continued safety and security of the American people and the integrity of our immigration system. DHS seeks to ensure that any discretionary grant of employment authorization to aliens is consistent with DHS's obligations under the INA to apprehend, detain, and promptly remove from the United States any criminal aliens, aliens who are a threat to national security or public safety, and aliens who are inadmissible or deportable or otherwise ineligible for relief under the INA. DHS also seeks to ensure that its rules are aligned with the Administration's efforts to reduce illegal immigration and the incentives for aliens to try to obtain immigration benefits outside of the comprehensive scheme Congress has provided for aliens to legally immigrate to the United States. Enforcement is essential to the integrity of the immigration system. It protects U.S. national security and ensures that only those who are legally qualified and lawfully in the United States are allowed to avail themselves of any benefits privileges under the INA.
根据(c)(18)类别签发的就业授权适用于因接受监管令(OSUP)而被美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)暂时解除羁押的外国人。该监管令允许外国人在等待驱逐出境或遣返期间留在美国,前提是由于外国人指定的所有国家拒绝接收该外国人,或依照该法第241条(美国法典第8编第1231条)拒绝接收该外国人而无法将其遣返,或者由于遣返该外国人在其他方面不可行或有悖公共利益。在依照8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)审理就业授权申请时,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)历来对所有已获得最终驱逐令并根据监管令获释的外国人授予该项福利,而未对该外国人是否因其指定的所有国家拒绝接收,或依照该法第241条拒绝接收,或因遣返不可行或有悖公共利益而确实无法被遣返进行个案评估。仅因外国人在获得驱逐令后根据监管令从ICE羁押中获释,而未经个案评估即授予就业授权,会损害移民制度的完整性,因为这可能促使外国人倾向于继续留在美国,而不是遵守其驱逐令、配合ICE迅速获取旅行证件并离境。
Employment authorization issued under the (c)(18) category is for aliens temporarily released from U.S. Immigration and Customs Enforcement (ICE) custody on orders of supervision (OSUP), which allow aliens to remain in the United States while awaiting deportation or removal when they cannot be removed due to the refusal of all countries designated by the alien or under section 241 of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1231, to receive the alien, or because the removal of the alien is otherwise impracticable or contrary to the public interest. When adjudicating employment authorization applications under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18), USCIS has historically granted the benefit to any alien with a final order of removal released on an order of supervision without conducting an individualized assessment of whether the alien cannot be removed due to the refusal of all countries designated by the alien or under section 241 of the Act to receive the alien or because removal is impracticable or contrary to the public interest. Granting employment authorization solely because the alien was released from ICE custody on an order of supervision after an order of removal without conducting an individualized assessment undermines the integrity of the immigration system as it can incentivize aliens to remain in the United States rather than complying with their removal orders, cooperating with ICE in swiftly obtaining travel documents, and departing the United States.
此外,除一项有限例外情形外,国土安全部(DHS)取消了对曾被逮捕、被指控、被起诉或被定罪犯有任何刑事罪行,或承认实施暴力或危险犯罪的外国人的自由裁量性就业授权,希望借此遏制犯罪行为的发生,并打消此类危险外国人继续留在美国的念头。DHS预计,这对打算重新申请就业授权的外国人尤其有效。除非DHS认定存在重大的相抗衡公共利益(可能包括协助美国境内的执法活动),否则这些外国人不应获得就业授权方面有利的自由裁量处理。
Furthermore, by eliminating, with one limited exception, discretionary employment authorization for an alien who has been arrested for, charged with, indicted for, or convicted of any criminal act, or who admits to committing a violent or dangerous crime, DHS hopes to deter the commission of crime and disincentivize such dangerous aliens from remaining in the United States. DHS anticipates this will be especially effective for aliens who intend to reapply for employment authorization. These aliens would not warrant a favorable exercise of discretion for employment authorization unless DHS has determined there are significant countervailing public interests, which may include assisting law enforcement activity in the United States.
本规则明确了在以下三种情形下自由裁量性批准就业授权的要求:根据(c)(11)条款,适用于因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而暂时假释入境美国的外国人;根据(c)(14)条款,适用于已获得暂缓执行行动的外国人;根据(c)(18)条款,适用于已存在最终驱逐令或遣返令并因监管令而暂时解除羁押的外国人。本规则将要求这些外国人证明其应获得有利的自由裁量处理。本规则还要求上述三类外国人证明其经济必要性。通过这样做,DHS促进了政策的一致性,相较于现状有所不同。目前的现状仅要求已获得暂缓执行行动批准((c)(14))的外国人或持有最终遣返令((c)(18))的外国人证明就业授权的经济必要性。这项拟议变更还将确保只有具有经济工作需要的外国人才有资格在这些类别中获得自由裁量性就业授权,并最大限度地降低对美国工人造成不利影响的潜在风险。不具有就业经济需要的外国人将没有资格在这些类别中获得就业授权及就业授权文件(EAD)。此前DHS并未要求这些类别下的所有外国人证明经济必要性,现在将一致要求其证明该必要性。DHS将在表格说明及其他次级监管指南中就可用于证明此类必要性的文件提供指导。
The rule clarifies the requirements for discretionary grants of employment authorization under (c)(11) for aliens paroled into the United States temporarily for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit, under (c)(14) for those who have been granted deferred action, or under (c)(18) for those against whom a final order of deportation or removal exists and who are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision. The rule will require these aliens to establish they warrant a favorable exercise of discretion. The rule also requires that aliens under these three categories establish economic necessity. In doing so, DHS promotes a consistent policy in contrast to the status quo, which currently only requires aliens who have received a grant of deferred action ((c)(14)) or those with final orders of removal ((c)(18)) to establish economic necessity for employment authorization. This proposed change will also ensure that only aliens with an economic need to work will be eligible for discretionary employment authorization in these categories, as well as minimize the potential risk of disadvantaging American workers. Aliens who do not have an economic need for employment will not be eligible for employment authorization and an employment authorization document (EAD) in these categories. Where DHS previously did not require all aliens under these categories to establish economic necessity, it will now consistently require them to do so. DHS will provide guidance on the documentation that may be used to establish such necessity in form instructions and other sub-regulatory guidance.
除上文详细讨论的所有因素外,本届政府和DHS同样认识到美国工人的重要性。DHS意图通过本规则大幅限制可能促使外国人在获得最终遣返令后仍继续留在美国的就业授权,并加强对美国工人的保护。
In addition to all the factors discussed at length above, this Administration and DHS recognize the importance of American workers as well. DHS intends for this rule to significantly restrict employment authorization that might incentivize aliens to remain in the United States after receiving a final order of removal and to strengthen protection for American workers.
关于某些以就业为基础的签证(例如H-2B临时非农业工人签证)的法定条款规定,此类外国工人不得取代有能力从事该等服务或劳动、合格且可雇用的美国工人,同样,此类外国人就业也不得对美国境内工人的工资和工作条件产生不利影响。[6] 美国国土安全部(DHS)绝无意将本拟议法规所讨论的外国人群体与临时非农业工人相提并论,DHS仅指出某些签证项目中对美国工人的强制性考量要求。然而,DHS认识到,在DHS确定外国人就业授权的可用性和范围时,存在考虑美国工人利益的历史先例。
Statutory provisions governing certain employment-based visas, such as H-2B temporary nonagricultural workers, mandate that such alien workers not displace qualified, available American workers who are capable of performing such services or labor, and similarly that such alien employment not adversely affect the wages and working conditions of workers in the United States.[6] DHS is in no way equating the populations of aliens discussed in this proposed regulation with temporary nonagricultural workers; rather, DHS merely notes the mandatory consideration for American workers in certain visa programs. However, DHS recognizes there is historical precedent to consider American workers when DHS determines the availability and scope of employment authorization for aliens.
例如,1974年,原移民归化局(INS)局长伦纳德·F·查普曼(Leonard F. Chapman, Jr.)宣布对外国学生暑期工作项目政策进行重大变更。[7] 根据新政策,寻求暑期工作的外国学生必须向INS申请并获得许可。在改变这一长期施行的学生就业政策时,INS承认在美留学的年轻外国人所带来的外交政策利益,但认定保护美国人的就业机会应是最终考量因素。[8] 次年,INS总法律顾问萨姆·伯恩森(Sam Bernsen)发表演讲,进一步阐述了INS这一决定的详情。他承认F-1学生工作并未被法律明文禁止,但担忧要确保“不会为了给非移民学生提供就业机会而解雇美国公民或美国合法永久居民的校园工作岗位”。(第34355页)[9] 伯恩森继续指出,“INS必须权衡对外交关系的不利影响与对劳动力市场的不利影响。”[10] 这最终意味着想要工作的学生必须向INS申请,并依照规定规则证明其资格。美国政府问责局(GAO)在1983年的一份报告中估计,1974年约有154,580名F-1学生。[11] 即使全部154,580名F-1学生都取代了美国工人,这一数字仍远低于基于近期就业授权申请所显示的当前取代风险。在2024财政年度,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)共收到33,024份(c)(18)监管令下就业授权文件(EAD)初次及续签申请;792,130份(c)(11)假释EAD初次及续签申请;以及153,154份(c)(14)暂缓执行遣返EAD初次及续签申请,受本拟议规则影响的各类别自由裁量EAD申请合计达978,308份。[12] 如果原INS为防止每年可能多达15万以上美国工人被取代而终止一种工作授权形式是正当的,那么DHS在审查各类自由裁量EAD时,也不能忽视每年最多可能影响978,308名美国工人的潜在影响。
For example, in 1974, the former Immigration and Naturalization Service (INS) Commissioner Leonard F. Chapman, Jr. announced a significant change to the summer program policy for foreign students.[7] Under the new policy, foreign students seeking summer employment had to apply and obtain permission from INS. In changing the long-standing student employment policy, INS recognized the foreign policy benefits for young aliens studying in the United States but determined that the protection of job opportunities for Americans should be the ultimate consideration.[8] The following year, INS General Counsel Sam Bernsen gave a presentation further detailing INS' decision. He recognized that F-1 student work was not expressly banned by statute but was concerned about ensuring that “a United States citizen or a United States lawful permanent resident will not be fired from a campus job to provide employment for a nonimmigrant ( printed page 34355) student.” [9] Continuing, Bernsen stated the “INS had to weigh the adverse effect on foreign relations against the adverse effect on the labor market.” [10] This ultimately meant students who wanted employment had to apply to the INS and establish eligibility under the prescribed rules. The Government Accountability Office (GAO) in a 1983 report estimated that there were approximately 154,580 F-1 students in 1974.[11] If all 154,580 F-1 students displaced American workers, it still falls far short of the current displacement risk based on more recent employment authorization applications. In FY 2024, USCIS received 33,024 (c)(18) Order of Supervision EAD initial and renewal applications; 792,130 (c)(11) Parole EAD initial and renewal applications; and 153,154 (c)(14) Deferred Action EAD initial and renewal applications, for a total of 978,308 discretionary EAD applications in the categories impacted by this proposed rule.[12] If the former INS was justified in terminating a form of work authorization in order to prevent the possible displacement of more than 150,000 American workers on an annual basis, it follows that DHS cannot discount the potential impact on up to 978,308 American workers annually when reviewing discretionary EAD categories.
此外,第14159号行政命令明确规定:
Additionally, E.O. 14159 specifically provides that:
执行我国的移民法律对美国的国家安全和公共安全至关重要。美国人民应当拥有一个将其利益置于首位的联邦政府,一个深知其肩负着优先保障美国人民安全、安保及财政与经济福祉这一神圣职责的联邦政府。[13]
Enforcing our Nation's immigration laws is critically important to the national security and public safety of the United States. The American people deserve a Federal Government that puts their interests first and a Federal Government that understands its sacred obligation to prioritize the safety, security, and financial and economic well-being of Americans.[13]
本规则还将要求以下寻求续签就业授权的外国人群体受雇于参加E-Verify项目并保持良好信誉的美国雇主,或正在寻求受雇于此类雇主:(1)依据(c)(11)条款申请的,因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被临时假释入境美国的外国人;(2)依据(c)(14)条款申请的,已获准暂缓执行遣返的外国人;或(3)依据(c)(18)条款申请的,已被作出最终驱逐出境或遣返令、但依监管令暂时获释的外国人。该要求将保护美国工人免受潜在的岗位取代及劳动力市场中的任何不利影响,并确保雇用这些外国人的美国雇主遵守我国移民法律,不雇用未经授权的工人。
This rule will also require the following groups of aliens who are seeking a renewal of employment authorization be employed by, or seeking employment with, a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in the E-Verify program: aliens who were (1) paroled into the United States temporarily for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit applying under (c)(11); (2) who have been granted deferred action applying under (c)(14); or (3) against whom a final order of deportation or removal exists and who are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision applying under (c)(18). This requirement will protect American workers against potential displacement and any disadvantages in the labor market and ensure that U.S. employers who hire these aliens are complying with our immigration laws and not employing unauthorized workers.
最后,本规则与本届政府保护和加强对美国工人保护的广泛目标相一致。总体而言,通过将就业授权限制于那些证明存在经济必要性并且应获得美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)有利裁量权的外国人,本规则将消除障碍,为美国工人开辟途径,使其能够从事原本可能由外国人填补的职位。此外,本规则限制了持有最终驱逐令的外国人留在美国的激励因素,从而扩大了美国工人的劳动力储备。总体而言,本规则是联邦政府更广泛倡议中的一部分[14],旨在实现总统关于培养美国工人以应对未来工作岗位和振兴经济的国内政策目标。国土安全部提议,本规则所作的变更仅适用于在最终规则生效日期当日或之后提交的初次和续期就业授权申请。
Finally, this rule is consistent with the Administration's broad objective to protect and strengthen protections for American workers. Generally, by limiting employment authorization to those aliens who establish an economic necessity for employment and warrant a favorable exercise of discretion by USCIS, this rule will remove barriers and open pathways for American workers to participate in positions that may otherwise be filled by aliens. Further, the rule limits incentives to remain in the United States for those aliens with final orders of removal, thereby expanding the labor pool for American workers. Overall, this rule represents one piece of a broader initiative [14] within the federal government to fulfill the President's domestic policy goal of developing American workers for jobs of the future and a revitalized economy. DHS proposes to apply changes made by this rule only to initial and renewal employment authorization applications filed on or after the effective date of the final rule.
B. 法律授权
B. Legal Authority
国土安全部部长(部长)在本提议规则中所作监管修订的授权来源于《移民与国籍法》(INA)第8编美国法典第1101条及其后各条以及2002年《国土安全法》(HSA)(公法107-296号,116 Stat. 2135,部分编入美国法典第6编第101条及其后各条)的各项规定。发布本提议规则的一般授权见于INA第103(a)条,即美国法典第8编第1103(a)条,该条授权部长管理和执行移民与归化法律,并制定部长认为执行该授权所必需的法规,以及HSA第122条,即美国法典第6编第112条,该条将国土安全部的全部职能赋予部长,并授权部长发布法规。[15]这包括授权发布法规,批准外国人在美国就业的类别,以及从申请移民福利(如就业授权)的外国人处收集或要求提交生物识别信息的权力。本提议规则的其他授权见于:
The Secretary of Homeland Security's (Secretary) authority for the regulatory amendments made in this proposed rule is found in various provisions of the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA), 8 U.S.C. 1101 et seq., and the Homeland Security Act of 2002 (HSA), Public Law 107-296, 116 Stat. 2135 (codified in part at 6 U.S.C. 101 et seq.). General authority for issuing this proposed rule is found in section 103(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a), which authorizes the Secretary to administer and enforce the immigration and naturalization laws and establish such regulations as the Secretary deems necessary for carrying out such authority, as well as section 122 of the HSA, 6 U.S.C. 112, which vests all of the functions of DHS in the Secretary and authorizes the Secretary to issue regulations.[15] This includes the authority to issue regulations authorizing categories of aliens to be employed in the United States and to collect from or require the submission of biometrics by aliens requesting immigration benefits, such as employment authorization. Additional authority for this proposed rule is found in:
第208条,即美国法典第8编第1158条,该条规范庇护申请的审查,并在特定条件下允许酌情授予庇护申请人就业授权等事项。
Section 208,8 U.S.C. 1158, which governs the consideration of asylum applications and allows, inter alia, discretion to grant asylum applicants employment authorization under specified conditions.
第212(d)(5)(A)条,即美国法典第8编第1182(d)(5)(A)条,该条授权部长规定假释的条件。
Section 212(d)(5)(A),8 U.S.C. 1182(d)(5)(A), which authorizes the Secretary to prescribe conditions on parole.
INA第241条,即美国法典第8编第1231条,该条规范外国人在收到行政终局驱逐令后的拘留、释放、就业授权和驱逐事宜;
Section 241 of the INA,8 U.S.C. 1231, which governs the detention, release, employment authorization, and removal of aliens after they have received an administratively final order of removal;
INA第274A(h)(3)(B)条,即美国法典第8编第1324a(h)(3)(B)条,该条确认部长有权向在美国的外国人延长就业授权;[16]
Section 274A(h)(3)(B) of the INA,8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B), which recognizes the Secretary's authority to extend employment authorization to aliens in the United States; [16]
1996年《非法移民改革和移民责任法》(IIRIRA)第401条至第405条(公法104-208号,110 Stat. 3009- (印刷页第34356页) 546),该条确立了建立和运行E-Verify的授权;
Sections 401-405 of the Illegal Immigration Reform and Immigrant Responsibility Act of 1996 (IIRIRA),Public Law 104-208, 110 Stat. 3009- ( printed page 34356) 546, which established the authority for the creation and operation of E-Verify;
HSA第101(b)(1)(F)条,即美国法典第6编第111(b)(1)(F)条,该条将“确保美国的整体经济安全不因旨在保障国土安全的努力、活动和计划而受到削弱”确立为国土安全部的主要使命之一;
Section 101(b)(1)(F) of the HSA,6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F), which establishes as a primary mission of DHS the duty to “ensure that the overall economic security of the United States is not diminished by efforts, activities, and programs aimed at securing the homeland”;
HSA第451(a)(3)条,即美国法典第6编第271(a)(3)条,该条授权USCIS局长制定“执行[移民裁决]职能的政策”;
Section 451(a)(3) of the HSA,6 U.S.C. 271(a)(3), which confers authority on the USCIS Director to establish “policies for performing [immigration adjudication] functions”;
INA第103条,即美国法典第8编第1103条;INA第287(b)条,即美国法典第8编第1357(b)条;以及美国联邦法规第8章第103.2(b)(9)条和第103.16条,该等条款规定并规范国土安全部对生物识别信息的收集、提交和使用;以及
Section 103 of the INA,8 U.S.C. 1103; section 287(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1357(b); and sections 103.2(b)(9) and 103.16 of chapter 8 of the CFR, which provides for and governs the collection, submission, and use of biometrics by DHS; and
《一个大而美丽法案》(H.R. 1)(公法119-21号,139 Stat. 72,相关部分编入美国法典第8编第1801条至第1815条),该法案对与某些移民福利相关的就业授权有效期以及某些费用施加了限制。
The One Big Beautiful Bill Act (H.R. 1),Public Law 119-21, 139 Stat. 72 (codified in relevant part at 8 U.S.C. 1801-1815), which imposes restrictions on validity periods of employment authorization in relation to certain immigration benefits, as well as certain fees.
C. 监管行动主要条款概述
C. Summary of the Major Provisions of the Regulatory Action
国土安全部提议修订其关于某些外国人酌情就业授权的法规。该拟议规则将包含以下条款,以澄清和限制某些外国人何时有资格获得酌情就业授权,以及USCIS在裁决酌情授予就业授权时将如何权衡某些酌情因素:
DHS is proposing to amend its regulations governing discretionary employment authorization for certain aliens. The proposed rule would include the following provisions to clarify and limit when certain aliens are eligible for discretionary employment authorization and how USCIS will weigh certain discretionary factors when adjudicating a discretionary grant of employment authorization:
关于被给予暂缓遣返或被假释入境美国的外国人的就业授权。美国国土安全部(DHS)建议修订下列外国人酌情就业授权的资格条件:根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11)规定,因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被假释入境美国的外国人;根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)规定,已被给予暂缓遣返的外国人,具体方式是确认此类批准要求外国人证明其有资格获得有利的自由裁量处理,要求申请续签就业授权的外国人受雇于或正在寻求受雇于在E-Verify系统中信誉良好的美国雇主,以及要求因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被假释入境美国的外国人证明其就业存在经济必要性。对8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)提议的具体修改不适用于基于获准暂缓遣返儿童入境者(DACA)的就业授权,后者依据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(33)授权,也不适用于基于给予T类非移民身份申请人及其符合条件家庭成员暂缓遣返的就业授权,该等申请人须有待决的善意申请且有资格获得有利的自由裁量处理,依据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(40)授权,而非8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14);但是,下文所述关于犯罪外国人和生物特征信息的提议修改,通常适用于8 CFR 274a.12(c)条下所有酌情就业授权,除非明确豁免,因此这些修改确实适用于基于DACA批准或善意T类申请的就业授权。
Employment Authorization for Aliens Granted Deferred Action or Paroled into the United States. DHS proposes to revise eligibility for discretionary employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11) for aliens who have been paroled into the United States based on urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit and under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14) for aliens who have been granted deferred action by confirming such a grant requires the alien establish they warrant a favorable exercise of discretion, by requiring aliens applying for renewal of employment authorization be employed by or seeking employment with a U.S. employer in good standing in E-Verify, and by requiring aliens who have been paroled into the United States based on urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit to establish an economic necessity for employment. The specific changes proposed to 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14) do not apply to employment authorization based on a grant of DACA, which is authorized under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(33), or to employment authorization based on a grant of deferred action to an applicant for T nonimmigrant status, and eligible family members, who have pending, bona fide applications, and who warrant a favorable exercise of discretion, authorized under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(40), and not 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14); however, the proposed changes described below relating to criminal aliens and biometrics, which are generally applicable to all discretionary employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c) unless specifically exempted, do apply to employment authorization based on a grant of DACA or a bona fide T application.
关于根据遣返监管令(OSUP)临时释放的外国人的就业授权。DHS建议限制根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)规定的酌情就业授权资格,该条款适用于已有最终遣返令并根据遣返监管令从羁押中被临时释放的外国人,仅限于DHS已认定因所有被要求签发旅行证件的国家均未能签发此类证件而使遣返不可行的外国人。DHS还建议要求申请续签此类别就业授权的外国人受雇于或正在寻求受雇于在E-Verify系统中信誉良好的美国雇主。
Employment Authorization for Aliens Temporarily Released on OSUP. DHS proposes to limit eligibility for discretionary employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18) for aliens who have final orders of removal and are temporarily released from custody on OSUP to aliens for whom DHS has determined that removal is impracticable because all countries from which DHS requested travel documents have failed to issue such documents. DHS also proposes to require aliens applying for renewal of employment authorization in this category to be employed by or seeking employment with a U.S. employer in good standing in E-Verify.
禁止向有犯罪记录的外国人发放酌情就业授权。DHS建议,除非DHS已认定存在重大的相反公共利益,其中可能包括外国人在美国境内协助执法活动,否则DHS通常不会行使有利的自由裁量权批准就业授权,具体情形如下:
Bar discretionary employment authorization for criminal aliens. DHS proposes that unless DHS has determined that there are significant countervailing public interests, which may include the presence of the alien in the United States to assist law enforcement activity in the United States, it generally will not favorably exercise its discretion to grant employment authorization, when:
○ 外国人曾因任何犯罪行为被逮捕、被指控(未经裁决)、被起诉或被定罪;或
○ An alien has been arrested for, charged with (without disposition), indicted for, or convicted of, any criminal act; or
○ 外国人承认实施了暴力或危险犯罪,即使该外国人从未被正式逮捕、指控、起诉或定罪;或
○ An alien admits to committing a violent or dangerous crime, even if the alien has never been formally arrested, charged, indicted or convicted; or
○ 有证据表明该外国人是帮派或恐怖组织的成员。
○ There is evidence of the alien's membership in a gang or terrorist organization.
尽管外国人成功参与州或联邦计划(例如审前转处计划)可能不构成《移民与国籍法》(INA)意义上的定罪,但DHS通常不会对签订施加某种形式的惩罚、处罚或限制自由的协议的外国人行使有利的自由裁量权批准就业授权。这包括外国人的犯罪记录已被封存或消除的协议或计划。在这些情况下,外国人最初的刑事逮捕记录将是决定性因素。
While an alien's successful participation in state or federal programs, such as pretrial diversion programs, may not constitute a conviction for the purposes of the INA, DHS generally will not favorably exercise its discretion to grant employment authorization for aliens who enter into agreements that impose some form of punishment, penalty, or a restraint on liberty. This includes agreements or programs where an alien's criminal record has been sealed or expunged. In these cases, the alien's initial criminal arrest would be the prevailing factor.
要求提交生物特征信息。DHS建议要求申请酌情就业授权的外国人提交生物特征信息。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将把外国人的生物特征信息提交给联邦调查局(FBI)进行犯罪记录核查,并使用外国人的生物特征信息协助身份核实及制作就业授权文件(EAD)。
Require biometrics submission. DHS proposes to require aliens seeking discretionary employment authorization to submit biometrics. USCIS will submit an alien's biometrics to the Federal Bureau of Investigation (FBI) for a criminal history check and use an alien's biometrics to facilitate identity verification and production of the EAD.
有效期。国土安全部(DHS)拟缩短受本拟议规则影响的酌情准予的就业许可文件(EAD)的有效期(例如,不超过一年),并将确保持续符合资格的责任置于申请此类别就业许可的外国人身上。2025年7月4日,总统签署了《一个美好的大法案》(One Big Beautiful Bill Act)(H.R. 1),即《公法》119-21号,139 Stat. 72,使之成为法律。该法案将任何以假释方式进入美国的外国人的就业授权有效期限制为一年或该外国人假释期限,以两者中较短者为准。[17] H.R.1还为基于临时保护身份(TPS)的就业授权设定了法定有效期,规定对于在2025年7月22日当日或之后待决或提交的就业授权初次申请和续签申请,与TPS相关的就业授权只能自动延长最多1年或TPS期限,以两者中较短者为准。[18] 鉴于这些变化,国土安全部正在更新基于暂缓遣返行动和OSUP(监督下有条件释放)的就业授权类别所签发的EAD的有效期,以与H.R. 1规定的其他有效期保持一致。除确保持续符合资格和处理一致性外,(印刷版第34357页)这一较短的有效期也有助于对处于OSUP状态的外国人进行持续管理,以确保外国人遵守OSUP的条款和条件,且未再犯或潜逃。外国人应承担责任,在每次申请就业授权时重新出现以提交生物识别信息,以确保美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)掌握最新和准确的背景调查信息。国土安全部和USCIS可酌情在未来通过发布次级规章指南缩短其他酌情性EAD的有效期。
Validity periods. DHS intends to shorten the validity period of the discretionary EADs ( e.g., not more than one year) impacted by the proposed rule and place the burden on the alien to ensure ongoing eligibility for those applying for EADs under these categories. On July 4, 2025, the President signed into law the One Big Beautiful Bill Act (H.R. 1), Public Law 119-21, 139 Stat. 72. It placed a limit on the validity of employment authorization for any alien paroled into the United States to one year or the duration of the alien's parole, whichever is shorter.[17] H.R.1 also established statutory validity periods for Temporary Protected Status (TPS)-based employment authorization, stating TPS-related employment authorization may only be automatically extended for up to 1 year or the duration of TPS, whichever is shorter, for initial and renewal applications for employment authorization pending or filed on or after July 22, 2025.[18] Considering these changes, DHS is updating the validity period for EADs issued for deferred action and OSUP-based employment authorization categories to align with the others imposed in H.R. 1. In addition to ensuring continuous eligibility and consistent treatment, this ( printed page 34357) shorter validity period also supports ongoing management of aliens on OSUP to ensure aliens are complying with the terms and conditions of OSUP and have not reoffended or absconded. The burden should be on the alien to reappear for biometrics submission with each application for employment authorization to ensure USCIS has the most up-to-date and accurate background check information. At their discretion, DHS and USCIS may shorten additional discretionary EAD validity periods by issuing sub-regulatory guidance in the future.
就业授权的自动终止。国土安全部拟扩大8 CFR 274a.14(a)(1)项下自动终止的理由范围,增加以下两项理由:
Automatic Termination of Employment Authorization. DHS is proposing to expand the reasons for automatic termination under 8 CFR 274a.14(a)(1), to include two additional reasons:
○ 当外国人根据任何遣返法规(例如《移民与国籍法》(INA)第217条、第235条、第238条、第240条)被下达具有行政终局效力的遣返令时;以及
○ When an alien has an administratively final order of removal under any of the removal statutes ( e.g., INA 217, 235, 238, 240); and
○ 当作为就业授权依据的基础被终止或被拒绝时。这可能包括国土安全部终止了作为就业授权依据的身份,或拒绝了作为就业授权依据的申请(例如假释、暂缓遣返行动)。
○ When the underlying basis for employment authorization is terminated or denied. This can include DHS's termination of status or denial of the application that was the basis of the employment authorization ( e.g., parole, deferred action).
有关基础身份或福利终止、待决申请被拒绝,或存在最终遣返令的通知,将导致根据第274a.12(c)条授予的任何外国人就业授权自动终止。
Notice of the termination of the underlying status or benefit, denial of a pending application, or having a final order of removal will result in the automatic termination of any alien's employment authorization granted under § 274a.12(c).
D. 成本与收益概述
D. Summary of Costs and Benefits
国土安全部估计,本拟议规则将导致获准暂缓遣返行动的外国人、获准假释的外国人以及具有最终遣返令的外国人中符合就业授权资格者的数量减少。这可能导致不再符合就业授权资格的外国人收入损失,同时也确保并加强对美国工人的保护。收入损失可能导致成本从该外国人转移至其支持网络,包括家庭成员、社区团体、非营利组织或为该外国人提供支持的第三方组织,以及任何受抚养人。此外,国土安全部估计,本拟议规则将增加仍符合就业授权资格的外国人的申请负担,同时确保就业的经济必要性,并使国土安全部能够在签发就业授权之前核实犯罪记录并对外国人身份进行生物识别验证,且外国人须令USCIS满意地证明其应获得有利的自由裁量处理。目前雇用在本拟议规则下将不再符合续签就业授权资格的外国工人的美国企业,可能因员工流动或为遵守拟议的E-Verify要求(以确保外国人获得工作授权)而产生新的成本。最后,本拟议规则可能导致税收收入的损失。
DHS estimates that this proposed rule would result in a reduction in the number of aliens with granted deferred action, aliens granted parole, and aliens with final orders of removal who are eligible for employment authorization. This could result in lost earnings for aliens who are no longer eligible for employment authorization, while also ensuring and strengthening protections of American workers. The lost earnings could result in a transfer of costs from the alien to their support network, including family members, community groups, non-profits or third-party organizations that provide for the alien, and any dependents. In addition, DHS estimates that the proposed rule would increase filing burdens for those aliens who remain eligible for employment authorization, while ensuring economic necessity for employment and permitting DHS to verify criminal history and biometrically verify an alien's identity before issuing employment authorization, and demonstrating to the satisfaction of USCIS that the alien warrants a favorable exercise of discretion. U.S. businesses that currently employ alien workers who would no longer be eligible to renew their employment authorization under this proposed rule could incur new costs due to employee turnover or compliance with the proposed E-Verify requirement that would ensure aliens' authorization to work. Finally, the proposed rule may result in a loss of tax revenue.
根据拟议规则,国土安全部(DHS)估算并量化了六类经济影响,包括:(1)可能不再有资格获得就业授权的外国人工人的潜在收入损失;(2)外国人提交表格所需增加的时间负担;(3)外国人提交生物识别信息所需增加的时间和费用;[19] (4)当就业许可文件(EAD)到期、被撤销或未获续签时,外国人工人的雇主可能产生的劳动力更替成本;(5)雇主为加入并维持良好信誉的E-Verify账户、以保留申请续签EAD的外国人工人所需支付的费用;以及(6)联邦政府潜在的就业税损失。
Under the proposed rule, DHS estimates and quantifies six types of economic impacts, including: (1) potential lost earnings of alien workers who may no longer be eligible for employment authorization; (2) increased time burden for aliens to submit forms; (3) added time and costs for aliens to submit biometrics; [19] (4) labor turnover costs that employers of alien workers could incur when EADs expire, are revoked, or are not renewed; (5) costs to employers to enroll in and maintain an E-Verify account as a participant in good standing to retain alien workers applying for renewal EADs; and (6) potential employment tax losses to the Federal government.
国土安全部估计,由于该拟议规则,部分外国人将不再具备申请自由裁量性EAD的资格。但由于数据限制,国土安全部无法精确估算该群体的规模,因此采用了一个设有上下限的区间。该拟议规则在10年内未折现的直接成本估计约为91亿美元至279亿美元(见表V.36),其中包括与生物识别及相关申请表格所需增加时间负担相关的成本,以及如雇主无法为因本规则而丧失就业授权资格的(c)(11)类、(c)(14)类和(c)(18)类外国人找到替代劳动力时所产生的估计成本。该拟议规则在10年内按3%贴现率折算的年化成本估计为9.205亿美元至28亿美元,按7%贴现率折算的年化成本估计为9.371亿美元至29亿美元。国土安全部估计,雇主和雇员向联邦政府转移的就业税(即医疗保险税和社会保障税)最高金额(10年未折现)为29亿美元(按3%贴现率年化为2.982亿美元,按7%贴现率年化为3.046亿美元)。
DHS estimates that some aliens would be ineligible for discretionary EADs due to the proposed rule. However, DHS cannot estimate this population with precision because of data constraints and, therefore, relies on a range with an upper and lower bound. The estimated 10-year undiscounted, direct costs of this proposed rule would range from about $9.1 billion to $27.9 billion (Table V.36), which includes costs associated with biometrics and added time burdens for relevant filing forms as well as estimated costs should employers not be able to find replacement labor for category (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) aliens who would become ineligible for employment authorization under this rule. The estimated 10-year costs of the proposed rule annualized at a 3 percent discount rate would range from $920.5 million to $2.8 billion, and at a 7 percent discount rate would range from $937.1 million to $2.9 billion. DHS estimates $2.9 billion (10-year undiscounted) as the maximum transfer of employment taxes (namely Medicare and Social Security) from employers and employees to the Federal Government ($298.2 million annualized at 3 percent and $304.6 million annualized at 7 percent).
该拟议规则的潜在益处是定性的。首先,由于该拟议规则将降低(c)(11)类、(c)(14)类和(c)(18)类外国人工人的就业授权资格,美国公民或合法永久居民工人总体上将更有可能获得目前由这些外国人群体所从事的工作。其次,该拟议规则可能降低(c)(18)类外国人在收到最终驱逐令后继续留在美国的动机,这可能减少政府在执行此类外国人最终驱逐令以及监控和追踪根据暂缓执行驱逐令(OSUP)暂时获释的外国人方面所耗费的资源。根据国土安全部2025年5月发布的一项公告,[20]逮捕、拘留和驱逐一名非法外国人的平均成本为17,121美元。[21]
The potential benefits of the proposed rule would be qualitative. First, U.S. citizen or lawful permanent resident workers on the whole would be more likely to obtain jobs currently held by category (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) alien workers since the proposed rule would reduce employment authorization eligibility for these populations of aliens. Second, the proposed rule may reduce the incentive for (c)(18) aliens to remain in the United States after receiving a final order of removal, which could reduce the amount of government resources expended on enforcing final orders of removal for such aliens as well as monitoring and tracking aliens temporarily released on OSUP. According to a May 2025 DHS announcement,[20] the average cost to arrest, detain, and remove an illegal alien is $17,121.[21]
其他无法量化的益处还包括:使国土安全部能够确定就业的经济必要性,在根据第274a.12(c)条签发任何就业授权之前通过生物识别验证外国人的身份,将外国人的生物识别信息与政府数据库进行比对以核查其是否存在犯罪行为,以及确保续签就业授权的外国人的就业授权经由其雇主核实,从而提高移民制度的完整性。
Additional unquantifiable benefits also include enabling DHS to determine an economic necessity for employment, biometrically verifying an alien's identity before issuing any employment authorization under § 274a.12(c), vetting an alien's biometrics against government databases for criminal activity, and ensuring that aliens who renew their employment authorization have their employment authorization verified by their employer, thereby increasing the integrity of the immigration system.
表II.1汇总展示了拟议法规变更的影响以及相关的估计成本和效益。[22]
Table II.1 shows the summary of impacts of the proposed regulatory changes and the associated estimated costs and benefits.[22]

减少有最终驱逐令的外国人、被授予暂缓遣返的外国人以及被授予假释的外国人中有资格获得就业授权者的人数所产生的影响[23],既包括潜在的分配性影响(转移支付),也包括成本。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将不再有资格获得就业授权的外国人所损失的报酬作为衡量这一变化影响的指标,即视为这些外国人向他人转移的分配性影响(转移支付),或作为企业生产力损失成本的替代衡量指标。如果所有公司都能轻易为这些外国人原本将填补的职位找到合理的劳动力替代者,国土安全部(DHS)估计最多将有28亿美元(按3%贴现率年度化)从这些工人转移至劳动力市场中的其他人(或促使其他人重新进入劳动力市场),或29亿美元(按7%贴现率年度化)(表II.2(A))。[24] 在此情形下,不会有联邦就业税损失。[25] 相反,如果公司无法为这些外国人原本将填补的职位找到合理的劳动力替代者,则估计最多28亿美元(按3%贴现率年度化)或29亿美元(按7%贴现率年度化)为生产力损失的货币化成本估计值,而从这些外国人向其他工人转移的货币化转移支付估计值为0美元。此外,在这种职位空缺无人填补的情形下,联邦政府将损失就业税收。USCIS估计,从公司和雇员向联邦政府转移的就业税中,最大减少额为2.982亿美元(按3%贴现率年度化)或3.046亿美元(按7%贴现率年度化)。
The impacts [23] of reducing the number of aliens with final orders of removal, aliens granted deferred action, and aliens granted parole who are eligible for employment authorization include both potential distributional impacts (transfers) and costs. USCIS uses the lost compensation to aliens who are no longer eligible for employment authorization as a measure of the impact of this change—either as distributional impacts (transfers) from these aliens to others or as a proxy for businesses' cost for lost productivity. If all companies can easily find reasonable labor substitutes for the positions the aliens would have otherwise filled, DHS estimates a maximum of $2.8 billion (annualized at a 3 percent discount rate) would be transferred from these workers to others in the labor force (or induced back into the labor force) or $2.9 billion (annualized at a 7 percent discount rate) (Table II.2(A)).[24] Under this scenario, there would be no Federal employment tax losses.[25] Conversely, if companies are unable to find reasonable labor substitutes for the positions the aliens would have filled, then a maximum of $2.8 billion (annualized at a 3 percent discount rate) or $2.9 billion (annualized at a 7 percent discount rate) is the estimated monetized cost in lost productivity, and $0 is the estimated monetized transfers from these aliens to other workers. In addition, under this scenario where jobs would go unfilled, there would be a loss of employment taxes to the Federal Government. USCIS estimates $298.2 million (annualized at a 3 percent discount rate) or $304.6 million (annualized at a 7 percent discount rate) as the maximum reduction in transfers of employment taxes from companies and employees to the Federal Government.
国土安全部认为,上述两种情形代表了在劳动力市场条件下雇主应对能力的不确定性。国土安全部对因影响有最终驱逐令的外国人、被授予暂缓遣返的外国人以及被授予假释的外国人就业资格的规定所产生的货币化影响范围估算了端点值。本次规则制定的效果部分取决于诸多复杂变量之间的相互作用,这些变量处于不断变化之中,(印刷页第34365页)包括全国、州及地方劳动力市场状况、经济和商业因素、所涉及的职业类型和技能,以及具有类似技能的工人的可获得性。国土安全部承认,关于移民对劳动力市场影响的文献十分丰富。[26] 国土安全部欢迎公众就上述情形中提出的估算以及关于受影响职位将被重新填补这一假设的有效性提出意见。
DHS believes the two scenarios described above represent the uncertainty in how employers will be able to respond given labor market conditions. DHS estimated endpoints for the range of monetized impacts resulting from the provisions that affect employment eligibility for aliens with final orders of removal, aliens granted deferred action, and aliens granted parole. Effects of this rulemaking would depend in part on the interaction of a ( printed page 34365) number of complex variables that are constantly in flux, including national, state, and local labor market conditions, economic and business factors, the type of occupations and skills involved, and the availability of similarly skilled workers. DHS acknowledges there is extensive literature on the impacts of immigration on labor markets.[26] DHS welcomes public comment on the estimates presented in these scenarios and on the validity of the assumptions on affected jobs being backfilled.
该规则还存在其他成本,包括E-Verify系统、生物识别信息采集、劳动力流动以及额外的表格填报负担所产生的成本。这些其他成本在上述两种情形下均存在,因此该规则的最低成本为420万美元(按3%贴现率年度化),或440万美元(按7%贴现率年度化)。
There are other costs of the rule, including E-Verify, biometrics, labor turnover, and additional form burdens. These other costs exist under both scenarios described above, and thus $4.2 million is the minimum cost of the rule (annualized at a 3 percent discount rate) or $4.4 million (annualized at a 7 percent discount rate).
上述情形所描述的影响范围,加上对其他成本的考量,汇总于表II.2中。表II.2中显示的主要估算值为每种情形下最低估算值与最高估算值之间的中位点。[27]

三、背景与目的
III. Background and Purpose
A. 先前及相关规则制定工作
A. Prior and Related Rulemaking Efforts
2020年11月19日,国土安全部(DHS)在《联邦公报》上发布了一份拟议规则制定通知(NPRM),题为《对具有最终驱逐令的特定类别外国人的就业授权》(以下简称“OSUP拟议规则”)。85 FR 74196。该规则拟取消因最终驱逐令而被临时释放并处于监管令下的外国人获得自由裁量性就业授权的资格,仅设一项有限例外。总体而言,OSUP拟议规则的依据源自特朗普总统发布的两项行政命令,即第13768号行政命令(《加强美国境内公共安全》)和第13788号行政命令(《购买美国货、雇用美国人》)。[29]
On November 19, 2020, DHS published a notice of proposed rulemaking (NPRM) in the Federal Register , Employment Authorization for Certain Classes of Aliens with Final Orders of Removal (OSUP NPRM). 85 FR 74196. It proposed to eliminate eligibility for discretionary employment authorization for aliens who have final orders of removal and are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision with one narrow exception. In general, the basis for the OSUP NPRM stemmed from two executive orders issued by President Trump, E.O. 13768 (Enhancing Public Safety in the Interior of the United States) and E.O. 13788 (Buy American and Hire American).[29]
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)为该拟议规则提供了为期30天的公众评议期,该评议期于2020年12月21日结束。国土安全部就OSUP拟议规则共收到306条评论意见。
USCIS provided a 30-day comment period to receive public comments on the proposed rule, which ended December 21, 2020. DHS received a total of 306 comments on the OSUP NPRM.
2021年1月20日,拜登总统发布了第13993号行政命令(《修订民事移民执法政策与优先事项》),废止了第13768号行政命令。[30]随后,2021年1月25日,拜登总统发布了第14005号行政命令(《确保由全体美国人民共同实现“美国制造”的未来》),废止了第13788号行政命令。[31]第13993号和第14005号行政命令指示各机构审查、修订或撤销与这两项行政命令不符的任何机构行动或指南。
On January 20, 2021, President Biden issued E.O. 13993 (Revision of Civil Immigration Enforcement Policies and Priorities), which revoked E.O. 13768.[30] Then, on January 25, 2021, President Biden issued E.O. 14005 (Ensuring the Future Is Made in All of America by All of America's Workers), which revoked E.O. 13788.[31] E.O.s 13993 and 14005 directed agencies to review, revise, or rescind any agency actions or guidance inconsistent with the executive orders.
在根据第13993号和第14005号行政命令审查了OSUP拟议规则及公众评论意见后,国土安全部于2021年5月10日撤回了OSUP拟议规则。《对具有最终驱逐令的特定类别外国人的就业授权;撤回》,86 FR 24751。因此,OSUP最终规则从未发布。本规则中的许多拟议条款与OSUP拟议规则最初提出的内容密切相关(印刷页34370)。但由于最终规则从未发布,国土安全部将重新处理此前提出的各项条款。
After reviewing the OSUP NPRM and the public comments in light of E.O.s 13993 and 14005, DHS withdrew the OSUP NPRM on May 10, 2021. Employment Authorization for Certain Classes of Aliens with Final Orders of Removal; Withdrawal, 86 FR 24751. Therefore, an OSUP final rule was never published. Many of the proposed provisions in this rule closely follow ( printed page 34370) what was originally proposed in the OSUP NPRM. However, as no final rule was published, DHS will address the previously proposed provisions anew.
与本规则同时,国土安全部正在推进多项处于不同制定阶段的规则制定行动。国土安全部已就这些其他规则对本规则可能产生的边际性、重叠性或相互关联的影响逐一进行了考虑和分析,并已将其影响(如有)纳入本拟议规则的支持性文件、政策及法规文本之中。
Concurrent with this rule, DHS is engaging in multiple rulemaking actions that are in various stages of development. DHS has considered and analyzed each of these other rules for peripheral, overlapping, or interrelated effects on this rule and has incorporated their effects, if any, into the supporting documentation, policies, and regulatory text for this proposed rule.
1. 庇护就业授权文件改革
1. Asylum EAD Reform
国土安全部近期发布了一项拟议规则(“庇护就业授权文件改革规则”),涉及根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8)和8 CFR 208.7规定(通俗称为“(c)(8)就业授权文件”)为正在申请庇护的外国人提供就业授权事宜。
DHS recently published a proposed rule (“Asylum EAD Reform Rule”) addressing employment authorization for aliens with pending applications for asylum under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8) and 8 CFR 208.7 (colloquially referred to as a “(c)(8) EAD”).
在拟议的《庇护就业授权文件改革规则》(91 FR 8616)中,国土安全部拟修订8 CFR 274a.13(a)(1),以使美国公民及移民服务局根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第208(d)(2)条,即8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)所赋予的自由裁量法定权力,对依据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8)申请庇护的外国人提交的就业授权申请拥有自由裁量权。由于庇护属于自由裁量性福利,因此美国公民及移民服务局同样应以自由裁量方式批准与未决庇护申请相关的工作授权。对于一个其救济申请多数最终未获成功的外国人群体,国土安全部不能继续在几乎没有资格限制且几乎无限可续期的情况下,持续向庇护申请人提供就业授权。在2023财年、2024财年以及2025财年(截至目前)中,在美国公民及移民服务局审结的约33,500件庇护案件中,约有18,000件,超过50%的结果为拒绝或移交移民法院处理。[32]美国司法部移民上诉执行办公室(EOIR)下属移民法院的法官,在2023财年和2024财年对实体审理的庇护申请的批准率同样约为50%,而在2025财年(截至第三季度)仅约为25%。[33]
In the proposed Asylum EAD Reform rule, 91 FR 8616, DHS seeks to amend 8 CFR 274a.13(a)(1) so that USCIS would have discretion to grant applications for employment authorization filed by aliens applying for asylum pursuant to 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8) in keeping with its discretionary statutory authority under section 208(d)(2) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2). As asylum is a discretionary benefit, it follows that USCIS should similarly grant work authorization associated with a pending asylum application as a matter of discretion. DHS cannot continue to provide employment authorization to asylum applicants with virtually no eligibility criteria and nearly limitless renewal opportunities to a population of aliens where many of the claims for relief are ultimately unsuccessful. In fiscal years 2023, 2024, and 2025 (year to date), in roughly 18,000 of the approximately 33,500 asylum cases completed by USCIS, over 50% resulted in a denial or referral to Immigration Court.[32] Judges in the Department of Justice, Executive Office for Immigration Review's (EOIR) immigration courts similarly granted about 50% of the asylum applications adjudicated on the merits in fiscal years 2023 and 2024 and only about 25% in fiscal year 2025 (through the third quarter).[33]
拟议的《庇护就业授权文件改革规则》旨在改革、完善并简化庇护就业授权文件的办理流程,以便能够优先处理并延长保护期限予那些提出真实合法庇护申请的人员。因此,该拟议规则将影响依据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8)为具有未决庇护申请的外国人签发就业授权文件的流程、(c)(8)就业授权文件申请的处理时限、申请(c)(8)就业授权文件的等待期、(c)(8)就业授权文件的裁决时间、(c)(8)就业授权文件的有效期,以及(c)(8)就业授权文件的资格要求。《庇护就业授权文件改革规则》将要求对现行法规文本进行修改,并制定新的法规文本。
The purpose of the proposed Asylum EAD Reform Rule is to reform, improve, and streamline the asylum EAD process, so that those with bona fide asylum claims can be prioritized and extended protection. Thus, that proposed rule would impact the process for issuance of EADs for aliens with a pending asylum application under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8), the processing timeframe for (c)(8) EAD applications, the waiting period to apply for a (c)(8) EAD, the time in which a (c)(8) EAD is adjudicated, (c)(8) EAD validity period, and eligibility requirements for (c)(8) EADs. The Asylum EAD Reform Rule would require changes to existing regulatory text and the creation of new regulatory text.
DHS考虑了《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》与本拟议规则可能产生的综合影响。本规则与《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》均强调向相关群体授予就业许可的自由裁量性质,本规则与《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》中提出的自由裁量因素及明确的资格要求通常是相互重叠的。例如,《自由裁量工作许可文件规则》与《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》均提议将某些有犯罪记录的外国人排除在就业许可资格之外。在《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》中,DHS提议将以下外国人排除在(c)(8)工作许可文件资格之外:根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第101(a)(43)条,即8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(43)所述被认定犯有加重重罪的外国人;被认定犯有特别严重罪行的外国人;有严重理由相信其在美国境外犯有严重非政治性罪行的外国人;以及未能证明本人不因8 CFR 208.13(c)规定的任何法规性犯罪理由而须被强制拒绝庇护的外国人。
DHS considered the possible combined effects of the Asylum EAD Reform Rule and this proposed rule. Both this rule and the Asylum EAD Reform Rule emphasize the discretionary nature of employment authorization for the pertinent populations and the proposed discretionary factors and clarified eligibility requirements included in this rule and the Asylum EAD Reform Rule generally overlap. For example, both the Discretionary EAD Rule and Asylum EAD Reform Rule propose to exclude certain criminal aliens from employment authorization eligibility. In the Asylum EAD Reform Rule, DHS is proposing to exclude (c)(8) EAD eligibility for any alien who has been convicted of an aggravated felony as described in section 101(a)(43) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(43), any alien who has been convicted of a particularly serious crime, any alien for whom there are serious reasons to believe that he or she committed a serious non-political crime outside the United States, and any alien who fails to establish that he or she is not subject to a mandatory denial of asylum due to any regulatory criminal grounds under 8 CFR 208.13(c).
尽管本拟议规则与《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》存在交叉,DHS仍以现行法规文本作为两项规则修改的基础。这是因为《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》在此阶段提出的任何修改仍仅处于“拟议”状态。因此,DHS承认,为使文本与截至发布时的任何更新法规保持一致,任一最终规则的法规文本可能与相关拟议规则中的具体条款有所不同。例如,《自由裁量工作许可文件规则》对8 CFR 274a.12(c)进行了修订,以列明目前属于非自由裁量性质的工作许可文件类别,其中包括(c)(8)项下的庇护工作许可文件。然而,为准确协调《自由裁量工作许可文件规则》与《庇护工作许可文件规则》,该条款在最终规则阶段可能需要进行修改。DHS指出,尽管拟议的《自由裁量工作许可文件规则》包含涉及8 CFR 274a.12(c)项下所有就业许可的修改内容,但《庇护工作许可文件改革规则》将处理所有(c)(8)项下的具体拟议修改。
Although this proposed rule intersects with the Asylum EAD Reform Rule, DHS is using current regulatory text as the basis for changes in both rules. This is because any changes proposed by the Asylum EAD Reform Rule at this point in the process are just that—proposed. Therefore, DHS acknowledges that the regulatory text for either final rule may differ from the exact provisions in the relevant proposed rule in order to align the text with any updated regulations as of the time of publication. For example, the Discretionary EAD Rule amends 8 CFR 274a.12(c) to outline the EAD categories that are currently non-discretionary, which includes asylum EADs under (c)(8). However, this provision may require edits at the final rule stage, to accurately align both the Discretionary EAD and Asylum EAD rules. DHS notes that while the proposed Discretionary EAD Rule includes changes that relate to all employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c), the Asylum EAD Reform Rule will address all (c)(8)-specific proposed changes.
2. 生物识别信息规则
2. Biometrics Rule
DHS同时还在拟议修订其关于在移民和归化法律的管理与执法以及任何移民申请、申请书或福利或任何其他相关请求或信息收集的裁决过程中使用和提交生物识别信息的规定(“生物识别信息规则”)。[34]《生物识别信息规则》拟要求任何提交移民福利请求或其他请求或信息收集、或与之相关联[35]的个人,无论年龄大小,均须提交生物识别信息,除非获得豁免。此要求涵盖任何申请人、申请书提交人、担保人、支持人、随行亲属、受抚养人或受益人,包括美国公民、美国国民和合法永久居民。该规则将界定“生物识别信息”一词,并扩大DHS获授权采集的生物识别方式种类。此外,拟议规则将把USCIS重复使用生物识别信息的能力纳入法规,即在完成基于生物识别信息的身份核实后,方可重复使用生物识别信息,而不要求申请人在所有情况下均须重新提交生物识别信息。最后,该规则将在外国人被逮捕时扩大生物识别信息采集权限;确立“特殊情况”标准,以免除未能按时出席生物识别服务预约者的责任;修改《对妇女暴力法》(VAWA)自行申请人及T类非移民身份申请人证明良好品行(印刷版第34371页)的方式;并明确DHS可为何种目的采集生物识别信息。
DHS is also concurrently proposing to amend its regulations concerning the use and submission of biometrics in the administration and enforcement of immigration and naturalization laws and the adjudication of any immigration application, petition, or benefit or any other related request or collection of information (“Biometrics Rule”).[34] The Biometrics Rule proposes to require the submission of biometrics by any individual, regardless of age, filing or associated [35] with an immigration benefit request or other request or collection of information, unless exempted. This incorporates any applicant, petitioner, sponsor, supporter, derivative, dependent, or beneficiary, including U.S. citizens, U.S. nationals, and lawful permanent residents. It will define “biometrics” and expand modalities authorized for collection by DHS. Further, the proposed rule will codify USCIS's ability to reuse biometrics rather than requiring applicants to submit new biometrics in all cases but permit the reuse of biometrics only after completion of a biometric-based identity verification. Finally, it will expand biometrics collection authority upon alien arrest; establish an “extraordinary circumstances” standard to excuse a failure to appear at a biometric services appointment; modify how VAWA self-petitioners and T nonimmigrant status applicants demonstrate good moral ( printed page 34371) character; and clarify the purposes for which DHS can collect.
《生物识别信息规则》的目的在于统一本部门采集生物识别信息的方式,并向将受生物识别信息要求约束的人群提供通知。正如《生物识别信息规则》中更详细说明的那样,利用生物识别信息进行身份核实[36]和案件管理,将有助于DHS打击人口贩运、核实背景犯罪记录审查结果,以及遏制欺诈行为。
The purpose of the Biometrics Rule is to standardize the Department's collection of biometrics and provide notice to those populations that will be subject to biometrics requirements. As explained more in-depth in the Biometrics Rule, using biometrics for identity verification [36] and case management will assist DHS's efforts to combat trafficking, confirm the results of biographical criminal history checks, and deter fraud.
DHS欢迎各界就本拟议规则与任何可能与本规则制定程序相重叠或同时进行的介入性立法、相关规则制定及政策变化所产生的综合影响发表意见。
DHS welcomes comments on the combined impact of this proposed rule with any intervening legislation, related rulemakings, and policy changes that could either overlap or coincide with this rulemaking.
B. 背景
B. Background
1. 被下令驱逐出境外国人的拘留、释放与遣返
1. Detention, Release, and Repatriation of Aliens Ordered Removed
《移民与国籍法》(INA)第241条,即《美国法典》第8编第1231条,规定了对最终驱逐令所涉外国人的拘留、释放和驱逐事宜。通常情况下,国土安全部(DHS)须在驱逐令成为行政终局裁决之日起90天内将该外国人驱逐出境。[37]如果该外国人未能或拒绝善意及时申请旅行证件或其离境所需的其他文件,或串谋或采取行动阻止驱逐,则该90天驱逐期可予以延长。[38]根据INA第241(a)(2)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1231(a)(2)条,国土安全部“应当拘留”该驱逐期内的外国人,并明确禁止释放依据INA第212(a)(2)或(a)(3)(B)条(即《美国法典》第8编第1182(a)(2)或(a)(3)(B)条)被裁定不可入境,或依据INA第237(a)(2)或(a)(4)(B)条(即《美国法典》第8编第1227(a)(2)或(a)(4)(B)条)被裁定可驱逐的外国人(涉及刑事、安全相关及恐怖主义事由)。
Section 241 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231, governs the detention, release, and removal of aliens subject to final orders of removal. DHS generally has 90 days after the date a removal order becomes administratively final to remove the alien from the United States.[37] This 90-day removal period can be extended if the alien fails or refuses to make timely application in good faith for travel or other documents necessary for the alien's departure or conspires or acts to prevent removal.[38] Under section 241(a)(2) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(a)(2), DHS “shall detain” an alien during the removal period and is specifically prohibited from releasing an alien during the removal period who has been found inadmissible under sections 212(a)(2) or (a)(3)(B) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(2) or (a)(3)(B), or deportable under sections 237(a)(2) or (a)(4)(B) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(2) or (a)(4)(B) (criminal, security-related, and terrorism grounds).
在某些情况下,国土安全部无法在90天驱逐期内将外国人驱逐出境。在此类情况下,国土安全部必须遵守美国最高法院在Zadvydas诉Davis案中的裁决。[39]在Zadvydas案中,最高法院裁定,一旦确定“在可预见的将来并无大幅可能性被驱逐”,持有最终驱逐令的外国人不得继续被拘留(除非存在特殊情形)。[40][41]法院确立了6个月作为“推定合理的拘留期限”。[42]在该6个月期限届满后,一旦外国人提供充分理由证明在可预见的将来并无大幅可能性被驱逐,政府必须以充分证据予以反驳。[43]若国土安全部认定在可预见的将来不太可能实现驱逐,则通常必须依据监管令暂时释放该外国人。[44]在此释放期间,该外国人须继续作出努力(或协助相关努力)促成其被驱逐,同时国土安全部将继续推进其驱逐事宜。[45]
In certain instances, DHS is not able to remove aliens within the 90-day removal period. In such cases, DHS must comply with the U.S. Supreme Court's decision in Zadvydas v. Davis.[39] In Zadvydas, the Supreme Court held that an alien with a final order of removal cannot be kept in detention (unless special circumstances exist) [40] once it has been determined that there is not a “significant likelihood of removal in the reasonably foreseeable future.” [41] The Court established 6 months as the “presumptively reasonable period of detention.” [42] After the 6-month period, once the alien provides good reason to believe there is no significant likelihood of removal in the reasonably foreseeable future, the Government must respond with sufficient evidence to rebut that showing.[43] In the event DHS determines that removal is not likely to occur in the reasonably foreseeable future, the alien must generally be temporarily released on an order of supervision.[44] During this period of release, the alien is required to continue to make efforts (or assist in efforts) towards his or her removal while DHS continues to pursue the alien's removal.[45]
若某外国人根据监管令被暂时释放,该监管令将载明释放条件,包括要求该外国人定期到移民官员处报到,并遵守监管令所规定的条件。[46]若外国人未能遵守监管令所规定的释放条件,国土安全部可将其重新收押拘留,直至其被驱逐出境。故意不遵守监管令的外国人,还可能依据INA第243(b)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1253(b)条,被追究刑事责任。
If an alien is temporarily released on an order of supervision, the order of supervision will contain conditions for release, including requiring the alien to appear periodically before an immigration officer and comply with the conditions prescribed in the order of supervision.[46] If an alien fails to comply with the conditions of release as specified in the order of supervision, DHS can take the alien back into custody and detain the alien until he or she is removed. Aliens who willfully fail to comply with an order of supervision can also be criminally prosecuted under section 243(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1253(b).
一旦某外国人被签发最终驱逐令,美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)即依据INA第241条(《美国法典》第8编第1231条)及《联邦法规汇编》第8编第241部分的规定,负责执行将该外国人驱逐出美国的事宜。通常情况下,须从外国政府处取得旅行证件,方可使该外国人离开美国,并被遣返至其出生国、公民所属国、国籍所属国或最后惯常居住国,或遣返至已同意接收该外国人的其他国家。根据2019年的驱逐数据,国土安全部平均需187.19天,约6个月的时间,方能取得旅行证件并将外国人驱逐出美国。[47]由于近年来这一平均时长有所缩短,预计被依据监管释放令(OSUP)释放的外国人数量将随之减少。[48]
Once an alien has been issued a final order of removal, ICE is responsible for effectuating the alien's removal from the United States pursuant to section 241 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231, and 8 CFR part 241. Generally, a travel document must be obtained from a foreign government that will allow the alien to depart the United States and be repatriated either to the alien's country of birth, citizenship, nationality, or last habitual residence or to an alternate country that has agreed to accept the alien. Based on 2019 removal data, it takes DHS an average of 187.19 days, roughly 6 months, to obtain travel documents and remove an alien from the United States.[47] As this average has declined in recent years, the population of aliens that will be released on OSUP will likely decrease.[48]
然而,一些国家拒绝或无理拖延向已被签发最终驱逐令的外国人签发必要的旅行证件。无理拖延接受遣返其公民或国民的国家,妨碍了美国国土安全部(DHS)及时将外国人驱逐出境的能力,并干涉了美国在执行其移民法方面的主权利益。根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第243(d)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1253(d)条,国土安全部部长有权通知国务卿某一特定国家拒绝或无理拖延接受其国民。在收到国土安全部部长的此类通知后,国务卿必须命令该国的领事官员停止向该国公民和国民签发移民签证、非移民签证或两者。[49]尽管国土安全部和美国国务院(DOS)通过各种外交渠道和途径努力促使此类国家遵守规定,且大多数国家确实遵守,但仍有一些国家拒绝协助遣返其公民和国民,因此,美国已根据《移民与国籍法》第243(d)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1253(d)条,对这些国家实施签证制裁,以促使其配合。[50]
However, some countries refuse or unreasonably delay the issuance of the necessary travel documents to aliens who have been issued a final order of removal. Countries that unreasonably delay accepting the repatriation of their citizens or nationals impede DHS's ability to remove aliens in a timely manner and interfere with the United States' sovereign interest in enforcing its immigration laws. Under section 243(d) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1253(d), the Secretary has the authority to notify the Secretary of State that a specific country is refusing or unreasonably delaying acceptance of its nationals. Upon such notification from the Secretary, the Secretary of State must order consular officers in that country to discontinue issuing immigrant visas, nonimmigrant visas, or both to citizens and nationals of that country.[49] While DHS and the U.S. Department of State (DOS) work through various diplomatic channels and avenues to get such countries to comply, and most countries do comply, there are countries that refuse to assist in the repatriation of their citizens and nationals, and as a result, the United States has imposed visa sanctions under section 243(d) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1253(d), to get such countries to cooperate.[50]
(印刷页第34372页)
( printed page 34372)
2. 根据《移民与国籍法》暂缓驱逐出境及执行《禁止酷刑公约》实施条例下的暂缓驱逐出境与延缓驱逐出境
2. Withholding of Removal Under the INA and Regulations Implementing CAT and Deferral of Removal Under Regulations Implementing CAT
即使外国人不可入境或应被驱逐出境且已获得最终驱逐令,国土安全部在某些情况下驱逐该外国人的权力可能会受到执行美国关于不驱回(non-refoulement)条约义务的特定法律和法规条款的进一步限制。美国是1967年《关于难民地位的议定书》(以下简称《议定书》)的缔约国,该议定书除其他内容外,纳入了1951年《关于难民地位的公约》第33条。198 U.N.T.S. 137。第33条明确规定:“任何缔约国不得以任何方式将难民驱逐或遣返(refouler)至其生命或自由因种族、宗教、国籍、属于特定社会群体或政治见解而受到威胁的领土边境。”[51]美国也是《禁止酷刑公约》(CAT)的缔约国。[52]《禁止酷刑公约》第3条规定:“如有充分理由相信某人将有遭受酷刑的危险,任何缔约国不得将该人驱逐、遣返(`refouler')或引渡至另一国。”[53]
Even if an alien is inadmissible or deportable and has a final order of removal, DHS's authority to remove an alien in certain cases may be further restricted by certain statutory and regulatory provisions implementing U.S. treaty obligations concerning non-refoulement (non-return). The United States is a party to the 1967 Protocol relating to the Status of Refugees (Protocol), which incorporates, inter alia, Article 33 of the 1951 Convention relating to the Status of Refugees. 198 U.N.T.S. 137. Article 33 specifically provides that “[n]o contracting state shall expel or return ( refouler ) a refugee in any manner whatsoever to the frontier of territories where his life or freedom would be threatened on account of his race, religion, nationality, membership of a particular social group, or political opinion.” [51] The United States is also a party to CAT.[52] Article 3 of CAT requires that “[n]o State Party shall expel, return (`refouler') or extradite a person to another state where there are substantial grounds for believing that he would be in danger of being subjected to torture.” [53]
尽管这两项条约均非自动执行条约,美国已通过制定法律和法规来履行其在这些条约下的不驱回义务。[54]关于《议定书》,国会在1980年《难民法》中将美国的不驱回义务纳入实施,即《移民与国籍法》第241(b)(3)条,《美国法典》第8编第1231(b)(3)条。关于《禁止酷刑公约》,国会在1998年《外交事务改革与重组法》(FARRA,公法第105-277号,G分编,第2442(b)条,1998年10月21日)中指示相关机构发布法规,以履行美国在《禁止酷刑公约》第3条项下的义务。美国司法部(DOJ)于1999年发布了实施FARRA第2442条的法规。参见64 FR 8478(1999年2月19日)。目前,关于根据《移民与国籍法》第241(b)(3)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1231(b)(3)条暂缓驱逐出境,以及根据《禁止酷刑公约》暂缓和延缓驱逐出境的法规,主要编入《联邦法规汇编》第8编第208.16条至第208.18条以及第1208.16条至第1208.18条。
Though neither of these treaties is self-executing, the United States has implemented its non-refoulement obligations under these treaties in statutes and regulations.[54] With respect to Protocol, Congress implemented the United States' non-refoulement obligations as part of the Refugee Act of 1980, section 241(b)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3). With respect to CAT, Congress directed the appropriate agencies to publish regulations to implement the United States' obligations under Article 3 of the CAT in the Foreign Affairs Reform and Restructuring Act of 1998 (FARRA), Public Law 105-277, div. G., sec. 2442(b) (Oct. 21, 1998). The Department of Justice (DOJ) published regulations in 1999 implementing FARRA sec. 2442. See64 FR 8478 (Feb. 19, 1999). The regulations governing withholding of removal based on section 241(b)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3), and withholding and deferral of removal under CAT are now codified in principal part at 8 CFR 208.16 through 208.18 and 1208.16 through 1208.18.
根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第241(b)(3)条,8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3),获准暂缓驱逐出境的外国人,以及根据实施《禁止酷刑公约》(CAT)的法规(8 CFR 208.16(c)和1208.16(c))获准暂缓驱逐出境的外国人,如果该外国人曾参与纳粹迫害、参与种族灭绝、实施酷刑和法外处决行为、参与迫害他人、被判犯有特别严重罪行、在抵达美国前在境外犯有严重非政治性罪行,或对美国国家安全构成危险,则均须受暂缓驱逐出境的强制性禁止条款的约束。[55]然而,即使某外国人因属于上述暂缓驱逐出境强制性禁止条款之一而不符合暂缓驱逐出境资格,只要移民法官(IJ)或移民上诉委员会(BIA)已认定将该外国人遣送至某国将导致其极有可能遭受酷刑,美国国土安全部(DHS)仍不得将该外国人驱逐出美国。见8 CFR 208.17和1208.17。在此类情形下,移民法官或移民上诉委员会将推迟将该外国人遣返至该国。
Aliens granted withholding of removal based on section 241(b)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3), and aliens granted withholding of removal based on the regulations implementing CAT, 8 CFR 208.16(c) and 1208.16(c), are both subject to mandatory bars to withholding if the aliens participated in Nazi persecution, participated in genocide, committed an act of torture and extrajudicial killing, participated in the persecution of others, have been convicted of a particularly serious crime, have committed a serious nonpolitical crime outside the United States prior to arrival, or are a danger to the security of the United States.[55] However, even if an alien is not eligible for withholding under the provisions noted above because he or she is subject to one of the mandatory bars to withholding, DHS still is not permitted to remove an alien from the United States if an Immigration Judge (IJ) or the Board of Immigration Appeals (BIA) has determined that removal would result in the alien being removed to a country where he or she would more likely than not be tortured. 8 CFR 208.17 and 1208.17. In such instances, the IJ or BIA defers removal to that country.
根据《移民与国籍法》第241(b)(3)条,8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3)作出的暂缓驱逐出境或暂缓移除决定,或根据实施美国在《禁止酷刑公约》第3条项下义务的法规作出的决定(“CAT暂缓移除”)(若该外国人不受强制性禁止条款约束)以及CAT推迟移除,均属强制性措施,只要外国人达到举证责任要求,就必须予以批准。见8 CFR 1208.16(b)、(c)(4)和1208.17(a)。一旦外国人获准暂缓移除或推迟移除,除非其案件被重新审理且根据8 CFR 208.24或1208.24终止暂缓移除,或根据8 CFR 208.17或1208.17终止推迟移除,否则DHS不得将该外国人驱逐至暂缓或推迟移除所涉及的国家。在大多数情况下,根据实施CAT的法规获准暂缓移除或推迟移除的外国人将根据监督令获释,但此类监督令并不改变或影响暂缓移除或推迟移除裁定的非自由裁量性质,即使该外国人随后违反监督令中规定的释放条件亦然。此类违规行为可能导致该外国人被重新收押至美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)羁押,但除非其案件被重新审理且根据8 CFR 208.24或1208.24终止暂缓移除,或根据8 CFR 208.17或1208.17终止推迟移除,否则不会导致该外国人被实际驱逐出美国前往相关国家。
Withholding of deportation or removal based on section 241(b)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(b)(3), or the regulations implementing U.S. obligations under CAT Article 3 (“CAT withholding”) (if the alien is not subject to a mandatory bar) and CAT deferral of removal are mandatory and must be granted if the alien meets the burden of proof. See8 CFR 1208.16(b), (c)(4) and 1208.17(a). Once an alien has been granted withholding or deferral of removal, DHS cannot remove the alien to the country from which removal has been withheld or deferred unless the alien's case is reopened and withholding is terminated under 8 CFR 208.24 or 1208.24, or deferral is terminated under 8 CFR 208.17 or 1208.17. In most instances, an alien granted withholding of removal or deferral of removal under the regulations implementing CAT will be released pursuant to an order of supervision, but such an order does not alter or affect the nondiscretionary nature of the withholding or deferral of removal grant, even if the alien subsequently violates the conditions for release as specified in the order of supervision. Such violations could result in a return of the alien to ICE custody but will not result in the alien's actual removal from the United States to the relevant country or countries in question unless the alien's case is reopened, and withholding is terminated under 8 CFR 208.24 or 1208.24 or deferral of removal is terminated under 8 CFR 208.17 or 1208.17.
3. 假释
3. Parole
《移民与国籍法》赋予部长有限的自由裁量权,可不论入境申请人是否符合入境资格,仅为“出于紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益,根据个案情况”准许其“在[DHS]规定的条件下”暂时假释入境美国。[56]此外,一旦DHS认定暂时性、自由裁量性假释的目的已经达成,该外国人即须离开美国,“或被遣返回其原被假释前所处的羁押状态,此后其案件应按照任何其他申请入境美国者的案件同样方式继续处理。”[57]
The INA confers upon the Secretary the narrow discretionary authority to parole applicants for admission, regardless of admissibility, into the United States “temporarily under such conditions as [DHS] may prescribe only on a case-by-case basis for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit.” [56] Additionally, upon a finding by DHS that the purpose of the temporary, discretionary parole has been served, the alien is required to depart the United States “or be returned to the custody from which he was paroled and thereafter his case shall continue to be dealt with in the same manner as that of any other applicant for admission to the United States.” [57]
自19世纪末以来,美国政府一直以某种形式的假释方式,允许不符合入境资格的外国人进入并暂时留在美国境内。[58]国会直到1952年颁布《移民与国籍法》时才将这一假释权力正式成文化。[59]1952年《移民与国籍法》第212(d)(5)条授权司法部长可“出于紧急原因或被认为完全符合公共利益的原因”,准许外国人假释入境美国。[60]在随附1952年《移民与国籍法》的一份众议院报告中,国会指出,假释措施旨在适用于
Since the late 19th century, the Government has used some form of parole to allow inadmissible aliens to come into and temporarily remain in the United States.[58] Congress did not codify this parole authority until 1952, with the enactment of the INA.[59] Section 212(d)(5) of the 1952 INA authorized the Attorney General to parole an alien into the United States for “emergent reasons or for reasons deemed strictly in the public interest.” [60] In a House Report accompanying the 1952 INA, Congress indicated that parole was meant for
紧急情况,例如某外国人在移民官员尚未有机会对其进行检查之前即需要立即获得医疗救治的情形,以及在让某不符合入境资格的外国人留在(印刷页第34373页)美国境内完全符合公共利益的情形,例如,作为证人或出于起诉目的的情形。[61]
emergency cases, such as the case of an alien who requires immediate medical attention before there has been an opportunity for an immigration officer to inspect him, and in cases where it is strictly in the public interest to have an inadmissible alien present in the ( printed page 34373) United States, such as, for instance, a witness or for purposes of prosecution.[61]
然而,《移民与国籍法》并未明确规定该授权是否可用于对一批不符合入境资格的外国人集体实行假释。
The INA, however, did not address whether the authority could be used to parole groups of inadmissible aliens.
1952年以后,假释权力多次被用于假释大批不可准入的外国人,即来自匈牙利、古巴、中国、捷克斯洛伐克、苏联、乌干达和越南的难民。[62] 尽管国会中有些人批评这种对假释权力的使用,[63] 国会仍通过立法,为某些已被美国政府假释入境美国的外国人群体提供了获得合法永久居留权的途径。[64]
Subsequent to 1952, the parole authority was repeatedly used to parole large groups of inadmissible aliens, namely refugees from Hungary, Cuba, China, Czechoslovakia, the Soviet Union, Uganda, and Vietnam.[62] Although some in Congress criticized this use of the parole authority,[63] Congress passed legislation to provide a path to lawful permanent residence for certain groups of aliens who had been paroled into the United States by the U.S. Government.[64]
1980年,国会通过了《难民法》,对假释权力加以限缩,规定除非“就该特定外国人而言,出于公共利益的迫切理由”而需要假释而非以难民身份准予入境,否则禁止对难民给予假释。[65] 对非难民的假释权力保持不变。
In 1980, Congress passed the Refugee Act, narrowing the parole authority by prohibiting the parole of refugees unless “compelling reasons in the public interest with respect to that particular alien” required parole rather than admission as a refugee.[65] The parole authority for non-refugees remained the same.
一些学者认为,《难民法》的这项修正反映了国会对行政部门在此前数十年间使用假释权力的持续不满。[66] 伴随《难民法》提交的参议院报告指出,该法案的目的之一是“终止多年来对假释权力的临时性使用,这种使用一直依惯例而非依法律明确规定来实施。”[67]
According to some scholars, the Refugee Act's amendment represented continued congressional displeasure with the Executive Branch's use of the parole authority in the preceding decades.[66] The Senate Report accompanying the Refugee Act states that one of the Act's purposes was to “[e]nd[ ] the years of ad hoc use of the parole authority, which has been implemented by custom rather than clearly defined by law.” [67]
尽管受到国会的这种批评,行政部门在1980年至1996年期间仍继续使用假释权力对若干类别的外国人实行假释,包括针对越南及其他东南亚人群以及美国侨民的假释。[68] 1996年,国会通过了《非法移民改革和移民责任法》(IIRIRA)。[69] 作为移民法改革的一部分,国会专门针对广泛使用假释权力以允许难民群体进入美国一事表达了关切。国会对《移民与国籍法》(INA)第212(d)(5)(A)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1182(d)(5)(A)条的文本进行了修订,以明确规定司法部长只能“基于个案逐案审查,出于紧迫的人道主义理由或重大公共利益,酌情给予假释”(强调为原文所加)。[70]
Despite this congressional criticism, the Executive Branch continued to use the parole authority to parole categories of aliens from 1980 until 1996, including for Vietnamese and other Southeast Asian populations, and U.S. expatriates.[68] In 1996, Congress passed the IIRIRA.[69] As part of its reform of the immigration laws, Congress specifically addressed its concerns about the broad use of the parole authority to allow groups of refugees to come to the United States. Congress amended the text of section 212(d)(5)(A) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1182(d)(5)(A), to make clear that the Attorney General could grant parole, as a matter of discretion, “ only on a case-by-case basis for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit” (emphasis added).[70]
在国会于1996年进行修订之后,假释权力仍继续被广泛用于创设新的类别性假释项目。例如,2000年,假释权力被用于全面管理依法即将日落失效的《移民与国籍法》第217条、《美国法典》第8编第1187条项下的签证豁免试点项目,以避免因突然对来自大多数发达国家的商务或休闲访客实施签证要求而造成的国际旅行和贸易的全面中断,以及对美国经济和对外关系造成的严重损害。[71] 在该签证豁免试点项目下,2000年5月1日至10月1日期间,数千万外国访客被假释入境美国。[72] 在此后的若干年中,假释权力被用于创设规模较小的类别性项目。例如,布什政府于2007年设立了古巴家庭团聚假释项目。[73] 奥巴马政府设立了多个假释项目,包括2014年的海地家庭团聚假释项目,[74] 2016年的菲律宾二战老兵假释项目,[75] 以及2017年的国际企业家假释项目。[76] 近期,拜登政府设立了多个类别性假释项目,[77] 其中包括针对来自古巴、海地、尼加拉瓜和委内瑞拉的不可准入外国人的假释项目(“CHNV假释项目”)。[78]
Following Congress' amendment in 1996, the parole authority continued to be used expansively to create new categorical parole programs. In 2000, for example, the parole authority was used to manage the statutorily sunsetting Visa Waiver Pilot Program under section 217 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1187, in its entirety, to avoid the wholesale disruption of international travel and commerce, and the serious harm to the U.S. economy and foreign relations, that would have resulted from suddenly imposing visa requirements on visitors for business or pleasure from most developed countries.[71] Under this Visa Waiver Pilot Program, tens of millions of foreign visitors were paroled into the United States between May 1 and October 1, 2000.[72] In subsequent years, the parole authority was used to create smaller categorical programs. For example, the Bush Administration created the Cuban Family Reunification Parole program in 2007.[73] The Obama Administration created several parole programs, including the Haitian Family Reunification Parole Program in 2014,[74] the Filipino World War II Veterans Parole program in 2016,[75] and Parole for International Entrepreneurs in 2017.[76] More recently, the Biden Administration created several categorical parole programs,[77] including the parole programs for inadmissible aliens from Cuba, Haiti, Nicaragua, and Venezuela (“CHNV parole programs”).[78]
2025年7月4日,总统签署了H.R. 1号法案,即《公法》第119-21号,139 Stat. 72,使其成为法律。该法确立了一项针对任何被假释入境美国的外国人首次或续期就业许可申请的新费用,并且还规定就业许可的有效期上限为一年或该外国人假释期限,以两者中较短者为准。[79]
On July 4, 2025, the President signed H.R. 1, Public Law 119-21, 139 Stat. 72 into law. It established a new fee for an initial or renewal application for employment authorization by any alien paroled into the United States and also placed a limit on the validity of employment authorization to one year or the duration of the alien's parole, whichever is shorter.[79]
(印刷页第34374页)
( printed page 34374)
4. 暂缓遣返(Deferred Action)
4. Deferred Action
自19世纪末以来，最高法院一直承认行政部门拥有驱逐或排除外国人出境美国的权力，并将此种权力视为“属于美国政府根据宪法所授予的主权权力之一部分的主权附带权利”。[80] 这项权力已被编入1952年《移民与国籍法》（Immigration and Nationality Act），8 U.S.C. 1101及以下条款。多年来，国会已对《移民与国籍法》（INA）中的移民执法权力和规定作出澄清并予以加强。[81] 2002年，随着国土安全部（DHS）的成立，国会通过授予部长执行移民法的权力，授权其拘捕、拘留并将外国人驱逐出美国，并制定“国家移民执法政策与优先事项”，从而重申了这一权力。[82]
Since the late 1800s, the Supreme Court has recognized the authority of the Executive Branch to expel or exclude aliens from the United States and viewed such power as “an incident of sovereignty belonging to the government of the United States as a part of those sovereign powers delegated by the Constitution.” [80] This authority was codified in the Immigration and Nationality Act of 1952, 8 U.S.C. 1101 et seq.
