2. 拟议规则的背景与目的
2. Background and Purpose of the Proposed Rule
USCIS正在起草相关法规,以使其授予工作许可的自由裁量权与本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项保持一致,[176]包括通过严格执行和管理我国移民法律,促使具有驱逐令的外国人被及时驱逐出境。
USCIS is drafting regulations to align its discretionary authority to grant employment authorization with the Administration's current immigration enforcement priorities,[176] including the prompt removal of aliens with removal orders through the rigorous enforcement and administration of our immigration laws.
a. 对处于监督下自愿离境(OSUP)状态的外国人给予自由裁量性工作许可(第(c)(18)项)
a. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens on OSUP ((c)(18))
如上文所述,美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)致力于迅速将受最终驱逐令约束的外国人从美国驱逐出境。驱逐行动需要综合协调、管理和推动方面的努力。根据法律规定,国土安全部(DHS)必须在最终驱逐令发布后90天内(“驱逐期限”)将被拘留的外国人驱逐出境或释放。[177] 此外,法律明确禁止国土安全部在驱逐期限内释放因犯罪或恐怖活动而被判驱逐的外国人。[178] 对于被拘留超过驱逐期限的外国人,国土安全部必须遵守Zadvydas案的裁决(本拟议规则通篇讨论该案),[179] 该裁决认为,一旦确定“在可预见的未来内不存在显著的驱逐可能性”,持有最终驱逐令的外国人就不能被继续拘留(除非存在特殊情况)。[180] (印刷页34407) 根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第241(a)(3)条,即8 U.S.C. 1231(a)(3)的规定,从ICE拘留中获释的持有最终驱逐令的外国人应受到监管。[181]
As discussed, ICE works to promptly remove aliens subject to a final order of removal from the United States. Removal operations require integrated coordination, management, and facilitation efforts. By law, DHS is required to remove or release a detained alien ordered removed within a period of 90 days (“removal period”) after the issuance of a final order of removal.[177] Furthermore, the law expressly prohibits DHS from releasing an alien during the removal period if the alien was ordered removed based on criminal grounds and/or terrorist activities.[178] For aliens detained beyond the removal period, DHS must comply with Zadvydas (discussed throughout this proposed rule),[179] which held that an alien with a final order of removal cannot be kept in detention (unless special circumstances exist) once it has been determined that there is not a “significant likelihood of removal in the reasonably foreseeable future.” [180] ( printed page 34407) Aliens with final orders of removal who are released from ICE custody under section 241(a)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(a)(3), are subject to supervision.[181]
如上所述,国土安全部目前根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)的规定,向已被判处驱逐并根据《移民与国籍法》第241(a)(3)条(即8 U.S.C. 1231(a)(3))以监管令形式暂时获释的外国人提供就业授权资格。参见8 CFR 241.5(c)、274a.12(c)(18)。
As noted above, DHS currently extends eligibility for employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18) to aliens who have been ordered removed and have been temporarily released from detention under section 241(a)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1231(a)(3), on an order of supervision. See8 CFR 241.5(c), 274a.12(c)(18).
如前言中详细说明,国土安全部已确定,就业授权应限于被判处驱逐并根据监管令(OSUP)暂时获释的外国人中的一部分,以便更好地与国土安全部的执法使命及本届政府当前的移民执法重点(包括第14159号行政命令中所述内容)保持一致,并加强对美国工人的保护。
As explained in detail in the preamble, DHS has determined that employment authorization should be limited to a subset of aliens ordered removed and temporarily released on OSUP to better align with the DHS enforcement mission and the Administration's current immigration enforcement priorities, including those outlined in E.O. 14159, and efforts to strengthen protections of American workers.
此外,国土安全部拟要求符合本例外情形的外国人证明其就业存在经济必要性。拟议的法规修改将要求外国人填写并提交I-765WS表格,并提交证明文件[182]以支持其就业经济必要性的主张。
Further, DHS intends to require aliens who qualify under this exception to establish an economic necessity for employment. The proposed regulatory change will require that aliens complete and submit Form I-765WS, as well as submit documentary evidence [182] to support their claim of economic necessity for employment.
国土安全部拟将本规则所作的变更仅适用于在本规则(如最终定稿)生效日期当日或之后根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)提交的初次申请和续签申请。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)不会将本规则所作的变更适用于在本规则(如最终定稿)生效日期之前收到的任何待决的补发工作许可证(EAD)申请,也不会适用于新提交的补发EAD申请,因为此类裁定不被视为就业授权的新批准,而是基于先前已获批准期限对EAD的补发。
DHS proposes to apply changes made by this rule only to initial and renewal applications under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18) filed on or after the effective date of the rule, if finalized. USCIS would not apply the changes made by this rule to any pending application for a replacement EAD received before the effective date of the rule, if finalized, or to new applications for replacement EADs, because such adjudications are not considered a new grant of employment authorization but a replacement of an EAD based on a previously authorized period.
b. 对获得延缓执行遣返(Deferred Action)的外国人的酌情就业授权((c)(14))
b. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Granted Deferred Action ((c)(14))
国土安全部目前根据(c)(14)类别向获得延缓执行遣返的外国人提供就业授权资格。参见8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)。此类外国人若要获得就业授权,必须提交I-765表格,并附上所需文件及相应费用。[183] 如果USCIS根据(c)(14)类别批准该外国人的I-765表格,其有效期通常与延缓执行遣返的授予期限相同,并将在延缓执行遣返期限的结束日期终止。[184] 如前言中详细说明,国土安全部已确定并拟议,对获得延缓执行遣返的外国人,其就业授权应限制在不超过一年的期限内。
DHS currently extends eligibility for employment authorization to aliens who have been granted deferred action under the (c)(14) category. See8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14). In order for such aliens to obtain employment authorization, the alien must file Form I-765 accompanied by required documentation and the proper fee.[183] If USCIS approves the alien's Form I-765 under the (c)(14) category, the validity period generally runs for the same period of time as the grant of deferred action and will end on the end date of the period of deferred action.[184] As explained in detail in the preamble, DHS has determined and is proposing that employment authorization should be limited for aliens granted deferred action not to exceed one year.
国土安全部拟将本规则所作的变更仅适用于在本规则(如最终定稿)生效日期当日或之后根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14)提交的初次申请和续签申请。USCIS不会将本规则(如最终定稿)所作的变更适用于在该生效日期之前收到的任何待决的补发EAD申请,也不会适用于新提交的补发EAD申请,因为此类裁定不被视为就业授权的新批准,而是基于先前已获批准期限对EAD的补发。
DHS proposes to apply changes made by this rule only to initial and renewal applications under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(14) filed on or after the effective date of the rule, if finalized. USCIS would not apply the changes made by this rule, if finalized, to any pending application for a replacement EAD received before the effective date of the rule or to new applications for replacement EADs, because such adjudications are not considered a new grant of employment authorization but a replacement of an EAD based on a previously authorized period.
c. 对获准假释进入美国的外国人的酌情就业授权((c)(11))
c. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Paroled Into the United States ((c)(11))
外国人作为入境申请人,可以基于紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益请求准予假释进入美国。假释允许可能不符合入境资格或在其他方面不符合入境条件的外国人暂时假释进入美国境内一段时间。通常情况下,假释在到期日或假释人离开美国或获得移民身份(以先发生者为准)时终止。除少数例外情况外,美国国土安全部(DHS)目前将就业许可资格扩大适用于根据(c)(11)类别获得假释的外国人。见8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11)。此类外国人若要获得就业许可,必须提交I-765表格,并附具所需文件及相应费用。[185]如果美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)根据(c)(11)类别批准外国人的I-765表格,则就业许可的有效期为外国人假释期限或一年,以两者中较短者为准。[186]正如序言中详细说明的那样,国土安全部已确定,应进一步限制获准假释的外国人的就业许可,以更好地契合国土安全部的执法使命及本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项,包括第14159号行政命令中所列事项,以及加强保护美国工人的各项努力。
Aliens who are applicants for admission may request to be paroled into the country based on urgent humanitarian reasons or a significant public benefit. Parole allows an alien who may be inadmissible or otherwise ineligible for admission into the United States to be paroled into the country for a temporary period of time. Generally, parole ends upon an expiration date or when a parolee departs the United States or acquires an immigration status, whichever occurs first. With some exceptions, DHS currently extends eligibility for employment authorization to aliens who have been granted parole under the (c)(11) category. See8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11). In order for such aliens to obtain employment authorization, the alien must file Form I-765 accompanied by required documentation and the proper fee.[185] If USCIS approves an alien's Form I-765 under the (c)(11) category, the validity period for employment authorization will be the shorter of either the duration of the alien's parole or one year.[186] As explained in detail in the preamble, DHS has determined that employment authorization should be further limited for aliens granted parole to better align with the DHS enforcement mission and the Administration's current immigration enforcement priorities, including those outlined in E.O. 14159, and efforts to strengthen protections of American workers.
国土安全部提议,本规则所做的变更仅适用于本规则(如最终确定)生效日期当日或之后提交的根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11)提出的初次申请和续签申请。对于在本规则(如最终确定)生效日期之前收到的任何待决的更换版就业许可证(EAD)申请,或对于新提交的更换版EAD申请,美国公民及移民服务局均不会适用本规则所做的变更,因为此类裁定并不被视为新的就业许可授予,而是基于此前已获授权期限对EAD进行更换。
DHS proposes to apply changes made by this rule only to initial and renewal applications under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(11) filed on or after the effective date of the rule, if finalized. USCIS would not apply the changes made by this rule to any pending application for a replacement EAD received before the effective date of the rule, if finalized, or to new applications for replacement EADs because such adjudications are not considered a new grant of employment authorization but a replacement of an EAD based on a previously authorized period.
3. 受影响人群
3. Population
可能受本拟议规则影响的人群包括:已有最终驱逐令但根据监管令暂时获准解除拘留,并根据(c)(18)类别获得就业许可的外国人;获准延缓执行遣返(暂缓遣返)并根据(c)(14)类别获得就业许可的外国人;以及基于紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益获准假释进入美国,并根据(c)(11)类别获得就业许可的外国人。
The populations that could be affected by this proposed rule consist of: aliens who have a final order of removal but who are temporarily released from custody on an order of supervision and are employment authorized under the (c)(18) category; aliens granted deferred action and are employment authorized under the (c)(14) category; and aliens granted parole into the country based on urgent humanitarian reasons or a significant public benefit and are employment authorized under the (c)(11) category.
如序言中所述,凡曾因任何刑事犯罪行为被逮捕、起诉或定罪,或已承认实施暴力或危险犯罪,或有证据表明属于帮派或恐怖组织成员的外国人,均不应获得自由裁量权的有利行使,除非存在重大的相反公共利益考量因素,(原书第34408页)该考量因素可能包括该外国人留在美国协助美国境内执法活动。这些拟议变更不仅将影响(c)(11)、(c)(14)和(c)(18)类人群,还将影响所有酌情裁量类EAD人群,[187]但不包括(c)(8)、[188](c)(19)、(c)(20)、(c)(22)和(c)(24)类别。虽然部分外国人,例如因监管令而被释放者,属于已知的已定罪罪犯,但国土安全部无法准确估计,若本拟议规则最终颁布为正式规则,将有多少外国人可能因自由裁量而被拒绝就业许可。自由裁量分析具有个案具体性,通常是在官员确定外国人符合所有适用的门槛资格要求之后进行评估的。该分析涉及审查个案中所有相关的具体事实和情况,并在案件记录全局范围内权衡特定案件中存在的所有积极因素与任何消极因素。此外,国土安全部也不掌握会因后续刑事定罪或帮派、恐怖组织关联而被自由裁量拒绝的外国人数量。基于上述原因,我们无法估计将有多少外国人会因这些因素而被自由裁量拒绝。然而,国土安全部承认,对于那些因犯罪记录而不再被认定符合酌情裁量类EAD资格的外国人而言,将产生类似的成本,本分析将对此作进一步讨论。
As noted in the preamble, aliens who have been arrested for, indicted for, or convicted of any criminal act, have admitted to committing a violent or dangerous crime, or for whom evidence exists that the alien is a member of a gang or terrorist organization do not warrant a favorable exercise of discretion, unless there are significant countervailing public interest, which ( printed page 34408) may include the presence of the alien in the United States to assist law enforcement activity in the United States. These proposed changes will not only affect the (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) populations, but all discretionary EAD populations,[187] excluding (c)(8),[188] (c)(19), (c)(20), (c)(22), and (c)(24). While some aliens, such as those released on orders of supervision, are known convicted criminals, DHS is unable to precisely estimate the number of aliens who could potentially be denied employment authorization as a matter of discretion should this proposed rule be promulgated as a final rule. The discretionary analysis is case-specific and typically assessed after an officer has determined that the alien meets all applicable threshold eligibility requirements. It involves the review of all relevant, specific facts and circumstances in an individual case and weighing all the positive factors present in a particular case against any negative factors in the totality of the record. Further, DHS does not know the number of aliens who would be denied as a matter of discretion because of subsequent criminal convictions or gang/terrorist organization affiliations. For these reasons, we cannot estimate how many aliens would be denied as a matter of discretion based on these factors. However, DHS recognizes that there would be similar costs, which are further discussed in this analysis, for those aliens that are no longer found eligible for employment authorization under a discretionary EAD based on criminal history.
国土安全部(DHS)根据2015财年至2024财年的历史数据估算受影响人数。预计人数估算是根据以下两种情形编制的：(1) 无本规则情形，以及(2) 有本规则情形。这两种情形稍后将用于“货币化影响分析”部分，以估算本拟议规则的经济影响。
DHS estimates the affected population based on historical data for FY 2015 through FY 2024. The projected population estimates are developed under two scenarios: (1) without this rule and (2) with this rule. The two scenarios will later be used in the “Monetized Impact Analysis” section to estimate the economic impact of this proposed rule.
a. 根据监管令临时释放的外国人自由裁量就业授权（(c)(18)）
a. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens on OSUP ((c)(18))
国土安全部根据2015财年至2024财年的历史数据估算受影响人数。表V.4显示了2015财年至2024财年期间，根据(c)(18)类别，为根据监管令临时释放的外国人使用I-765表申请就业授权的初次申请和续签申请的年度收案量和批准量。
DHS estimates the affected population based on historical data for FY 2015 through FY 2024. Table V.4 shows the annual receipts and approvals for initial and renewal applications for employment authorization using Form I-765 under the (c)(18) category for aliens temporarily released on an order of supervision for FY 2015 through FY 2024.
（印刷页34409）
( printed page 34409)
在2015财年至2024财年期间，就业授权初次申请收案量从2015财年的9,632件下降至2018财年的4,421件（该期间的最低值），随后上升至2022财年的10,435件（该期间的最高值），之后又下降至2024财年的5,228件。就业授权初次申请批准量从2015财年的最高值8,745件下降至2021财年的最低值3,210件，随后上升至2024财年的4,420件。就业授权续签申请收案量从2015财年的22,816件下降至2019财年的19,315件（该期间的最低值），随后上升至2023财年的27,840件（该期间的最高值），之后又下降至2024财年的27,433件。就业授权续签申请批准量从2015财年的21,241件下降至2021财年的最低值17,220件，随后再次上升至2024财年的最高值27,796件。虽然国土安全部估计拟议规则将减少符合就业授权资格的外国人数量，并预计(c)(18)类别的初次申请和续签申请的收案量和批准量都将下降，但由于本分析中进一步讨论的原因，国土安全部无法确定下降的幅度。
The number of initial receipts of employment authorization over the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 decreased from 9,632 in FY 2015 through 4,421 in FY 2018 (a period low), then increased to 10,435 in FY 2022 (a period high), and subsequently, decreased to 5,228 in FY 2024. The number of initial approvals of employment authorization decreased from a high of 8,745 in FY 2015 to a low of 3,210 in FY 2021, then increased to 4,420 in FY 2024. The number of renewal receipts of employment authorization decreased from 22,816 in FY 2015 to 19,315 in FY 2019 (a period low) before increasing to 27,840 FY 2023 (a period high) and subsequently decreased to 27,433 in FY 2024. The number of renewal approvals for employment authorization decreased from 21,241 in FY 2015 to a low of 17,220 in FY 2021 before increasing again to a high of 27,796 in FY 2024. While DHS estimates the proposed rule would reduce the number of aliens eligible for employment authorization and anticipates a decline in (c)(18) receipts and approvals for both initial and renewal applications, DHS is unable to determine the magnitude of decline for reasons discussed further in this analysis.
表V.5根据表V.4中列出的收案量，显示了初次申请和续签申请收案量的年增长率。对于初次申请收案量，2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年增长率下降了9.8%；2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年增长率下降了3.7%；2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年增长率下降了5.9%。[189] 对于续签申请收案量，2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年增长率下降了3.3%；2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年增长率上升了3.8%；2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年增长率上升了1.9%。[190] 国土安全部使用这些增长率来估算在有本规则和无本规则两种情形下初次申请和续签申请预计收案量。
Table V.5 shows annual growth rates of initial and renewal receipts based on the receipts presented in Table V.4. For initial receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 decreased 9.8 percent; the 5-year annual growth rate for the period 2020 through 2024 decreased 3.7 percent; and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 decreased 5.9 percent.[189] For renewal receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 decreased 3.3 percent; the 5-year annual growth rate for the period 2020 through 2024 increased 3.8 percent; and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 increased 1.9 percent.[190] DHS uses these growth rates to estimate the projected number of initial and renewal receipts under the two scenarios with and without this rule.
为估算无本规则情形下初次申请预计收案量，国土安全部选择使用2020财年至2024财年期间初次申请收案量的下降增长率负3.7%。通过选择这一较为保守的年增长率，初次申请收案量的估算预测值将更高，这将导致潜在成本估算的范围更大。
To estimate the projected number of initial receipts without this rule, DHS chooses to use the declining growth rate of −3.7 percent for initial receipts for the period FY 2020 through FY 2024. By choosing this more conservative annual growth rate, the estimated projection will be higher for initial receipts, which would lead to a greater range of potential cost estimates.
为估算无本规则情形下续签申请预计收案量，国土安全部承认，根据监管令临时释放的外国人持有驱逐令，并且正在持续不断地从美国被驱逐出境。此外，初次申请收案量的下降增长率在某一时点将导致续签申请收案量趋于平稳或下降。因此，我们认为使用上升的年增长率来估算续签申请预计收案量是不合理的。在本分析中，我们使用2015财年至2019财年期间负3.3%的5年平均年增长率（表V.5）。
To estimate the projected number of renewal receipts without this rule, DHS acknowledges that aliens temporarily released on OSUP have removal orders and are deported from the United States on an ongoing basis. Additionally, the declining growth rate for initial receipts would, at some point, result in either a plateau or a decrease for renewal receipts. Therefore, we do not find it reasonable to use an increasing annual growth rate to estimate the projected number of renewal receipts. In this analysis, we use the 5-year average annual growth rate of −3.3 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.5).
接下来，在表V.6中，我们计算了初次申请和续签申请收案量和批准量的平均数量，以及相应的批准率。[191] 2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请的批准率为83.6%，2020财年至2024财年期间为64.5%，2015财年至2024财年期间为73.7%。2015财年至2019财年期间续签申请的批准率为94.2%，2020财年至2024财年期间为93.9%，2015财年至2024财年期间为94.0%。
Next, in Table V.6, we calculate the average number of receipts and approvals, along with approval rates for initial and renewal receipts.[191] The approval rate for initials for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 83.6 percent, for FY 2020 through FY 2024 is 64.5 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 73.7 percent. The approval rate for renewals for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 94.2 percent, for FY 2020 through FY 2024 is 93.9 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 94.0 percent.
（印刷页34410）
( printed page 34410)
为了在表V.7中预测在没有本规则的情况下的初次批准数量,国土安全部(DHS)选择了较为保守的初次批准率,即2015财年至2019财年的5年年均比率83.6%。此外,为了预测续期批准数量,国土安全部选择了较为保守的续期批准率,即2015财年至2019财年的5年年均比率94.2%。然而,我们注意到,在表V.6中分析的三个时期内,平均续期批准率的差异范围相对较小,仅为0.3个百分点(94.2%减去93.9%)。通过选择这些年度批准率,“无本规则情景”下的初次批准和续期批准预测数值将更高,这将导致潜在成本估算的范围更大。
To project the number of initial approvals without this rule in Table V.7, DHS chooses the more conservative initial approval rate by choosing the 5-year average annual rate of 83.6 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019. Moreover, to project the number of renewal approvals, DHS chooses the more conservative renewal approval rate by choosing the 5-year annual rate of 94.2 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019. However, we note that the average renewal approval rate over the three periods analyzed in Table V.6 are within a relatively small range of 0.3 percentage (94.2 percent minus 93.9 percent). By choosing these annual approval rates, the projections under the “without the rule scenario” will be higher for initial and renewal approvals, which will lead to a greater range of potential cost estimates.
为预测2025财年的初次申请和续签申请接收量,我们依据本届政府现行的移民执法优先事项对相关人口数量进行了估算。2015财年至2019财年期间的初次申请和续签申请接收量平均值更符合上述执法方针,可作为2025财年的合理估算依据。为预测2025财年的初次申请接收量,采用了2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请接收量的5年平均年度数值6,944件。2025财年初次批准量的预测值,是以平均初次批准率83.6%(表V.6)乘以2025财年初次申请接收量估算值(6,944件)计算得出,结果为5,805件(表V.7)。
To project FY 2025 initial and renewal receipts, we estimate the population in accordance with the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities. The average initial and renewal receipts from FY 2015 through FY 2019 fall more in line with these directives and are reasonable estimates for FY 2025. To project FY 2025 initial receipts, the 5-year average annual number of initial receipts of 6,944 from FY 2015 through FY 2019 is used. The projected FY 2025 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the average initial approval rate of 83.6 percent (Table V.6) by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2025 (6,944), which equals 5,805 (Table V.7).
为预测2026财年的初次申请接收量,将2020财年至2024财年期间的5年平均年增长率负3.7%(表V.5)乘以2025财年初次申请接收量预测值(6,944件),结果为负257件(四舍五入)。用2025财年初次申请接收量预测值减去257件,结果为6,687件(表V.7)。[192] 2026财年初次批准量的计算方法为,以相同的平均初次批准率83.6%乘以2026财年初次申请接收量估算值(6,687件),结果为5,590件(四舍五入)。此后各年度均依此方法重复计算。
To project FY 2026 initial receipts, the 5-year average annual growth rate of −3.7 percent from FY 2020 through FY 2024 (Table V.5) is multiplied by the number of projected initial receipts from FY 2025 (6,944), which equals −257 (rounded). Subtracting 257 from the projected initial receipts for FY 2025 equals 6,687 (Table V.7).[192] The FY 2026 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the same average initial approval rate of 83.6 percent by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2026 (6,687), which equals 5,590 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years.
为推算2025财年续签收案数,我们采用2015财年至2019财年期间负3.3%的五年平均年增长率(表V.5)。[193] 2025财年续签批准数的计算方法是将94.2%的平均续签批准率(表V.6)乘以2025财年续签收案估计数(23,047),等于21,710(表V.7)。
To project FY 2025 renewal receipts, we use the 5-year average annual growth rate of −3.3 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.5).[193] The FY 2025 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the average renewal approval rate of 94.2 percent (Table V.6) by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2025 (23,047), which equals 21,710 (Table V.7).
为推算2026财年续签收案数,采用2015财年至2019财年期间较为保守的负3.3%的五年年增长率(表V.5),乘以2025财年预计续签收案数(23,047),等于负761(四舍五入)。将761从2025财年预计续签收案数中减去,等于22,286(表V.7)。[194] 2026财年预计续签批准数的计算方法是将94.2%的平均续签批准率乘以2026财年续签收案估计数(22,286),等于20,993(四舍五入)。该过程随后在后续各年度中重复进行。这些推算数据见表V.7。
To project FY 2026 renewal receipts, the more conservative 5-year annual growth rate of −3.3 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.5) is multiplied by the number of projected renewal receipts from FY 2025 (23,047), which equals −761 (rounded). Subtracting 761 from the projected renewal receipts for FY 2025 equals 22,286 (Table V.7).[194] The projected number of FY 2026 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the average renewal approval rate of 94.2 percent by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2026 (22,286), which equals 20,993 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years. These projections are shown in Table V.7.
(印刷页第34411页)
( printed page 34411)
本拟议规则将取消以临时方式批准监督下释放(OSUP)的外国人获得就业许可的资格,但对于国土安全部(DHS)已认定因所有被要求出具旅行证件的国家均未能出具此类证件而使遣返不具可行性的外国人除外。为推算遣返不具可行性的外国人数量,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)从美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)获取了过去10个财年中因无法获得旅行证件而被释放出拘留的外国人数量数据。表V.8显示了在相应财年中以临时方式批准监督下释放且旅行证件申请被拒的外国人数量。国土安全部估计,本拟议规则将导致以临时方式批准监督下释放且有资格获得就业许可的外国人数量减少,并将导致在该例外情形下最多有322名外国人仍具有就业许可资格。
This proposed rule would eliminate the eligibility for employment authorization for aliens temporarily released on OSUP with an exception of aliens for whom DHS has determined removal is impracticable because all countries from which DHS has requested travel documents have failed to issue such documents. To estimate the number of aliens whose removal is impracticable, USCIS obtained data from ICE over the last 10 fiscal years on the number of aliens released from custody who have been unable to obtain travel documents. Table V.8 shows the number of aliens temporarily released on OSUP denied a travel document in the corresponding fiscal year. DHS estimates this proposed rule would result in fewer aliens temporarily released on OSUP who are eligible for employment authorization and would result in a maximum of 322 aliens remaining eligible for employment authorization under the exception.
根据实施《禁止酷刑公约》(CAT)的法规,即8 CFR 208.17和1208.17条款,获准暂缓驱逐的外国人将根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)条款有资格获得工作许可。获准CAT暂缓驱逐的外国人将持续暂缓驱逐,直至该暂缓被终止为止。[195] 表V.9显示了2015财年至2024财年获准CAT暂缓驱逐案件的数量(印刷页第34412页)。自2015财年以来,获准CAT暂缓驱逐的案件数量呈上升趋势,在2023财年达到316起案件的高点,随后在2024财年下降至245起案件。基于10年平均值,年度案件数量约为167起。[196]
Aliens who have been granted deferral of removal under the regulations implementing CAT at 8 CFR 208.17 and 1208.17 will be eligible for employment authorization pursuant to 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18). Aliens granted CAT deferral of removal continue to have their removal deferred until it is terminated.[195] Table V.9 shows the number of CAT cases granted deferral of ( printed page 34412) removal for FY 2015 through FY 2024. Since FY 2015, the number of CAT cases granted deferral of removal has trended upward reaching a high of 316 cases in FY 2023 before decreasing to 245 cases in FY 2024. The annual number of cases is approximately 167 based on a 10-year average.[196]
基于国土安全部(DHS)已确定因所有被DHS要求出具旅行证件的国家均明确拒绝出具此类证件而使驱逐不可行的外国人这一例外情形(322起),以及获准CAT暂缓驱逐的例外情形(167起),DHS估计,根据本拟议规则,未来仍将有资格获得工作许可的(c)(18)类别初始批准案件数量的上限估计值为每年489起(322加167)。DHS认识到,上限估计值并未考虑因后续定罪而不再具备资格的外国人数量。DHS同样不知道有多少外国人将根据经济必要性要求具备资格,也不知道有多少人将申请或因其他考量因素而被拒绝,例如外国人遵守监管令条件的情况,以及外国人的犯罪历史,包括但不限于外国人从监管令下获释后发生的任何刑事逮捕、指控、起诉或定罪。DHS认识到,如果322起可能属于该例外情形的潜在(c)(18)类别初始批准案件中有任何外国人未申请工作许可或被拒绝工作许可,则489的上限估计值将被高估。因此,我们所采用的489上限估计值假设,所有因无法获得旅行证件或根据实施CAT第三条的法规获准暂缓驱逐而暂时从监管令下获释的外国人中,百分之百将在本拟议规则下仍具备工作许可资格。
Based on the exception regarding aliens for whom DHS has determined removal is impracticable because all countries from which DHS has requested travel documents have affirmatively declined to issue such documents (322), and the grant of CAT deferral of removal exception (167), DHS estimates an upper bound estimate for initial (c)(18) category approvals that would remain eligible for employment authorization under this proposed rule in the future is 489 annually (322 plus 167). DHS recognizes an upper bound estimate does not account for the number of aliens who would no longer be eligible due to subsequent convictions. DHS also does not know how many aliens would be eligible under the economic necessity requirement or how many would apply for or be denied for other considerations, such as the alien's compliance with the order of supervision conditions and the alien's criminal history, including but not limited to any criminal arrests, charges, indictments, or convictions subsequent to the alien's release from custody on an order of supervision. DHS recognizes if any of the 322 potential initial (c)(18) category approvals who may fall under the exception do not apply for work authorization or are denied employment authorization that the upper bound estimate of 489 would be an overestimate. Thus, the upper bound estimate of 489 we use assumes that 100 percent of aliens temporarily released on OSUP who have either been unable to obtain travel documents or who have been granted deferral of removal under the regulations implementing CAT Article 3, would remain employment authorization eligible under this proposed rule.
此外,我们使用167(表V.10(A)A列)作为下限估计值,以涵盖根据实施CAT的8 CFR 208.17和1208.17法规获准暂缓驱逐、并将根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)条款有资格获得工作许可的外国人群体。[197] DHS强调,尽管获准CAT暂缓驱逐的外国人具备工作许可资格,但美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)仅在外国人值得获得有利的自由裁量权行使时,才会根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18)条款批准工作许可。DHS认识到,如果167起可能属于获准CAT暂缓驱逐例外情形的潜在(c)(18)类别批准案件中有任何外国人未申请工作许可(印刷页第34413页)或被拒绝工作许可,则167的下限估计值将被高估。因此,167的下限估计值假设,所有属于获准CAT暂缓驱逐例外情形的外国人中,百分之百将申请工作许可并在本拟议规则下获得批准。
Additionally, we use a lower bound estimate of 167 (Table V.10(A) column A) to capture the populations of aliens who have been granted deferral of removal under the regulations implementing CAT at 8 CFR 208.17 and 1208.17 and will be eligible for employment authorization pursuant to 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18).[197] DHS emphasizes that while aliens who are granted CAT deferral of removal are eligible for employment authorization, USCIS would only grant employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(18) if the alien warrants a favorable exercise of discretion. DHS recognizes if any of the 167 potential (c)(18) category approvals who may fall under the grant of CAT deferral of removal exception do not apply for ( printed page 34413) work authorization or are denied employment authorization that the lower bound estimate of 167 would be an overestimate. Thus, the lower bound estimate of 167 assumes that 100 percent of aliens that fall under the grant of CAT deferral of removal exception would apply for work authorization and be approved under this proposed rule.
这些上限和下限估计值被用作初始接收数量的预测值,取决于从ICE监管下获释但无法获得旅行证件的外国人的平均数量,以及获准CAT暂缓驱逐的外国人的平均数量。从2015财年到2024财年,从ICE监管下获释的外国人数量下降了约67.5%,而获准CAT暂缓驱逐的案件数量增加了约102.5%(表V.8和表V.9)。[198] 在本分析中,DHS依据这些群体的10年平均值,因为存在本规则制定之外的各种因素,可能导致被认定为无法获得旅行证件或获准CAT暂缓驱逐的外国人数量减少或增加。然而,DHS目前无法确定这些群体规模的趋势将会增加、减少还是保持稳定。因此,DHS在本分析中使用各自的10年平均值。
These upper and lower bound estimates are used as the projected number of initial receipts and depend on the average number of aliens released from ICE custody who are unable to obtain travel documents and aliens granted CAT deferral of removal. From FY 2015 through FY 2024, the number of aliens released from ICE custody decreased approximately 67.5 percent and cases granted CAT deferral of removals increased approximately 102.5 percent (Tables V.8 and V.9).[198] For this analysis, DHS relies on 10-year averages for these populations as there are various factors outside of this rulemaking that may result in a decrease or increase in the number of aliens identified as unable to obtain travel documents or granted CAT deferral of removal. However, DHS cannot predict with certainty at this time if the trend in the size of these populations would increase, decrease, or remain stable. Therefore, DHS uses the respective 10-year averages for this analysis.
国土安全部(DHS)估计,根据本拟议规则,最初批准的就业授权中仍有资格续签的比例下限,从2025财年的2.9%到2034财年的4.0%不等(表V.10(A)第C栏)。[199]在假设最初批准中具有续签资格的比例相同的前提下,我们将续签受理和批准的总数乘以这些百分比,得出每个财年对应的续签就业授权文件(EAD)下限估计值(表V.10(A)第E栏和第G栏)。此外,我们还假设最初批准中具有续签资格的比例相同,从而估计上限值。表V.10(B)重复了最初申请和续签申请上限数量的估计值。
DHS estimates that the lower bound share of initial grants of employment authorization that would continue to be eligible for renewal under this proposed rule ranges from 2.9 percent in FY 2025 to 4.0 percent in FY 2034 (Table V.10(A) column C).[199] Under the assumption that the same share of initial approvals would be eligible as renewals, we multiply the renewal receipts and approvals populations by these percentages to obtain a corresponding lower bound renewal EAD estimate for each fiscal year (Table V.10(A) columns E and G). Further, we estimate the upper bound assuming that the same share of initial approvals would be eligible as renewals. Table V.10(B) repeats the estimates for the upper bound populations for initials and renewals.
(印刷页第34414页)
( printed page 34414)
我们估计续签批准数量的下限区间为626至647(表V.10(A)第G栏)。国土安全部认识到,预计的续签批准数量可能无法完全反映出,如果外国人的雇主未注册E-Verify系统并选择不注册,且该外国人无法找到参与E-Verify且资格良好的美国雇主提供的替代工作,则将不再符合获得就业授权资格的外国人人数。一些申请续签的外国人目前可能也未在就业,因此不符合续签的新要求。此外,国土安全部不清楚这些外国人中有多少人符合经济必要性要求,也不清楚有多少人将被认定不应酌情获得就业授权。国土安全部认识到,如果部分续签批准申请人未提出申请或被拒绝获得就业授权,则估计的626至647的下限区间可能会更低。[200]
We estimate the lower bound range for renewal approvals to be 626 to 647 (Table V.10(A) Column G). DHS recognizes that the projected renewal approvals may not fully account for the number of aliens who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization due to the proposed E-Verify requirement if the aliens' employers are not enrolled and opt not to enroll in E-Verify, and if the aliens are unable to find alternative employment with a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify. Some aliens applying for renewal may also not be currently employed and therefore would not meet the new requirements for renewal. Additionally, DHS does not know how many of these aliens would be eligible under the economic necessity requirement or how many would be determined not to warrant employment authorization as a matter of discretion. DHS recognizes that the estimated lower bound range of 626 to 647 could be even lower if any of the renewal approvals do not apply for or are denied employment authorization.[200]
(印刷页第34415页)
( printed page 34415)
b. 获得暂缓执行(Deferred Action)的外国人的酌情就业授权((c)(14))。
b. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Granted Deferred Action ((c)(14)).
国土安全部根据2015财年至2024财年的历史数据估计受影响人数。表V.11显示了使用I-765表格数据统计的,2015财年至2024财年期间获得暂缓执行的外国人就业授权初次申请和续签申请的年度受理量和批准量。
DHS estimates the affected population based on historical data for FY 2015 through FY 2024. Table V.11 shows the annual receipts and approvals using Form I-765 data for initial and renewal applications of employment authorization for aliens granted deferred action for FY 2015 through FY 2024.
2015财年至2024财年期间,就业授权初次受理数量从2015财年的35,538件减少至2020财年的14,302件(期间最低点),随后增加至2024财年的148,398件(期间最高点)。就业授权初次批准数量从2015财年的22,177件减少至2016财年的6,454件(期间最低点),随后增加至2024财年的155,468件(期间最高点)。就业授权续签受理数量从2015财年的最高点11,303件减少至2019财年的3,357件(期间最低点),随后增加至2024财年的4,756件。就业授权续签批准数量从2015财年的最高点9,073件减少至2020财年的最低点2,731件,随后增加至2024财年的4,100件。尽管国土安全部估计本拟议规则将减少符合就业授权资格的外国人数量,并预计(c)(14)类初次申请和续签申请的受理量和批准量将下降,但由于本分析中进一步讨论的原因,国土安全部无法确定下降幅度。
The number of initial receipts of employment authorization over the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 decreased from 35,538 in FY 2015 through 14,302 in FY 2020 (a period low), then increased to 148,398 in FY 2024 (a period high). The number of initial approvals of employment authorization decreased from 22,177 in FY 2015 to 6,454 in FY 2016 (a period low) before increasing to 155,468 in FY 2024 (a period high). The number of renewal receipts of employment authorization decreased from a high of 11,303 in FY 2015 to 3,357 in FY 2019 (a period low) before increasing to 4,756 in FY 2024. The number of renewal approvals of employment authorization decreased from a high of 9,073 in FY 2015 to a low of 2,731 in FY 2020 before increasing to 4,100 in FY 2024. Although DHS estimates this proposed rule would reduce the number of aliens eligible for employment authorization and anticipates a decline in (c)(14) receipts and approvals for initial and renewal applications, DHS is unable to determine the magnitude of decline for reasons discussed further in this analysis.
表V.12根据表V.11所列的受理数据,显示了初次申请和续签申请受理量的年度增长率。就初次受理而言,2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年均增长率下降了11.9%,2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年均增长率上升了59.7%,2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年均增长率上升了15.4%。[201]就续签受理而言,2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年均增长率下降了21.6%;2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年均增长率上升了5.0%,2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年均增长率下降了8.3%。[202]国土安全部利用(印刷页第34416页)这些增长率,在有本规则和无本规则两种情景下,估算初次申请和续签申请受理量的预计数量。
Table V.12 shows annual growth rates of initial and renewal receipts based on the receipts presented in Table V.11. For initial receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 to FY 2019 decreased 11.9 percent, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period 2020 through 2024 increased 59.7 percent, and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 increased 15.4 percent.[201] For renewal receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 decreased 21.6 percent; the 5-year annual growth rate for the period 2020 through 2024 increased 5.0 percent, and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 decreased 8.3 percent.[202] DHS uses ( printed page 34416) these growth rates to estimate the projected number of initial and renewal receipts under the two scenarios of with and without this rule.
为在没有本规则的情况下估算初次申请收件量的预测数,国土安全部(DHS)选择采用2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请收件量的-11.9%的下降增长率。我们认为,基于本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项,采用递增的年度百分比增长率来预测初次申请收件量是不合理的。[203]
To estimate the projected number of initial receipts without this rule, DHS chooses to use the declining growth rate of -11.9 percent for initial receipts for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019. We do not find it reasonable to use an increasing annual percentage growth rate to project initial receipts based on the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities.[203]
为在没有本规则的情况下估算续签申请收件量的预测数,国土安全部承认,对于已获得暂缓遣返和工作许可的外国人而言,暂缓遣返的批准可由国土安全部自行决定随时终止。[204] 国土安全部还认识到,若本规则最终确定,(c)(14)类工作许可证(EAD)的有效期将不超过一年。此外,初次申请收件量的下降增长率在某个时点上将导致续签申请收件量趋于平稳或下降。因此,我们认为采用递增的年度增长率来估算续签申请收件量的预测数是不合理的。在本分析中,我们使用2015财年至2024财年期间-8.3%的十年平均年增长率,而非2015财年至2019财年期间-21.6%的年增长率(表V.12),后者是两者中更为保守的续签申请收件量增长率。通过选择-8.3%的年增长率,在没有本规则的情况下,续签申请收件量的预测值将更高,这将导致潜在成本估算的范围更大。
To estimate the projected number of renewal receipts without this rule, DHS acknowledges that for aliens who have been granted deferred action and employment authorization, the grant of deferred action can be terminated at any time at DHS discretion.[204] DHS also recognizes that the validity period for a (c)(14) EAD would not exceed one year, should this rule become finalized. Additionally, the declining growth rates for initial receipts would, at some point, result in either a plateau or a decrease in the number of renewal receipts. Therefore, we do not find it reasonable to use an increasing annual growth rate to estimate the projected number of renewal receipts. In this analysis, we use the 10-year average annual growth rate of −8.3 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 instead of the annual growth rate of −21.6 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.12), which is the more conservative rate of renewal receipts between the two. By choosing an annual growth rate of −8.3 percent, the projection without this rule would be higher for renewal receipts, which would lead to a greater range of potential cost estimates.
接下来,在表V.13中,我们根据表V.11所示的年度收件量和批准量,计算初次申请和续签申请的平均收件量、批准量及批准率。[205] 2015财年至2019财年初次申请的批准率为38.8%,2020财年至2024财年为95.9%,2015财年至2024财年为77.9%。2015财年至2019财年续签申请的批准率为72.9%,2020财年至2024财年为81.5%,2015财年至2024财年为76.0%。
Next, in Table V.13, we calculate the average receipts, approvals, and approval rates for initial and renewal receipts from the annual receipts and approvals shown in Table V.11.[205] The approval rate for initials for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 38.8 percent, for FY 2020 through FY 2024 is 95.9 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 77.9 percent. The approval rate for renewals for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 72.9 percent, for FY 2020 through FY 2024 is 81.5 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 76.0 percent.
(印刷页第34417页)
( printed page 34417)
为预测表V.14中若无本规则情况下的初次批准数量，国土安全部(DHS)选择了较为保守的初次批准率，即2020财年至2024财年5年平均年率95.9%。此外，为预测续签批准数量，国土安全部选择了较为保守的续签批准率，即2020财年至2024财年5年平均年率81.5%。然而，我们注意到，表V.13中所分析的三个时期的平均续签批准率处于8.6个百分点（81.5%减72.9%）的相对较小范围内。通过选择这些年度批准率，“若无本规则”情景下的初次批准和续签批准预测数值将更高，这将导致潜在成本估算的范围更大。
To project the number of initial approvals without this rule in Table V.14, DHS chooses the more conservative initial approval rate by choosing the 5-year average annual rate of 95.9 percent from FY 2020 through FY 2024. Moreover, to project the number of renewal approvals, DHS chooses the more conservative renewal approval rate by choosing the 5-year annual rate of 81.5 percent from FY 2020 through FY 2024. However, we note that the average renewal approval rate over the three periods analyzed in Table V.13 are within a relatively small range of 8.6 percent (81.5 percent minus 72.9 percent). By choosing these annual approval rates, the projections under the “without the rule” scenario will be higher for initial and renewal approvals, which will lead to a greater range of potential cost estimates.
为预测2025财年的初次受理量和续签受理量,我们依照本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项对相关人数进行了估算。2015财年至2019财年期间的初次受理量和续签受理量平均值更加符合这些政策指引,可作为2025财年的合理估算依据。预计2025财年的初次批准量是通过将95.9%的平均初次批准率(见表V.13)乘以2025财年预计的初次受理量31,165份而得出的,计算结果为29,887份(见表V.14)。
To project FY 2025 initial and renewal receipts, we estimate the population in accordance with the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities. The average initial and renewal receipts from FY 2015 through FY 2019 fall more in line with these directives and are reasonable estimates for FY 2025. The projected FY 2025 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the average initial approval rate of 95.9 percent (Table V.13) by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2025, 31,165, which equals 29,887 (Table V.14).
为预测2026财年的初次申请接收量,将2015财年至2019财年期间负11.9%的五年平均年增长率(表V.12)乘以2025财年预计初次申请接收量(31,165件),得出负3,709件(四舍五入)。将2025财年预计初次申请接收量减去3,709件,等于27,456件(表V.14)。[206] 2026财年初次申请批准量的计算方法是,将同样的95.9%平均初次批准率乘以2026财年预计初次申请接收量(27,456件),得出26,330件(四舍五入)。随后对后续年份重复该计算过程。
To project FY 2026 initial receipts, the 5-year average annual growth rate of −11.9 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.12) is multiplied by the number of projected initial receipts from FY 2025 (31,165), which equals −3,709 (rounded). Subtracting 3,709 from the projected initial receipts for FY 2025 equals 27,456 (Table V.14).[206] The FY 2026 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the same average initial approval rate of 95.9 percent by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2026 (27,456), which equals 26,330 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years.
为预测2025财年延期申请接收量,我们使用2015财年至2024财年期间负8.3%的十年平均年增长率(表V.12)。[207] 2025财年延期申请批准量的计算方法是,将81.5%的平均延期批准率(表V.13)乘以2025财年预计延期申请接收量(7,159件),得出5,835件(表V.14)。
To project FY 2025 renewal receipts, we use the 10-year average annual growth rate of −8.3 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 (Table V.12).[207] The FY 2025 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the average renewal approval rate of 81.5 percent (Table V.13) by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2025 (7,159), which equals 5,835 (Table V.14).
为预测2026财年续签申请收件量,将2015财年至2024财年期间较为保守的10年年均增长率负8.3%(表V.12)乘以2025财年预计续签申请收件量(7,159件),得出负594(四舍五入)。将2025财年预计续签申请收件量减去594,得出6,565件(表V.14)。[208] 2026财年预计续签批准数量的计算方法是,将平均续签批准率81.5%乘以2026财年预计续签申请收件量估计值(6,565件),得出5,350件(四舍五入)。随后对后续年度重复该计算过程。这些预测数据列于表V.14。
To project FY 2026 renewal receipts, the more conservative 10-year annual growth rate of −8.3 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2024 (Table V.12) is multiplied by the number of projected renewal receipts from FY 2025 (7,159), which equals −594 (rounded). Subtracting 594 from the projected renewal receipts for FY 2025 equals 6,565 (Table V.14).[208] The projected number of FY 2026 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the average renewal approval rate of 81.5 percent by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2026 (6,565), which equals 5,350 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years. These projections are shown in Table V.14.
为预测本规则下未来(c)(14)类就业许可批准的年度数量,将表V.14中(无本规则情况下)初次申请和续签申请收件量的估计值,乘以表V.13中两个最不保守的批准率。为估算本规则下初次申请批准数量的下限,采用38.8%的批准率,上限采用77.9%的批准率。为估算本规则下续签申请批准数量的下限,采用72.9%的批准率,上限采用76.0%的批准率。这些批准率区间是合理的,因为它们均低于无本规则情况下相应估算所采用的较为保守的比率,即初次申请95.9%和续签申请81.5%,且更符合本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项。( 印刷页 34418) 本规则下预计批准数量列于表V.15。
To project the annual number of future (c)(14) employment authorization approvals under this rule, the estimates (without rule) for initial and renewal receipts from Table V.14 are multiplied by the two least conservative approval rates from Table V.13. To estimate the lower bound projected approvals for initials under this rule the approval rate of 38.8 percent is used and 77.9 percent is used for the upper bound. To estimate the lower bound projected approvals for renewals under this rule the approval rate of 72.9 percent is used and 76.0 percent is used for the upper bound. These ranges of approval rates are reasonable as they are both below the more conservative rates of 95.9 percent (initials) and 81.5 percent (renewals) used in the respective estimates without the rule and they fall more in line with the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities. The ( printed page 34418) projected approvals under this rule are presented in Table V.15.
我们估算续签批准数量下限区间为2,393件至5,219件(表V.15(B)第C列)。国土安全部(DHS)认识到,预计的续签批准数量可能未能完全反映因拟议的E-Verify要求而不再有资格获得就业许可的外国人数量,这种情况可能发生在外国人雇主未参加且选择不参加E-Verify系统,且外国人无法从参加E-Verify且信誉良好的美国雇主处另觅工作的情况下。部分申请续签的外国人目前可能也未就业,因此不符合续签的新要求。此外,国土安全部不清楚其中有多少外国人将符合经济必要性要求,也不清楚有多少外国人将被酌情认定不应获得就业许可。国土安全部认识到,如果部分续签批准申请人未申请就业许可或其申请被拒绝,则估计的下限区间2,393件至5,219件可能会更低。209
We estimate the lower bound range for renewal approvals to be 2,393 to 5,219 (Table V.15(B) Column C). DHS recognizes that the projected renewal approvals may not fully account for the number of aliens who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization due to the proposed E-Verify requirement if the aliens' employers are not enrolled and opt not to enroll in E-Verify, and if the aliens are unable to find alternative employment with a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify. Some aliens applying for renewal may also not be currently employed and therefore would not meet the new requirements for renewal. Additionally, DHS does not know how many of these aliens would be eligible under the economic necessity requirement or how many would be determined not to warrant employment authorization as a matter of discretion. DHS recognizes that the estimated lower bound range of 2,393 to 5,219 could be even lower if any of the renewal approvals do not apply for or are denied employment authorization.209
c. 对被假释入境美国的外国人给予的酌情就业许可(第(c)(11)类)
c. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Paroled Into the United States ((c)(11))
国土安全部基于2015财年至2024财年的历史数据估算受影响人口。表V.16显示了2015财年至2024财年期间使用I-765表格提交的获假释外国人就业许可初次申请和续签申请的年度收件量及批准数量。( 印刷页 34419)
DHS estimates the affected population based on historical data for FY 2015 to FY 2024. Table V.16 shows the annual receipts and approvals for initial and renewal applications of employment authorization for aliens granted parole ( printed page 34419) using Form I-765 for FY 2015 through FY 2024.
在2015财年至2024财年期间,就业许可初次申请收件量从2015财年的52,709件降至2020财年的7,099件(期间最低值),随后增至2024财年的748,544件(期间最高值)。就业许可初次申请批准数量从2015财年的48,060件增至2016财年的74,526件,随后降至2019财年的最低值4,341件。此后,初次申请批准数量逐年增加,2024财年达到最高值753,357件。就业许可续签申请收件量从2015财年的5,133件降至2022财年的最低值1,862件,随后增至2024财年的最高值43,586件。就业许可续签申请批准数量总体上从2015财年的3,741件降至2021财年的最低值856件,随后增至2024财年的最高值36,718件。尽管国土安全部估计本拟议规则将减少有资格获得就业许可的外国人数量,并预计(c)(11)类初次申请和续签申请的收件量及批准数量均将下降,但由于本分析后续讨论的原因,国土安全部无法确定下降的幅度。
The number of initial receipts of employment authorization over the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 decreased from 52,709 in FY 2015 to 7,099 in FY 2020 (a period low), then increased to 748,544 in FY 2024 (a period high). The number of initial approvals for employment authorizations increased from 48,060 in FY 2015 to 74,526 in FY 2016, before decreasing to a low of 4,341 in FY 2019. Subsequently, the number of initial approvals increased annually with a high of 753,357 in FY 2024. The number of renewal receipts for employment authorizations decreased from 5,133 in FY 2015 to a low of 1,862 in FY 2022, before increasing to a high of 43,586 in FY 2024. The number of renewal approvals for employment authorization generally decreased from 3,741 in FY 2015 to a low of 856 in FY 2021 before increasing to a high of 36,718 in FY 2024. Although DHS estimates this proposed rule would reduce the number of aliens eligible for employment authorization and anticipates a decline in (c)(11) receipts and approvals for both initial and renewals, DHS is unable to determine the magnitude of decline for reasons discussed further in this analysis.
表V.17显示了基于表V.16所列申请数量计算得出的初次申请和延期申请收件量的年增长率。对于初次申请收件量,2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年增长率下降了22.7%;2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年增长率上升了153.9%;2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年增长率上升了30.4%。[210]对于延期申请收件量,2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年增长率下降了8%;2020财年至2024财年期间的5年年增长率上升了75.5%;2015财年至2024财年期间的10年年增长率上升了23.9%。[211]国土安全部(DHS)使用这些增长率来估算在本规则实施与不实施两种情形下初次申请和延期申请预计收件量。
Table V.17 shows annual growth rates of initial and renewal receipts based on the receipts presented in Table V.16. For initial receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 decreased 22.7 percent; the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2020 through FY 2024 increased 153.9 percent; and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 increased 30.4 percent.[210] For renewal receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 decreased 8 percent; the 5-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2020 through FY 2024 increased 75.5 percent; and the 10-year annual growth rate for the period FY 2015 through FY 2024 increased 23.9 percent.[211] DHS uses these growth rates to estimate the projected number of initial and renewal receipts under the two scenarios with and without this rule.
(印刷页第34420页)
( printed page 34420)
为估算在不实施本规则情形下的初次申请预计收件量,国土安全部选择使用2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请收件量的下降增长率负22.7%。鉴于本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项,我们认为使用不断上升的年增长率来预测初次申请收件量并不合理。[212]
To estimate the projected number of initial receipts without this rule, DHS chooses to use the declining growth rate of −22.7 percent for initial receipts for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019. We do not find it reasonable to use an increasing annual percentage growth rate to project initial receipts based on the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities.[212]
为估算在不实施本规则情形下的延期申请预计收件量,国土安全部承认,获准假释并获得工作授权的外国人的假释身份可由国土安全部酌情终止。国土安全部还认识到,工作授权期限是可变的。通常,根据(c)(11)条款授予的工作授权期限为假释期限或一年,以两者中较短者为准。[213]此外,初次申请收件量的下降增长率在某一时点将导致延期申请收件量趋于平稳或下降。因此,我们认为使用不断上升的年增长率来估算延期申请预计收件量并不合理。在本分析中,我们使用2015财年至2019财年期间的5年平均增长率负8%(表V.17)。
To estimate the projected number of renewal receipts without this rule, DHS acknowledges that aliens granted parole and have been authorized to work can have their parole terminated at DHS discretion. DHS also recognizes that the period of employment authorization is variable. Typically, employment authorization under (c)(11) is granted for the duration of parole or one year, whichever is shorter.[213] Additionally, the declining growth rate for initial receipts would, at some point, result in either a plateau or a decrease for renewal receipts. Therefore, we do not find it reasonable to use an increasing annual growth rate to estimate the projected number of renewal receipts. In this analysis, we use the 5-year average growth rate of −8 percent for the period FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.17).
接下来,在表V.18中,我们根据表V.16所示的年度收件量和批准量,计算出初次申请和延期申请的平均收件量、批准量及批准率。[214]2015财年至2019财年初次申请的批准率为95.7%,2020财年至2024财年为88.9%,2015财年至2024财年为89.8%。2015财年至2019财年延期申请的批准率为66.1%,2020财年至2024财年为78.6%,2015财年至2024财年为75.7%。
Next, in Table V.18, we calculate the average receipts and approvals and approval rates for initial and renewal receipts from the annual receipts and approvals shown in Table V.16.[214] The approval rate for initials for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 95.7 percent, for FY 2020 to FY 2024 is 88.9 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 89.8 percent. The approval rate for renewals for FY 2015 through FY 2019 is 66.1 percent, for FY 2020 through FY 2024 is 78.6 percent, and for FY 2015 through FY 2024 is 75.7 percent.
为预测在不实施本规则情形下表V.19中的初次申请批准量,国土安全部选择了较为保守的初次申请批准率,即2015财年至2019财年的5年年率95.7%。此外,为预测2025财年延期申请批准量,国土安全部选择了较为保守的延期申请批准率,即(印刷页第34421页)2020财年至2024财年的5年年率78.6%。然而,我们注意到,表V.18中所分析的三个时期的平均延期申请批准率处于相对较小的范围内,相差12.5个百分点(78.6%减去66.1%)。通过选择这些年批准率,初次申请和延期申请的预测批准量将会更高,从而导致潜在成本估算的范围更大。
To project the number of initial approvals without this rule in Table V.19, DHS chooses the more conservative initial approval rate by choosing the 5-year annual rate of 95.7 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019. Moreover, to project FY 2025 renewal approvals, DHS chooses the more conservative renewal approval rate by ( printed page 34421) choosing the 5-year annual rate of 78.6 percent from FY 2020 through FY 2024. However, we note that the average renewal approval rate over the three periods analyzed in Table V.18 are within a relatively small range of 12.5 percentage (78.6 percent minus 66.1 percent). By choosing these annual approval rates, the projections will be higher for initial and renewal approvals, which will lead to a greater range of potential cost estimates.
为预测2025财年初次申请和延期申请收件量,我们根据本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项对相关人群数量进行估算。2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请和延期申请的平均收件量与这些政策指令更为一致,是2025财年的合理估算值。为预测2025财年初次申请收件量,我们使用了2015财年至2019财年期间初次申请收件量的5年平均年数41,120件。2025财年初次申请预计批准量的计算方法是,将2015财年至2019财年初次申请平均批准率95.7%(表V.18)乘以2025财年初次申请预计收件量(41,120件),得出39,352件(四舍五入)。
To project FY 2025 initial and renewal receipts, we estimate the population in accordance with the administration's current immigration enforcement priorities. The average initial and renewal receipts from FY 2015 through FY 2019 fall more in line with these directives and are reasonable estimates for FY 2025. To project FY 2025 initial receipts, the 5-year average annual number of initial receipts of 41,120 from FY 2015 through FY 2019 is used. The projected FY 2025 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the average initial approval rate of 95.7 percent (Table V.18) for FY 2015 through FY 2019 by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2025 (41,120), which equals 39,352 (rounded).
为预测2026财年初次申请受理量,将2015财年至2019财年期间的5年平均年增长率负22.7%(表V.17)乘以2025财年预测初次申请受理量(41,120),得出负9,334(四舍五入)。用2025财年预测初次申请受理量减去9,334,等于31,786(表V.19)。[215]2026财年初次批准量的计算方法是,将同样的平均初次批准率95.7%乘以2026财年预测初次申请受理量估计值(31,786),等于30,419(四舍五入)。随后对后续年份重复此过程。
To project FY 2026 initial receipts, the 5-year average annual growth rate of −22.7 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.17) is multiplied by the number of projected initial receipts from FY 2025 (41,120), which equals −9,334 (rounded). Subtracting 9,334 from the projected initial receipts for FY 2025 equals 31,786 (Table V.19).[215] The FY 2026 initial approvals are calculated by multiplying the same average initial approval rate of 95.7 percent by the estimated number of initial receipts from FY 2026 (31,786), which equals 30,419 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years.
为预测2025财年续签申请受理量,采用2015财年至2019财年期间的5年平均年度续签申请受理量4,407。[216]2025财年预测续签批准量的计算方法是,将2020财年至2024财年的平均续签批准率78.6%(表V.18)乘以2025财年续签申请受理量估计值(4,407),等于3,464(表V.19)。
To project FY 2025 renewal receipts, the 5-year average annual number of renewal receipts of 4,407 from FY 2015 through FY 2019 is used.[216] The projected FY 2025 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the average renewal approval rate of 78.6 percent (Table V.18) for FY 2020 through FY 2024 by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2025 (4,407), which equals 3,464 (Table V.19).
为预测2026财年续签申请受理量,将2015财年至2019财年期间的5年年增长率负8.0%(表V.17)乘以2025财年预测续签申请受理量(4,407),得出负353(四舍五入)。用2025财年预测续签申请受理量减去353,等于4,054(表V.19)。[217]2026财年续签批准量的计算方法是,将同样的平均续签批准率78.6%乘以2026财年续签申请受理量估计值(4,054),等于3,186(四舍五入)。随后对后续年份重复此过程。这些预测数据列于表V.19。
To project FY 2026 renewal receipts, the 5-year annual growth rate of −8.0 percent from FY 2015 through FY 2019 (Table V.17) is multiplied by the number of projected renewal receipts from FY 2025 (4,407), which equals −353 (rounded). Subtracting 353 from the projected renewal receipts for FY 2025 equals 4,054 (Table V.19).[217] The FY 2026 renewal approvals are calculated by multiplying the same average renewal approval rate of 78.6 percent by the estimated number of renewal receipts from FY 2026 (4,054), which equals 3,186 (rounded). The process was then repeated for subsequent years. These projections are shown in Table V.19.
为预测本规则下未来(c)(11)类工作许可批准的年度数量,将表V.19中“无本规则情况下”初次及续签申请受理量的预测估计值分别乘以表V.18中的批准率。为估算本规则下初次批准量的下限预测值,采用批准率88.9%,上限预测值采用89.8%。[218]为估算本规则下续签批准量的下限预测值,采用批准率66.1%,上限预测值采用75.7%。这些批准率区间是合理的,因为它们均低于在无本规则情况下各自预测估计值(表V.19)中所采用的更为保守的比率,即初次申请95.7%和续签申请78.6%,且更符合本届政府当前的移民执法优先事项。本规则下的预测批准量列于表V.20。(印刷页34422)
To project the annual number of future (c)(11) employment authorization approvals under this rule, the projected “without the rule” estimates for initial and renewal receipts from Table V.19 are multiplied by approval rates from Table V.18. To estimate the lower bound projected initial approvals under this rule the approval rate of 88.9 percent is used and 89.8 percent is used for the upper bound.[218] To estimate the lower bound projected renewal approvals under this rule the approval rate of 66.1 percent is used and 75.7 percent is used for the upper bound. These ranges of approval rates are reasonable as they are both below the more conservative rates of 95.7 percent (initials) and 78.6 percent (renewals) used in the respective projected estimates without this rule (Table V.19) and they fall more in line with the Administration's current immigration enforcement priorities. The projected approvals ( printed page 34422) under this rule are presented in Table V.20.
我们估计续签批准量的下限区间为1,375至2,913(表V.20(B)栏C)。国土安全部(DHS)认识到,该续签批准量预测值可能未能完全反映因拟议的E-Verify要求而不再具备工作许可资格的外国人数量,这些情形包括:外国人的雇主未参加E-Verify且选择不参加,以及外国人无法找到参加E-Verify且信誉良好的美国雇主提供的替代就业机会。部分申请续签的外国人目前也可能处于未就业状态,因而无法满足续签的新要求。此外,国土安全部不清楚其中有多少外国人将符合经济必要性要求,也不清楚有多少人将被自由裁量认定为不应获得工作许可。国土安全部认识到,若部分续签批准申请人未提出申请或工作许可申请被拒绝,则所估计的1,375至2,913的下限区间可能会更低。[219]
We estimate the lower bound range for renewal approvals to be 1,375 to 2,913 (Table V.20(B) Column C). DHS recognizes that the projected renewal approvals may not fully account for the number of aliens who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization due to the proposed E-Verify requirement if the aliens' employers are not enrolled and opt not to enroll in E-Verify, and if the aliens are unable to find alternative employment with a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify. Some aliens applying for renewal may also not be currently employed and therefore would not meet the new requirements for renewal. Additionally, DHS does not know how many of these aliens would be eligible under the economic necessity requirement or how many would be determined not to warrant employment authorization as a matter of discretion. DHS recognizes that the estimated lower bound range of 1,375 to 2,913 could be even lower if any of the renewal approvals do not apply for or are denied employment authorization.[219]

d. 申请续签工作许可的外国人,E-Verify
d. Aliens Applying for Renewal of Employment Authorization—E-Verify
DHS提议,对于在本最终规则生效日期之后获得就业授权的(c)(11)、(c)(14)和(c)(18)类外国人,仅在该外国人符合本规则提议的初次就业授权资格标准,并证明其受雇于E-Verify(即美国国土安全部的雇佣资格核实计划)的良好信誉参与雇主的情况下,方可续签其就业授权,具体方式为提供该美国雇主在E-Verify中的公司识别号以及该美国雇主在E-Verify中登记的名称。由于本规则提议限制并厘清对因监管令而暂时获释的外国人,以及获得延期执行和假释的外国人的就业授权资格,此项规定对续签群体的影响将取决于哪些外国人仍符合资格,以及该外国人的雇主是否已经参与E-Verify,或者在雇主尚未注册的情况下是否愿意注册并参与E-Verify。[220]由于就业授权资格存在不确定性,美国国土安全部(DHS)无法估算将受此规定影响的续签群体的范围,若试图进行此类估算将属于推测性质。然而,DHS承认将有申请续签的外国人受此规定影响。
DHS proposes to allow (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) aliens who are granted employment authorization after the effective date of the final rule to have their employment authorization renewed only if the alien meets the initial employment authorization eligibility criteria proposed in this rule and establishes that he or she is employed by a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify, the DHS employment eligibility verification program, by providing the U.S. employer's E-Verify company identification number and the U.S. employer's name as listed in E-Verify. Because this rule proposes to limit and clarify eligibility for employment authorization for aliens temporarily released on an order of supervision and aliens granted deferred action and parole, the impact on renewal populations would depend on which aliens remain eligible and if an alien's employer already participates in E-Verify, or would be willing to enroll and participate in E-Verify if the employer is not enrolled.[220] Because of the uncertainty regarding eligibility, DHS is unable to estimate a range for renewal populations that would be impacted by this provision, and attempting to do so would be speculative. However, DHS acknowledges there would be aliens applying for renewal who would be impacted by this provision.
e. 就业授权的终止,8 CFR 274a.14
e. Termination of Employment Authorization—8 CFR 274a.14
如前言所述,DHS提议扩大8 CFR 274a.14项下自动终止的事由。本分析中先前描述的各类群体将受此规定影响,因为相较于没有本规则的情形,其就业授权将提前终止。[221]由于就业授权终止及触发终止的因素存在不确定性,DHS无法估算受影响人群的具体比例,若试图进行此类估算将属于推测性质。然而,DHS承认,对于就业授权被终止的外国人而言,其所承担的成本与归属于不再符合就业授权资格的外国人的成本相似。
As noted in the preamble, DHS is proposing to expand the reasons for automatic termination under 8 CFR 274a.14. The populations previously described in this analysis would be impacted by this provision because their employment authorization will be terminated earlier than it would have been without this rule.[221] Because of the uncertainty regarding the termination of employment authorization and the factors triggering termination, DHS is unable to estimate the precise portion of population impacts and attempting to do so would be speculative. However, DHS acknowledges costs for aliens whose employment authorization is terminated similar to those ascribed to aliens no longer eligible for employment authorization.
f. 雇主群体
f. Employer Population
DHS认识到,本拟议规则将影响目前或未来雇用(c)(11)、(c)(14)和(c)(18)类外国工人的雇主。然而,由于裁量性EAD(就业授权文件)的就业授权被视为“开放市场”性质,外国工人并不与特定雇主绑定,因此DHS无法精确估算可能产生成本的雇主数量。此类就业也不要求在就业授权申请过程的任何阶段(初次申请、续签或更换阶段)向美国劳工部(DOL)提交劳动条件申请或临时劳工证明,或提供其他雇主数据。DHS认识到,诸多因素会影响雇主是否参与E-Verify计划。尽管E-Verify是一项免费的自愿性计划,但部分雇主根据联邦合同条件,或根据州立法或其他适用法律规定的营业执照条件,被要求注册参与该计划。然而,DHS无法预测将有多少雇主使用E-Verify,也无法预测因本拟议规则而发生多少劳动力流动。此外,DHS不了解有多少雇主会选择注册E-Verify以保留其获得裁量性就业授权的外国雇员,也不了解如果这些雇主注册,将为多少名雇员建立E-Verify案件。DHS同样无法确定,由于本拟议规则,有多少雇主的获得裁量性就业授权的外国雇员将继续保持就业资格。DHS欢迎公众就雇主为保留获得裁量性就业授权的外国雇员而注册E-Verify计划的情况提供意见或数据,以及雇主在核实雇员就业授权时将为多少名雇员建立E-Verify案件的总体数量提供意见或数据。DHS指出,若某公司选择不注册E-Verify,此项规定可能成为该公司雇用或继续雇用获得裁量性就业授权的外国人的障碍。即使在获得裁量性就业授权的雇员仍保持就业资格的情况下,此类障碍也会因增加美国企业可能承担的人员流动成本而加重本规则的成本核算。
DHS recognizes that this proposed rule would impact employers who currently, or will in the future, employ (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) alien workers. However, DHS cannot precisely estimate the number of employers that could incur costs because employment authorization for discretionary EADs is considered to be “open market,” where alien workers are not tied to a specific employer. Such employment also does not require a Labor Condition Application or a Temporary Labor Certification from the U.S. Department of Labor (DOL), or other employer data at any point in the employment authorization application process (initial, renewal, or replacement stage). DHS recognizes that many factors influence whether an employer participates in the E-Verify program. While E-Verify is a free, voluntary program, some employers are required to enroll in the program as a condition of Federal contracting or as a condition of business licensing under State legislation or other applicable laws. However, DHS cannot predict the number of employers who would use E-Verify or how many would experience labor turnover due to this proposed rule. Further, DHS does not know the number of employers that would choose to enroll in E-Verify to retain their discretionary employment authorized alien employees or the overall number of employees for whom these entities would create an E-Verify case, should the employers enroll. DHS is also unable to determine the number of employers whose discretionary employment authorized alien employees would remain employment eligible as a result of this proposed rule. DHS welcomes public comment or data on employers who enroll in the E-Verify program to retain discretionary employment authorized alien employees as well as the overall number of employees for whom employers would create E-Verify cases, should they verify the employment authorization of employees. DHS notes that this provision may act as a barrier to a company hiring or continuing to employ a discretionary employment authorized alien should the company make the choice to not enroll in E-Verify. Such barriers contribute to the cost calculation of this rule by increasing the potential for turnover costs incurred by U.S. businesses, even in situations where a discretionary employment authorized employee remains employment authorized.
4. 货币化影响分析
4. Monetized Impact Analysis
本节介绍与拟议规则相关的货币化成本估算。拟议条款的影响是与“无该规则”情景相比估算得出的,该基准情景假设不实施任何拟议行动。美国国土安全部(DHS)预计,修订资格条件并针对酌处性就业授权引入新的证据要求可能产生若干影响,包括外籍工人潜在的收入损失、填写I-765表所需时间增加30分钟而产生的成本、填写I-765WS表的要求及相关的30分钟时间负担、前往申请支持中心(ASC)提交生物特征信息,以及填写I-131表所需时间增加15分钟(仅适用于(c)(11)类别)。
This section presents the estimated monetized costs associated with the proposed rule. The impacts of the proposed provisions are estimated in comparison with the “without the rule” scenario, a baseline that assumes no proposed action would be implemented. DHS anticipates that revising eligibility and introducing new evidentiary requirements for discretionary employment authorization could have several impacts, including potential lost earnings to alien workers, the costs associated with a 30-minute time burden increase to complete Form I-765, the requirement to complete Form I-765WS and the associated 30-minute time burden, traveling to an ASC and submitting biometrics, and the 15-minute increase to complete Form I-131 ((c)(11) category only).
拟议规则预计将导致符合就业授权资格的外籍人员数量在(c)(11)类、(c)(14)类和(c)(18)类项下有所减少。减少符合就业授权资格的(c)(11)类、(c)(14)类和(c)(18)类外籍人员数量所产生的影响,既包括潜在的分配性影响(转移),也包括成本。美国国土安全部以这些不再具备就业授权资格的外籍人员群体的薪酬损失及相关税收影响,作为衡量此项变化影响的指标,既可将其视为这些外籍人员向他人转移的分配性影响(转移),也可将其作为企业生产力损失成本的替代衡量指标。
The proposed rule is estimated to result in a reduction in the number of aliens who are eligible for employment authorization, under the (c)(11), the (c)(14), and the (c)(18) categories. The impacts of reducing the number of (c)(11), (c)(14), and (c)(18) aliens who are eligible for employment authorization include both potential distributional impacts (transfers) and costs. DHS uses lost compensation to these alien populations who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization and associated tax implications as a measure of the impact of this change—either as distributional impacts (transfers) from these aliens to others or as a proxy for businesses' cost for lost productivity.
a. 针对处于延期强制遣返行动(OSUP)状态外籍人员的酌处性就业授权((c)(18)类)
a. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens on OSUP ((c)(18))
一、收入
i. Earnings
美国国土安全部没有关于在(c)(18)类别下获得就业授权的外籍工人在初次申请或续签阶段的工资或职业信息,因为这些外籍工人获得的是开放市场就业授权文件(EAD),该文件不包含也不要求提供有关其就业情况的任何数据。由于许多申请(c)(18)类别的外籍人员可能是相对较新进入劳动力市场的人员,我们预计其中许多人不会获得“高层级”工资。联邦最低工资目前为(印刷版第34424页)每小时7.25美元,[222]但许多州已实施更高的最低工资标准。[223]然而,联邦政府并未追踪全国范围内按人口加权的最低工资估算值。目标人群中的外籍人员可能分布在美国各地,根据其所居住的州或城市不同,可能适用不同的最低工资标准。
DHS has no information on wages or occupations of alien workers with employment authorization under the (c)(18) category, at the initial or renewal stage, since these alien workers obtain an open-market EAD that does not include or require any data on their employment. Because many of the aliens applying for (c)(18) category would be relatively new entrants to the labor force, we would not expect many of them to earn “high-tier” wages. The Federal minimum wage is currently ( printed page 34424) $7.25 per hour,[222] but many States have implemented higher minimum wage rates.[223] However, the Federal Government does not track a nationwide population-weighted minimum wage estimate. Aliens in the population of interest could be located anywhere within the United States and may be subject to a range of minimum wage rates depending on the State or city in which the alien lives.
与其他规则保持一致,美国国土安全部采用劳工统计局(BLS)全国职业就业与工资估算中所有职业的第10百分位小时工资,作为可能获得入门级工资人员有效最低工资的合理替代指标。劳工统计局的估算数据反映了美国劳动力市场工资的变化情况,该数据每年更新,因此能够体现各州最低工资标准的任何变化。目前,所有职业的第10百分位小时工资估算值为13.97美元,未计入员工福利。[224]
Consistent with other rules, DHS uses the 10th percentile hourly wage from the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) National Occupational Employment and Wage Estimates for all occupations as a reasonable proxy for the effective minimum wage for individuals who are likely to earn an entry-level wage. BLS estimates account for changes in wages across the United States labor market, which is updated annually and will thus reflect any changes to State minimum wage rates. The 10th percentile hourly wage estimate for all occupations is currently $13.97, not accounting for worker benefits.[224]
受影响的部分外籍人员的工资很可能高于最低工资标准。由于受影响的就业授权文件(EAD)在初次申请或续签阶段均不包含也不要求提供任何有关工资的数据,美国国土安全部无法从相关表格中获取有关可能雇用此类工人的收入、职业、行业、职位或企业的信息。然而,美国国土安全部不排除部分人群可能获得所有职业平均工资的可能性。因此,本分析同时采用13.97美元的有效最低小时工资率作为下限估算值,并考虑到各州平均工资的差异,采用所有职业31.48美元的平均工资率作为上限估算值。[225]
It is likely that some aliens impacted would earn wages above the minimum. Because the EADs impacted do not include or require, at the initial or renewal stage, any data regarding wages, DHS has no information from the associated forms concerning earnings, occupations, industries, positions, or businesses that may employ such workers. However, DHS does not rule out the possibility that some portion of the population might earn the average wage for all occupations. Therefore, this analysis uses both the effective minimum hourly wage rate of $13.97 to estimate a lower bound and an average wage rate for all occupations of $31.48 as an upper bound in consideration of the variance in average wages across States.[225]
国土安全部(DHS)在估算时间机会成本时考虑工人福利因素,方法是利用劳工统计局(BLS)最新报告中关于全体美国平民工人平均总薪酬的数据,计算出福利与工资比率乘数。[226] 国土安全部估计该福利与工资比率乘数为1.45,该乘数包含雇员工资薪金以及带薪休假、保险和退休金等福利的全部成本。[227] 因此,利用该福利与工资比率乘数,国土安全部计算出处于区间高端的个人的总薪酬率为每小时45.65美元,其中所有职业的平均工资估算为每小时31.48美元,平均福利为每小时14.17美元。[228] 国土安全部计算出处于区间低端的个人的总薪酬率为每小时20.26美元,其中第10百分位的每小时工资估算为13.97美元,平均福利为每小时6.29美元。[229] 本分析中所有量化的成本和转移支付估算均采用基于有效最低时薪和所有职业平均时薪的低限和高限薪酬区间。
DHS accounts for worker benefits when estimating the opportunity cost of time by calculating a benefits-to-wage multiplier using the most recent BLS report detailing average total employee compensation for all civilian American workers.[226] DHS estimates the benefits-to-wage multiplier to be 1.45, which incorporates employee wages and salaries and the full cost of benefits, such as paid leave, insurance, and retirement.[227] Therefore, using the benefits-to-wage multiplier, DHS calculates the total rate of compensation for individuals at the high end of the range as $45.65 where the average wage estimate for all occupations is $31.48 per hour and the average benefits are $14.17 per hour.[228] DHS calculates the total rate of compensation for individuals at the lower end of the range as $20.26 per hour, where the 10th percentile hourly wage estimate is $13.97 per hour and the average benefits are $6.29 per hour.[229] All of the quantified estimates of costs and transfer payments in this analysis incorporate lower and upper bound compensation ranges based on the effective minimum hourly wage and the average hourly wage across all occupations.
为了得出年薪,我们将每小时工资乘以年工作小时数。典型的年工作小时数为2,080小时(每周40小时乘以一年52周)。然而,并非所有美国工人均为全职就业,因此我们对每周工作小时数进行了调整。劳工统计局目前报告称,所有私营非农行业的平均每周工作小时数为34.3小时。[230] 利用此调整,我们得出每年工作1,784小时(每周34.3小时乘以一年52周)。由于目前(c)(18)类就业授权文件(EAD)的有效期最长为1年,国土安全部将有效最低时薪的平均值20.26美元和所有职业的平均每小时薪酬率45.65美元分别乘以1,784小时,从而估算出年收入分别为36,144美元和81,440美元。
To obtain the annual salary we multiply the hourly wage by annual work hours. The typical annual number of work hours is 2,080 (40 hours per week times 52 weeks in a year). However, not all American workers are employed full-time, so we make an adjustment to number of hours worked per week. BLS currently reports that average weekly hours across all private nonfarm industries is 34.3 hours.[230] Using this adjustment we arrive at 1,784 hours worked per year (34.3 hours per week times 52 weeks in a year). Since the current validity period of a (c)(18) EAD is up to 1 year, DHS multiplied the total rate of compensation using the average effective minimum hourly wage rate of $20.26 and the average hourly compensation rate across all occupations of $45.65 by 1,784 hours to estimate the annual earnings of $36,144 and $81,440, respectively.
表V.21显示了针对在最后未决诉讼程序期间(OSUP)临时获释的外国人,初次批准和续期批准两种工资估算范围下的两个估计人口范围,以及相应的潜在收入损失。关于本规则下报告于A栏和C栏的估计批准数,以及未实施本规则情况下报告于E栏的基准申请人估计数,相关影响评估均基于以下假设:所有根据(c)(18)类别获批就业授权的人员均进入劳动力市场。这一假设具有合理性,因为除极少数例外情形外,外国人通常不会在不期望获得经济利益的情况下,承担申请就业授权所需的直接申请费用(就存在申请费用的相关就业授权类别而言)和与时间相关的机会成本。然而,实际上,外国人可能因与本行动无特定关联的其他各种原因而未能就业。
Table V.21 shows the two estimated population ranges for initial and renewal approvals for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens temporarily released on OSUP and the corresponding potential lost earnings. Regarding the estimated approvals under this rule reported in Columns A and C and the estimated baseline filers without this rule reported in Column E, the assessments of possible impacts rely on the assumption that everyone who was approved for employment authorization under the (c)(18) category entered the labor force. This assumption is justifiable because aliens, with few exceptions, generally would not have expended the direct filing (for the pertinent employment authorization categories in which there is a filing fee) and time-related opportunity costs associated with applying for employment authorization if the aliens did not expect to recoup an economic benefit. Realistically, however, aliens might not be employed for any number of other reasons not specifically relevant to this action.
截至2024年12月,全国失业率为4.1%。[231] 即使在失业率较低的情况下,劳动力市场中仍存在持续且相当大的职位流动。外国人可能因这种正常的职位流动或任何特定案件相关的因素和条件而处于失业状态。因此,我们认为合理的做法是对表V.10中A栏、B栏和G栏的按比例调整后的人口数进行预测,得出表V.21中A栏、C栏和E栏的数据,以反映当前的失业状况,具体做法是将就业率(即1减去0.041)纳入计算,得出0.959。[232]
The national unemployment rate as of December 2024 was 4.1 percent.[231] There is constant and considerable job turnover in the labor market even when the unemployment rate is low. Aliens could be unemployed due to this normal turnover or from any number of case-specific factors and conditions. As such, we believe it is reasonable to project scaled populations (from Table V.10 Columns A, B, and G) in Table V.21 Columns A, C, and E to account for current unemployment, which is conducted by integrating the employment rate, as unity minus 0.041, to arrive at 0.959.[232]
表V.21(A)显示了基于下限( 印刷页34425)工资年收入36,144美元得出的初次就业许可批准数量下限和上限范围的成本估算。根据(c)(18)“人口”部分所制定的预测,该规则下每个群体的总收入在B列、D列和F列中列出。G列和H列列出了潜在的收入损失,计算方法是用当前基线(F列)减去该规则所涉群体的潜在收入(B列和D列)。同样,表V.21(B)基于上限(平均)工资年收入81,440美元,重复列出了初次就业许可批准数量下限和上限范围的估算。表V.21(C)和表V.21(D)分别基于下限和上限工资年收入,针对续期就业许可批准数量的下限和上限范围,重复列出了表V.21(A)和表V.21(B)中的估算。
Table V.21(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the lower bound ( printed page 34425) wage annual earnings of $36,144. The total earnings for each population under the rule based on the projections developed in the (c)(18) “Population” section is reported in Columns B, D and F. Columns G and H present the potential lost earnings, by subtracting the potential earnings from rule populations (Columns B and D) from the current baseline (Column F). Similarly, Table V.21(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the upper bound (average) wage annual earnings of $81,440. Tables V.21(C) and V.21(D) repeat the estimates from Table V.21(A) and V.21(B) for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal employment authorization approvals based on the lower and upper bound wage annual earnings, respectively.
