( printed page 34427)
美国国土安全部(DHS)将因取消(c)(18)类别就业许可资格而给暂时根据监管令被释放的外国人造成的收入损失,作为衡量该规则总体影响的指标,这既可以作为分配性影响(转移支付),也可以作为企业生产力损失成本的替代指标。该指标并未包括企业因利润损失和机会成本而产生的额外成本,也未包括对外国人支持网络造成的分配性影响。不过,本分析将在后文进一步讨论这些成本。如表V.21所示,潜在的收入损失取决于暂时根据监管令被释放、且仍符合就业许可资格并继续工作的外国人数量,以及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间,总收入损失预计在74亿美元至179亿美元之间。[233] 按7%的贴现率折算为年度值,初次和续期就业许可文件(EAD)持有人的收入损失预计在7.552亿美元至18亿美元之间(表V.25)。234 ( 印刷页34428) 按3%的贴现率折算为年度值,初次和续期EAD持有人的收入损失预计在7.463亿美元至18亿美元之间(表V.25)。
DHS uses the lost compensation to aliens temporarily released on an order of supervision as a measure of the overall impact of removing eligibility for employment authorization under the (c)(18) category—either as distributional impacts (transfers) or as a proxy for costs to businesses for lost productivity. It does not include additional costs to businesses for lost profits and opportunity costs or the distributional impacts for those in an alien's support network. However, these costs will be discussed further in this analysis. As shown in Table V.21, the potential lost earnings depend on the number of aliens released temporarily on OSUP who remain eligible for employment authorization and continue to work, as well as their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 to FY 2034, the total lost earnings would range from $7.4 billion to $17.9 billion.[233] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $755.2 million to $1.8 billion (Table V.25). 234 ( printed page 34428) Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $746.3 million to $1.8 billion (Table V.25).
根据本拟议规则中提出的资格标准,不再符合续期就业许可资格的EAD持有人将遭受收入损失。此外,国土安全部承认,如果申请续期的外国人的雇主目前未加入E-Verify系统且选择不加入该系统,则这些外国人可能会面临额外的收入损失。在这种情况下,如果申请续期的外国人无法在参与E-Verify的雇主处找到工作,他们可能会遭受收入损失。
EAD holders who would no longer be eligible to renew their employment authorization under the proposed eligibility criteria in this proposed rule would incur lost earnings. Additionally, DHS acknowledges the potential for additional lost compensation to aliens applying for renewal if their employers are not currently enrolled in E-Verify and opt not to enroll in the E-Verify program. In such cases, aliens applying for renewal could lose earnings if they are unable to find employment with an employer who participates in E-Verify.
国土安全部认识到,在不考虑通货膨胀影响的情况下,收入通常会随时间推移而增长,EAD持有人未来的收入可能高于本分析中的估算值。此外,由于就业许可续期必然是在初次就业许可获批之后发生的,随着时间推移,续期所获工资及总体报酬可能会更高。因此,这一因素可能会使收入损失的估算值产生向下偏误。然而,我们目前尚未找到可行的方法将这种可能性纳入量化估算之中。
DHS recognizes that, excluding the effects of inflation, earnings generally rise over time and the earnings of EAD holders could be higher in the future than estimated in this analysis. Moreover, since employment authorization renewals necessarily follow initial employment authorization approvals, in time, wages earned and, hence total compensation, could be higher for renewals. Accordingly, this effect could have a downward bias in the estimate of earnings losses. However, we see no tractable way at present to incorporate this possibility into the quantified estimates.
除上述量化影响外,对于因不再符合就业许可资格而受到影响的、根据监管令被释放的外国人而言,还可能存在定性影响。对于无法续期就业许可或无法获得初次就业许可的(c)(18)类人群而言,很可能会因收入损失而面临经济困难。该群体成员可能需要依靠其支持网络获得经济和社会援助,这些支持网络可能包括但不限于家庭成员和朋友、宗教和慈善组织、私营非营利性服务机构、州和地方政府以及非政府组织(NGO)。国土安全部认为,本规则对外国人支持网络造成的直接间接影响,很可能不会显著超过该外国人在没有本规则情况下本应获得的工资和福利。
In addition to the above quantified impacts, there could be qualitative impacts for aliens released on an order of supervision who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization. For the (c)(18) population that would not be able to renew their employment authorization or obtain initial employment authorization, there would likely be an impact in terms of lost income, which could pose economic hardships. Members of this population may need to rely on their support networks for financial and social assistance, which could involve, but may not be limited to, family members and friends, religious and charitable organizations, private non-profit providers, State and local governments, and NGOs. DHS believes that the immediate indirect impact of this rule to an alien's support network is likely not significantly more than the wages and benefits the alien would have earned without this rule.
二、生物识别信息
ii. Biometrics
如前言所述,现行国土安全部(DHS)法规赋予美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)一般权限,可要求任何申请移民福利的外国人在个案基础上提交生物识别信息。[235] 当USCIS确定申请(c)(18)类别的外国人须提交生物识别信息时,该外国人将收到USCIS发出的生物识别服务预约通知,要求其前往应用支持中心(ASC)提交生物识别信息,以协助身份核实并便利(c)(18)就业授权文件(EAD)卡的制作等事项。[236] DHS拟将提交生物识别信息的要求予以法典化,即所有根据(c)(18)类别提交I-765表格的外国人,无论是初次申请还是续签申请,均须前往ASC提交生物识别信息。DHS拟使用申请(c)(18)的外国人所提交的生物识别信息进行犯罪记录筛查。[237] 提交生物识别信息要求外国人前往ASC参加生物识别服务预约。在以往的规则制定中,DHS估计前往ASC的平均往返距离为50英里,平均往返耗时为2.5小时。[238] 出行成本还包括基于估计的50英里往返距离,按2025年总务管理局(GSA)私人自用汽车使用里程费率每英里0.70美元计算的里程费。[239] 由于每名外国人在ASC提交生物识别信息需花费1小时10分钟(即1.17小时),将ASC耗时与出行时间相加,共计3.67小时。[240] 按工资下限和上限估算,我们估计提交生物识别服务的时间机会成本分别为每小时74.35美元和167.54美元。[241] 估计的出行成本为35美元,即每英里报销费率0.70美元乘以50英里的出行距离。将时间机会成本与出行成本相加,得出每名外国人提交生物识别信息的成本在工资下限为109.35美元,在工资上限为202.54美元。[242]
As discussed in the preamble, current DHS regulations provide general authority for USCIS to require the submission of biometrics from any alien filing for an immigration benefit on a case-by-case basis.[235] When USCIS determines that an alien applying for (c)(18) is required to submit biometrics, they receive a biometrics services appointment notice from USCIS to submit biometrics at an ASC to assist in identity verification and facilitate (c)(18) EAD card production, among other things.[236] DHS is proposing to codify the requirement to submit biometrics, where all aliens who file Form I-765 under the (c)(18) category—for both initial and renewal applications—would be required to appear at an ASC and submit biometrics. DHS proposes to use the biometrics submitted by aliens applying for (c)(18) to screen for criminal history.[237] The submission of biometrics requires that aliens travel to an ASC for the biometric services appointment. In past rulemakings, DHS estimated that the average round-trip distance to an ASC is 50 miles, and that the average travel time for the trip is 2.5 hours.[238] The cost of travel also includes a mileage charge based on the estimated 50 mile round trip at the 2025 General Services Administration (GSA) rate of $0.70 per mile for use of a privately owned automobile.[239] Because an individual alien would spend 1 hour and 10 minutes (1.17 hours) at an ASC to submit biometrics, summing the ASC time and travel time yields 3.67 hours.[240] At the lower and upper wage bounds, we estimate the opportunity costs of time to submit biometrics services are $74.35 and $167.54 per hour, respectively.[241] The estimated travel cost is $35, which is the per mileage reimbursement rate of $0.70 multiplied by the 50-mile travel distance. Summing the opportunity cost of time and travel costs generates a per alien biometrics submission cost of $109.35 at the lower bound wage and $202.54 at the upper bound wage.[242]
表V.22显示了针对根据监管令获释的外国人的初次申请和续签申请的两个工资估计范围所对应的两个人口数量区间,以及提交生物识别信息的相应总成本。表V.22(A)显示了在工资下限提交成本109.35美元的情况下,I-765表格初次申请接收量的下限和上限成本估计。B列和D列中的下限和上限预计生物识别信息接收量,是根据本规则在(c)(18)“人口”部分表V.10中给出的下限和上限批准量。C列和E列的总成本给出了基于下限的未折现成本范围。同样,表V.22(B)重复了基于上限提交成本202.54美元的I-765表格初次申请接收量的下限和上限范围估计。表V.22(C)和表V.22(D)分别重复了基于下限和上限提交成本的续签I-765表格接收量的下限和上限范围估计。
Table V.22 shows the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens released on an order of supervision and the corresponding total cost to submit biometrics. Table V.22(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts at the lower bound submission cost of $109.35. The lower and upper bound projected biometrics receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.10 in the (c)(18) “Population” section. The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound. Similarly, Table V.22(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts based on the upper bound submission cost of $202.54. Tables V.22(C) and V.22(D) repeat these estimates for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal Form I-765 receipts based on the lower and upper bound submission costs, respectively.

如表V.22所示,提交生物识别信息的成本取决于根据监管令暂时获释并申请就业授权的外国人数量及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间内,估计提交生物识别信息的总成本范围为919,629美元至490万美元。[243] 按7%的贴现率年化计算,提交生物识别信息的估计成本范围为91,843美元至498,612美元(表V.25)。按3%的贴现率年化计算,提交生物识别信息的估计成本范围为91,910美元至499,194美元(表V.25)。DHS认识到,所呈现的估计数可能偏高,因为有一部分人口已经收到生物识别服务预约通知并完成了生物识别信息提交。根据本拟议规则的新要求,这部分人口将继续完成生物识别信息提交,因此不会产生新的成本。
As shown in Table V.22, the cost to submit biometrics depends on the number of aliens temporarily released on an order of supervision who apply for employment authorization and their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034, the estimated total cost to submit biometrics would range from $919,629 to $4.9 million.[243] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $91,843 to $498,612 (Table V.25). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $91,910 to $499,194 (Table V.25). DHS recognizes that the estimates presented may be an overestimate, as a portion of the population already receives a biometrics services appointment notice and completes biometrics submission. Under the new requirement of this proposed rule, this portion of the population would continue to complete biometrics and thus not incur new costs.
iii. 表格
iii. Forms
对于仍符合就业授权资格的外国人而言,拟议规则将增加申请就业授权的外国人群体填写I-765表格的时间负担。本规则还拟议为根据监管令被释放、正在申请初次就业授权或续展就业授权的外国人增加申请程序和证据要求。拟议的新要求包括提交I-765WS表格及支持性文件[244],以证明该外国人就业的经济必要性,并且,仅针对申请续展的外国人,还需提供该外国人美国雇主在E-Verify系统中登记的名称及该雇主的E-Verify公司识别号。
For aliens who remain eligible to be employment authorized, the proposed rule would increase the time burden for Form I-765 on the population of aliens applying for employment authorization. This rule also proposes to add filing procedures and evidentiary requirements for aliens released on an order of supervision who are seeking initial employment authorization or renewing employment authorization. The proposed new requirements include submitting Form I-765WS along with supporting documentation [244] to establish the alien's economic necessity for employment and, for aliens applying for renewal only, the name of the alien's U.S. employer as listed in E-Verify and that employer's E-Verify Company Identification Number.
国土安全部(DHS)估计填写I-765表格的时间负担为4.38小时。246如果本规则最终确定生效,在生效日期之后申请就业授权的根据监管令被释放的外国人,本拟议规则将使I-765表格的时间负担增加30分钟(0.5小时),合计为4.88小时。246时间负担增加的原因是拟议的法规要求,即外国人必须受雇于在E-Verify系统中保持良好资格的美国雇主,方可获得就业授权续展资格。这一变化将使每份申请的时间机会成本按有效最低时薪计算增加约10.13美元(第34431页),按所有职业的平均薪酬计算增加约22.83美元。247
DHS estimates the time burden for completing Form I-765 is 4.38 hours.245 For aliens released on OSUP who apply for employment authorization after the effective date of this rule should it be finalized, this proposed rule would increase the time burden of Form I-765 by 30 minutes (0.5 hours) for a total of 4.88 hours.246 The increased time burden is due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens be employed by a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify to be eligible for a renewal of employment authorization. This change would increase the opportunity cost of time for each application by approximately $10.13 based on the effective minimum hourly ( printed page 34431) compensation and by about $22.83 based on the average compensation for all occupations.247
本拟议规则还将要求根据(c)(18)类别申请就业授权的外国人提交I-765WS表格。目前,证明存在经济必要性以进行就业仅被列为可酌情考虑的因素之一,而并非(c)(18)类别下的强制性要求。在本拟议规则中,国土安全部拟将其规定为强制性要求。国土安全部估计目前填写I-765WS表格的时间负担为30分钟(0.5小时)。[248]然而,由于拟议的法规要求外国人就其在I-765WS表格中申报的财务记录提供补充文件,该时间负担将增加30分钟(0.5小时),使填写该表格的新时间负担达到1小时。对于暂时根据监管令被释放、持续符合资格并在本规则(如最终确定)生效日期之后申请就业授权的外国人,本拟议规则将使每名外国人的时间机会成本按有效最低时薪计算增加20.26美元,按所有职业的平均薪酬计算增加45.65美元。[249]综合I-765表格和I-765WS表格的新增成本,每名外国人增加的总时间负担成本,在较低和较高薪酬标准下分别将增加30.39美元和68.48美元。[250]
This proposed rule would also make it a requirement to submit Form I-765WS for aliens applying for employment authorization under the (c)(18) category. Currently, proving the existence of economic necessity to be employed is listed as a discretionary factor for consideration, but it is not a requirement under the (c)(18) category. In this proposed rule, DHS would make this a mandatory requirement. DHS estimates the current time burden for completing Form I-765WS is 30 minutes (0.5 hours).[248] However, due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens provide supplementary documentation for their financial records reported in Form I-765WS, the time burden will increase 30 minutes (0.5 hours), making the new time burden to complete the form 1 hour. For aliens temporarily released on an order of supervision who continue to be eligible and apply for employment authorization after the effective date of the rule should it be finalized, the proposed rule would increase the opportunity cost of time for each alien by $20.26 based on the effective minimum hourly compensation and $45.65 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[249] Combining the new costs of the Forms I-765 and I-765WS, the total per alien increased time burden would add costs of $30.39 and $68.48 at the respective lower and upper bound compensation rates.[250]
表V.23列出了针对初次申请和续展申请两类人群填写I-765表格和I-765WS表格所增加的时间机会成本。表V.23(A)显示了基于30.39美元的下限额外时间机会成本,针对I-765表格初次申请收件量的下限和上限范围的成本估算。B栏和D栏中的下限和上限预计表格收件量,是根据(c)(18)“人群”部分表V.10中本规则下的下限和上限批准量得出的。C栏和E栏的总成本提供了基于下限薪酬标准的未贴现成本范围。同样,表V.23(B)根据68.48美元的上限额外时间机会成本,重复列出了EAD初次申请收件量下限和上限范围的估算值。表V.23(C)和表V.23(D)则分别根据下限和上限薪酬时间机会成本,针对I-765表格续展申请收件量的下限和上限范围重复列出了这些估算值。
Table V.23 shows the additional opportunity cost of time for filing Form I-765 and Form I-765WS for the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts. Table V.23(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts based on the lower bound additional opportunity cost of time of $30.39. The lower and upper bound projected forms receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.10 in the (c)(18) “Population” section. The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound compensation. Similarly, Table V.23(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial EAD receipts based on the upper bound additional opportunity cost of time of $68.48. Tables V.23(C) and V.23(D) repeat these estimates for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal Form I-765 receipts based on the lower and upper bound wage opportunity cost of time, respectively.

如表中所示,在2025财年至2034财年这10年期间,因填写表格的时间负担增加而产生的预计总时间机会成本,预计将在约255,579美元至170万美元之间。[251]按7%的贴现率折算为年化值,预计成本将在25,525美元至168,584美元之间(表V.25)。按3%的贴现率折算为年化值,预计成本将在25,543美元至168,781美元之间(表V.25)。
As indicated in the table, the estimated total opportunity costs of time incurred as a result of the increased time burden for completing the forms over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034 would range from about $255,579 to $1.7 million.[251] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated costs would range from $25,525 to $168,584 (Table V.25). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated costs would range from $25,543 to $168,781 (Table V.25).
对于根据暂缓执行监管(OSUP)临时释放并使用ICE第I-220B表格的外国人而言,预计的时间负担不会发生变化。ICE负责填写I-220B表格,并且该表格目前已在就业授权申请过程中提交。
There would be no change in the estimated time burden for aliens temporarily released on OSUP for ICE Form I-220B. ICE completes Form I-220B and it is currently already submitted during the employment authorization application process.
四、税收
iv. Taxes
该拟议规则短期内可能减少向联邦政府缴纳的税款(一种转移支付)。在原可由(c)(18)类外国劳工担任的职位(初次情形)或曾由其担任的职位(续期情形)出现空缺期间,联邦政府将无法征收相关税款。
This proposed rule could reduce taxes paid to the Federal Government (a transfer payment) in the short term. During the period of vacancy for a job that could be (initial) or formerly (renewal) held by the (c)(18) alien worker, the Federal Government would not be collecting taxes.
如果企业无法为原本由不再具备工作授权资格的受影响外国人所从事的职位找到劳动力,那么未能履行的劳动将导致雇主和雇员向各级政府缴纳的税款减少。因此,(c)(18)“收入”部分所述的收入损失将导致此类税收减少。由于个人和家庭的纳税情况差异很大,各州所得税税率也各不相同,因此很难量化劳动力市场就业对联邦和州所得税收入的影响。[252]但是,国土安全部(DHS)能够估算就业税,即医疗保险税和社会保障税的潜在缴纳影响,两者合计税率为7.65%(分别为6.2%和1.45%)。[253]由于雇员和雇主均需各自缴纳相应部分的医疗保险税和社会保障税,因此雇员和雇主向医疗保险和社会保障缴纳的税收转移支付总水平为15.3%。[254]DHS按未计负担的收入基础估算税收影响。该计算方法为将稳定后的收入乘以15.3%的就业税率,再将所得乘积除以1.45的福利负担倍数。(第34434页)255
If businesses cannot find labor for positions that affected aliens who are no longer eligible for work authorization would have occupied, then the unperformed labor would result in a reduction in taxes from employers and employees to governments. Accordingly, the lost earnings derived in the (c)(18) “Earnings” section will contribute to such a reduction in taxes paid. It is challenging to quantify impacts on Federal and State income tax revenue from employment in the labor market because individual and household tax situations vary widely as do the various States income tax rates.[252] However, DHS is able to estimate the potential contributory effects on employment taxes, namely Medicare and Social Security, which have a combined tax rate of 7.65 percent (6.2 percent and 1.45 percent, respectively).[253] With both the employee and employer paying their respective portion of Medicare and Social Security taxes, the total level of tax transfer payments from employees and employers to Medicare and Social Security is 15.3 percent.[254] DHS estimates the tax impacts on the unburdened earnings basis. This is calculated by multiplying the stabilized earnings by the employment tax rate of 15.3 percent and dividing the resulting ( printed page 34434) product by the benefits burden multiple of 1.45.255
为估算就业税损失的范围,我们采用表V.21第G列和第H列中按现行工资率和平均薪酬率估算的初次和续期申请人预计收入损失范围,将每年数值乘以15.3%,再除以1.45。这些计算结果列于表V.24中。税收影响的实际数值将取决于在没有本规则的情况下,企业能够轻易为多少受影响的EAD持有人找到合理劳动力替代者。
To estimate the range of employment tax losses, we take the estimated lost earnings for the range of initial and renewal projected filers at the prevailing and average compensation rates from Table V.21, columns G and H, multiply each year by 15.3 percent, and divide by 1.45. These calculations are shown in Table V.24. The actual value of tax impacts would depend on the number of affected EAD holders that businesses would have been able to easily find reasonable labor substitutes for in the absence of this rule.
(第34435页)
( printed page 34435)
(第34436页)
( printed page 34436)
DHS估计,在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间,收入损失可能介于74亿美元至179亿美元之间,[256]由此产生的相应就业税损失或转移支付将介于7.802亿美元至19亿美元之间。[257]按7%的贴现率年化计算,就业税损失约为7970万美元至1.923亿美元(表V.25)。按3%的贴现率年化计算,就业税损失约为7870万美元至1.902亿美元(表V.25)。此外,根据雇员的具体情况,可能还存在本文未估算的其他联邦所得税损失。也可能存在因管辖区不同而有所差异的州和地方所得税损失,但DHS无法对此进行量化。我们注意到,税收转移支付的潜在减少仅适用于薪酬影响,而不适用于劳动力更替成本、表格填写时间负担相关成本,或E-Verify系统的实施与使用成本。
Lost earnings, which DHS estimates could range between $7.4 billion and $17.9 billion over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034,[256] would result in corresponding employment tax losses or transfers ranging between $780.2 million and $1.9 billion.[257] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, employment tax losses would range from approximately $79.7 million to $192.3 million (Table V.25). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, employment tax losses would range from approximately $78.7 million to $190.2 million (Table V.25). Again, depending on the circumstances of the employee, there could be additional Federal income tax losses not estimated here. There may also be State and local income tax losses that would vary according to the jurisdiction, but that DHS is unable to quantify. We note that the potential decrease in tax transfers only applies to the compensation impacts, not to labor turnover costs, costs associated with form time burdens, or implementation and use of E-Verify.
五、总计
v. Total
在前述(c)(18)“货币化影响分析”各部分中,我们列出了该拟议规则在劳动收入损失、生物特征信息提交、表格填写时间负担增加以及税收减少形式的转移支付方面的影响估算。总影响是通过汇总表V.21至表V.24中初次和续期影响的最大值和最小值估算而得出的。因此,表V.25列出了该拟议规则估算的货币化成本范围,其中表V.25(A)列出最大估算值,表V.25(B)列出最小估算值。该表的每个分节均提供未贴现的10年期总值,以及按7%和3%贴现率计算的现值成本和年化成本。
In the previous (c)(18) “Monetized Impact Analysis” sections we presented estimates of the impacts of the proposed rule germane to lost labor earnings, biometrics submission, increased time burdens for completing forms, and transfer payments in the form of reduced taxes. The total impacts are aggregated by summing the total initial and renewal impacts from Tables V.21 through V.24 in terms of the maximum and minimum estimates. Therefore, Table V.25 shows the range of estimated monetized costs of the proposed rule, where Table V.25(A) presents the maximum estimates, and Table V.25(B) presents the minimum estimates. For each subsection of the table, the 10-year totals are provided in undiscounted 10-year total values, as well as the present value costs and annualized costs discounted at 7 percent and 3 percent.

如表V.25所示,拟议规则在2025财年至2034财年期间的预计10年货币化未贴现成本，按所采用的假设计算，最高可达约197.6亿美元，最低成本估计为81.8亿美元。[258] 本规则的大部分成本将源于劳动收入损失,如果企业无法为临时根据境外监管下的监督释放（OSUP）获释的外国人原本将填补的职位找到合理的劳动力替代者。国土安全部（DHS）指出,存在未反映在上述估计中的未量化成本。
As Table V.25 shows, the projected 10-year monetized undiscounted costs of the proposed rule for the period FY 2025 through FY 2034 could be as high as about $19.76 billion with a minimum cost estimate of $8.18 billion under the assumptions relied on.[258] The majority of the costs of this rule would result from lost labor earnings, if companies are unable to find reasonable labor substitutes for the positions that aliens temporarily released on OSUP would have filled. DHS notes there are unquantified costs not reflected in the estimates above.
b. 对获得暂缓遣返行动的外国人的酌情就业授权（(c)(14)）
b. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Granted Deferred Action ((c)(14))
一、收入
i. Earnings
国土安全部没有关于获得暂缓遣返行动（无论是初次还是续期阶段）的外国人的工资或职业的信息,因为这些外国人劳动者获得的是开放市场就业授权文件（EAD）,该文件不包含或要求任何有关其就业情况的数据。由于许多申请(c)(14)的外国人与申请(c)(18)的外国人具有许多相同的特征,例如都是相对较新进入劳动力市场的人员,且有效期均不超过一年等,国土安全部采用了与(c)(18)“收入”部分所述相同的工资和收入估算。对于每名外国人,所采用的估计年收入下限为36,144美元,上限为81,440美元。
DHS has no information on wages or occupations of aliens who have been granted deferred action, at the initial or renewal stage, since these alien workers obtain an open-market EAD that does not include or require any data on their employment. Because many of the aliens applying for (c)(14) would have many of the same characteristics as aliens applying for (c)(18), such as being relatively new entrants to the labor force and having a validity period not exceeding one year, etc., DHS used the same wage and earnings estimates described in the (c)(18) “Earnings” section. For each alien, the estimated annual lower bound earnings used is $36,144 and the upper bound earnings is $81,440.
表V.26显示了针对获得暂缓遣返行动的外国人的初次和续期批准的两个估计人口范围,以及相应的工资估计两个范围及其对应的潜在收入损失。关于A栏和C栏中报告的本规则下的估计批准数,以及E栏中报告的无本规则情况下的基线申请人估计数,对可能影响的评估依赖于以下假设,即每一位根据(c)(14)类别获得就业授权的人都进入了劳动力市场。这一假设是合理的,因为外国人(极少数例外情况除外)通常不会在没有预期获得经济利益的情况下,承担申请就业授权所涉及的直接申请费(适用于收取申请费的相关就业授权类别)以及与时间相关的机会成本。然而实际上,外国人可能由于与本行动并无特别相关性的其他多种原因而未能就业。
Table V.26 shows the two estimated population ranges for initial and renewal approvals for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens granted deferred action and the corresponding potential lost earnings. Regarding the estimated approvals under this rule reported in Columns A and C and the estimated baseline filers without this rule reported in Column E, the assessments of possible impacts rely on the assumption that everyone who was approved for employment authorization under the (c)(14) category entered the labor force. This assumption is justifiable because aliens, with few exceptions, generally would not have expended the direct filing (for the pertinent employment authorization categories in which there is a filing fee) and time-related opportunity costs associated with applying for employment authorization if the aliens did not expect to recoup an economic benefit. Realistically, however, aliens might not be employed for any number of other reasons not specifically relevant to this action.
截至2024年12月,全国失业率为4.1%。[259] 即使在失业率较低时,劳动力市场中仍存在持续且相当大的工作岗位流动。外国人可能因这种正常的岗位流动或任何其他具体案例的因素和情况而处于失业状态。因此,我们认为有理由对表V.26 A栏、C栏和E栏中的按比例调整人口数（源自表V.15：初次和续期基线申请量、本规则下初次和续期批准的下限数、以及本规则下初次和续期批准的上限数）进行预测,以反映当前的失业状况,具体做法是将就业率(即1减去0.041)纳入计算,得出0.959。[260]
The national unemployment rate as of December 2024 was 4.1 percent.[259] There is constant and considerable job turnover in the labor market even when the unemployment rate is low. Aliens could be unemployed due to this normal turnover or from any number of case-specific factors and conditions. As such, we believe it is reasonable to project scaled populations (from Table V.15: initial and renewal baseline receipts, lower bound initial and renewal approvals under this rule, and upper bound initial and renewal approvals under this rule) in Table V.26 Columns A, C, and E to account for current unemployment, which is conducted by integrating the employment rate, as unity minus 0.041, to arrive at 0.959.[260]
表V.26(A)显示了基于下限工资年收入36,144美元计算的初次就业授权批准下限和上限范围的成本估算。根据(c)(14)“人口”部分制定的预测,本规则下每类人口的总收入分别在B栏、D栏和F栏中报告。G栏和H栏列出了潜在的收入损失,计算方法是将规则人口的潜在收入(B栏和D栏)与当前基线(F栏)相减。同样,表V.26(B)基于上限(平均)工资年收入81,440美元,重复了初次就业授权批准下限和上限范围的估算。表V.26(C)和表V.26(D)分别基于下限和上限工资年收入,针对续期就业授权批准的下限和上限范围,重复了表V.26(A)和表V.26(B)中的估算。
Table V.26(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the lower bound wage annual earnings of $36,144. The total earnings for each population under the rule based on the projections developed in the (c)(14) “Population” section is reported in Columns B, D and F. Columns G and H present the potential lost earnings, by subtracting the potential earnings from rule populations (Columns B and D) from the current baseline (Column F). Similarly, Table V.26(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the upper bound (average) wage annual earnings of $81,440. Tables V.26(C) and V.26(D) repeat the estimates from Table V.26(A) and V.26(B) for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal employment authorization approvals based on the lower and upper bound wage annual earnings, respectively.
(印刷页第34439页)
( printed page 34439)
(印刷页第34440页)
( printed page 34440)
美国国土安全部（DHS）将对获得延缓强制遣返待遇的外国人造成的损失补偿，作为衡量取消其在（c）(14)类别下就业授权资格所产生的总体影响的一项指标，这既可以视为分配性影响（转移支付），也可以作为企业因生产力损失而产生成本的替代指标。该指标不包括企业因利润损失和机会成本而产生的额外成本，也不包括对外国人支持网络中其他相关方所产生的分配性影响。然而，本分析将在后文进一步讨论这些成本。如表V.26所示，潜在的收入损失（第34441页印刷版）取决于获得延缓强制遣返待遇并仍具备就业授权资格并继续工作的外国人人数，以及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间，总收入损失预计在13亿美元至87亿美元之间。[261] 按7%的贴现率年化计算，初次申请和续期就业授权文件（EAD）持有人的收入损失预计在1.355亿美元至9.323亿美元之间（表V.30）。[262] 按3%的贴现率年化计算，初次申请和续期EAD持有人的收入损失预计在1.307亿美元至8.986亿美元之间（表V.30）。
DHS uses the lost compensation to aliens granted deferred action as a measure of the overall impact of removing eligibility for employment authorization under the (c)(14) category—either as distributional impacts (transfers) or as a proxy for costs to businesses for lost productivity. It does not include additional costs to businesses for lost profits and opportunity costs or the distributional impacts for those in an alien's support network. However, these costs will be discussed further in this analysis. As shown in Table V.26, the potential lost ( printed page 34441) earnings depend on the number of aliens granted deferred action who remain eligible for employment authorization and continue to work, as well as their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034, the total lost earnings would range from $1.3 billion to $8.7 billion.[261] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $135.5 million to $932.3 million (Table V.30).[262] Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $130.7 million to $898.6 million (Table V.30).
根据本规则拟议的资格标准，不再具备续期就业授权资格的EAD持有人将遭受收入损失。此外，美国国土安全部认识到，如果申请续期的外国人的雇主目前尚未加入E-Verify系统且选择不加入该系统，则这些外国人可能会面临额外的补偿损失。在此类情况下，如果申请续期的外国人无法找到参与E-Verify系统的雇主提供的工作，他们可能会因此遭受收入损失。
EAD holders who would no longer be eligible to renew their employment authorization under the proposed eligibility criteria in this rule would incur lost earnings. Additionally, DHS acknowledges the potential for additional lost compensation to aliens applying for renewal if their employers are not currently enrolled in E-Verify and opt not to enroll in the E-Verify program. In such cases, aliens applying for renewal could lose earnings if they are unable to find employment with an employer who participates in E-Verify.
美国国土安全部认识到，在不考虑通货膨胀影响的情况下，收入通常会随时间推移而增长，EAD持有人未来的收入可能高于本分析中的估计值。此外，由于就业授权续期必然在初次就业授权获批之后进行，随着时间推移，续期申请人所获得的工资乃至总薪酬可能会更高。因此，这种影响可能会使收入损失的估计值出现向下偏差。然而，我们目前尚未找到切实可行的方法将这一可能性纳入量化估计之中。
DHS recognizes that, excluding the effects of inflation, earnings generally rise over time and the earnings of EAD holders could be higher in the future than estimated in this analysis. Moreover, since employment authorization renewals necessarily follow initial employment authorization approvals, in time, wages earned and, hence total compensation, could be higher for renewals. Accordingly, this effect could have a downward bias in the estimate of earnings losses. However, we see no tractable way at present to incorporate this possibility into the quantified estimates.
除上述量化影响外，对于不再具备就业授权资格的已获延缓强制遣返待遇的外国人而言，还可能存在定性影响。对于无法续期其就业授权或无法获得初次就业授权的（c）(14)类人群而言，很可能会产生收入损失方面的影响，这可能导致经济困难。该群体成员可能需要依赖其支持网络获得经济和社会援助，这可能包括但不限于家庭成员和朋友、宗教和慈善组织、私营非营利性服务提供者、州和地方政府以及非政府组织（NGO）。美国国土安全部认为，本规则对外国人支持网络造成的直接间接影响，很可能不会显著超过该外国人在没有本规则情况下本应获得的工资和福利。
In addition to the above quantified impacts, there could be qualitative impacts for aliens granted deferred action who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization. For the (c)(14) population that would not be able to renew their employment authorization or obtain initial employment authorization, there would likely be an impact in terms of lost income, which could pose economic hardships. Members of this population may need to rely on their support networks for financial and social assistance, which could involve, but may not be limited to, family members and friends, religious and charitable organizations, private non-profit providers, State and local governments, and NGOs. DHS believes that the immediate indirect impact of this rule to an alien's support network is likely not significantly more than the wages and benefits the alien would have earned without this rule.
二、生物识别信息
ii. Biometrics
美国国土安全部现行法规赋予美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）逐案要求任何申请移民福利的外国人提交生物识别信息的一般权力。当USCIS确定申请（c）(14)的外国人须提交生物识别信息时，该外国人将收到USCIS发出的生物识别服务预约通知，要求其前往申请支持中心（ASC）提交生物识别信息，以协助身份核实并为（c）(14) EAD卡的制作等提供支持。[263] 美国国土安全部拟将提交生物识别信息的要求正式纳入法规，规定所有依据（c）(14)类别提交I-765表格的外国人，无论是初次申请还是续期申请，均须前往ASC提交生物识别信息。美国国土安全部拟使用申请（c）(14)的外国人所提交的生物识别信息进行犯罪记录筛查。[264] 申请（c）(14)的外国人所承担的成本可能与（c）(18)“生物识别信息”部分所述成本相似，即按照本分析所采用的较低和较高工资标准，每名外国人提交生物识别信息的成本分别为109.35美元和202.54美元。
Current DHS regulations provide general authority for USCIS to require the submission of biometrics from any alien filing for an immigration benefit on a case-by-case basis. When USCIS determines that an alien applying for (c)(14) is required to submit biometrics, they receive a biometrics services appointment notice from USCIS to submit biometrics at an ASC to assist in identity verification and facilitate (c)(14) EAD card production, among other things.[263] DHS is proposing to codify the requirement to submit biometrics, where all aliens who file Form I-765 under the (c)(14) category—for both initial and renewal applications—would be required to appear at an ASC and submit biometrics. DHS proposes to use the biometrics submitted by aliens applying for (c)(14) to screen for criminal history.[264] Aliens applying for (c)(14) would likely experience similar costs to those outlined in the (c)(18) “Biometrics” section, a per alien biometrics submission cost of $109.35 and $202.54 at the respective lower and upper wage rates for this analysis.
表V.27显示了获准延期遣返行动的外国人在初次申请和续签申请两种人口范围内,对应两种工资估算范围的生物特征信息提交总成本。表V.27(A)显示了基于109.35美元的下限提交成本,I-765表初次申请下限和上限人口范围的成本估算。B栏和D栏的生物特征信息申请下限和上限预测值,即本规则下批准数的下限和上限,来自表V.15“(c)(14)人口”部分。[265] C栏和E栏的总成本提供了下限情形下未贴现成本的范围。同样,表V.27(B)基于202.54美元的上限提交成本,重复了I-765表初次申请下限和上限范围的估算。表V.27(C)和V.27(D)分别基于下限和上限提交成本,重复了I-765表续签申请下限和上限范围的这些估算。
Table V.27 shows the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens granted deferred action and the corresponding total cost to submit biometrics. Table V.27(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts at the lower bound submission cost of $109.35. The lower and upper bound projected biometrics receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.15 in the (c)(14) “Population” section.[265] The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound. Similarly, Table V.27(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts based on the upper bound submission cost of $202.54. Tables V.27(C) and V.27(D) repeat these estimates for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal Form I-765 receipts based on the lower and upper bound submission costs, respectively.
(印刷页第34442页)
( printed page 34442)
(印刷页第34443页)
( printed page 34443)
如表V.27所示,提交生物特征信息的成本取决于申请就业授权的获准延期遣返行动外国人人数及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间内,提交生物特征信息的估计总成本预计在1200万美元至3740万美元之间。[266] 按7%的贴现率年化计算,提交生物特征信息的估计成本预计在130万美元至400万美元之间(表V.30)。按3%的贴现率年化计算,提交生物特征信息的估计成本预计在120万美元至380万美元之间(表V.30)。美国国土安全部(DHS)认识到,所列估算数字可能偏高,因为该人群中有一部分人已经收到生物特征信息采集服务预约通知并完成了生物特征信息提交。根据本拟议规则的新要求,这部分人群将继续完成生物特征信息提交,因此不会产生新的成本。
As shown in Table V.27, the cost to submit biometrics depends on the number of aliens granted deferred action who apply for employment authorization and their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 to FY 2034, the estimated total cost to submit biometrics would range from $12.0 million to $37.4 million.[266] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $1.3 million to $4.0 million (Table V.30). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $1.2 million to $3.8 million (Table V.30). DHS recognizes that the estimates presented may be an overestimate, as a portion of the population already receives a biometrics services appointment notice and completes biometrics submission. Under the new requirement of this proposed rule, this portion of the population would continue to complete biometrics, and thus not incur new costs.
三、表格
iii. Forms
对于仍符合就业授权资格的外国人而言,本拟议规则将增加申请就业授权的外国人群体的时间负担。本规则拟为获准延期遣返行动并寻求初次就业授权或续签就业授权的外国人增加申请程序和证明要求。目前,在没有本规则的情况下,根据(c)(14)延期遣返行动类别申请就业授权的外国人必须填写I-765WS表,以确定该外国人是否存在经济工作需要。由于该工作表已是该人群的现有要求,因此填写该工作表所需的时间负担未计入本分析。但是,拟议规则将要求提供书面证明文件,以支持I-765WS表中所提供的财务信息。[267] 对于获准延期遣返行动并仍符合就业授权资格的外国人而言,拟议规则将要求申请(c)(14)续签的外国人证明其受雇于或正在寻求受雇于在E-Verify系统中保持良好参与资格的美国雇主。该外国人须列明美国雇主名称,并提供其E-Verify公司识别号。这项新要求将增加申请续签就业授权的外国人群体的时间负担。
For aliens who remain eligible to be employment authorized, the proposed rule would increase the time burden on the population of aliens applying for employment authorization. This rule proposes to add filing procedures and evidentiary requirements for aliens granted deferred action who are seeking initial employment authorization or renewing employment authorization. Currently, without this rule, aliens applying for employment authorization under the (c)(14), deferred action category, must complete Form I-765WS to determine if the alien has an economic need to work. The time burden for completing this worksheet is not included in this analysis since it is already a requirement for this population. However, the proposed rule would require documentary evidence to support the financial information provided in Form I-765WS.[267] For aliens granted deferred action and who remain eligible to be employment authorized, the proposed rule would require an alien applying for a (c)(14) renewal to show that he or she is employed or is seeking employment with a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify. The alien would be required to list the U.S. employer's name and supply its E-Verify Company Identification Number. This new requirement would increase the time burden on the population of aliens applying for renewal of employment authorization.
美国国土安全部估计,填写I-765表所需的时间负担为4.38小时。[268] 对于在本规则(如最终确定)生效日期后申请就业授权的获准延期遣返行动外国人而言,(印刷页第34444页)本拟议规则将使I-765表的时间负担增加30分钟(0.5小时),总计达到4.88小时。[269] 该时间负担的增加,是由于拟议的监管要求规定,外国人必须受雇于在E-Verify系统中保持良好参与资格的美国雇主,方可具备续签就业授权的资格。根据有效最低小时薪酬计算,这一变化将使每份申请的时间机会成本增加约10.13美元,根据所有职业的平均薪酬计算,则增加约22.83美元。[270]
DHS estimates the time burden for completing Form I-765 is 4.38 hours.[268] For aliens granted deferred action who apply for employment authorization after the effective date of this rule ( printed page 34444) should it be finalized, this proposed rule would increase the time burden of Form I-765 by 30 minutes (0.5 hours) for a total of 4.88 hours.[269] The increased time burden is due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens be employed by a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify to be eligible for a renewal of employment authorization. This change would increase the opportunity cost of time for each application by approximately $10.13 based on the effective minimum hourly compensation and by about $22.83 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[270]
目前,获得暂缓遣返行动且符合资格并申请就业许可的外国人,须填写并提交I-765WS表。目前,填写I-765WS表的预估时间负担为每份30分钟(0.5小时)。[271]然而,由于拟议的法规要求外国人就其在I-765WS表中申报的财务记录提供补充文件,该时间负担将增加30分钟(0.5小时),使填写该表的新时间负担达到1小时。对于在本规则如最终定稿后生效之日起继续符合资格并申请就业许可的获得暂缓遣返行动的外国人而言,拟议规则将使每份申请的时间机会成本按有效最低小时报酬计算增加约10.13美元,按所有职业的平均报酬计算增加约22.83美元。[272]将填写I-765表和I-765WS表的新增成本合并计算,每名外国人增加的时间负担在较低和较高两档报酬率下将分别增加20.26美元和45.65美元的成本。273
Aliens granted deferred action, who are eligible and apply for employment authorization, are currently required to complete and submit Form I-765WS. The estimated time burden for completing Form I-765WS is currently 30 minutes (0.5 hours) per response.[271] However, due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens provide supplementary documentation for their financial records reported in Form I-765WS, the time burden will increase 30 minutes (0.5 hours), making the new time burden to complete the form 1 hour. For aliens granted deferred action who continue to be eligible and apply for employment authorization after the effective date of the rule should it be finalized, the proposed rule would increase the opportunity cost of time for each application by approximately $10.13 based on the effective minimum hourly compensation and by about $22.83 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[272] Combining the new costs of completing Form I-765 and Form I-765WS, the total per alien increased time burden would add costs of $20.26 and $45.65 at the respective lower and upper bound compensation rates.273
表V.28列出了针对初次受理和续期受理两个人口范围填写I-765表和I-765WS表所产生的额外申请时间机会成本。表V.28(A)根据20.26美元的较低额外时间机会成本,列出了续期I-765表受理量下限和上限范围的成本估算。B列和D列中的下限和上限预计受理量,系依据(c)(14)“人口”部分表V.15中本规则下的下限和上限批准量。C列和E列的总成本给出了按较低报酬率计算的未贴现成本范围。同样,表V.28(B)根据45.65美元的较高额外时间机会成本,重复列出了续期受理量下限和上限范围的估算。
Table V.28 shows the additional filing opportunity cost of time for filing Form I-765 and Form I-765WS for the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts. Table V.28(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of renewal Form I-765 receipts based on the lower bound additional opportunity cost of time of $20.26. The lower and upper bound projected receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.15 in the (c)(14) “Population” section. The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound compensation. Similarly, Table V.28(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of renewal receipts based on the upper bound additional opportunity cost of time of $45.65.

如表中所示,在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间内,因填写表格的时间负担增加而产生的预估时间机会成本总额,预计在约220万美元至840万美元之间。按7%的贴现率年化计算,填写I-765表和I-765WS表产生的预估额外成本将在235,496美元至897,077美元之间(表V.30)。按3%的贴现率年化计算,填写I-765表和I-765WS表产生的预估额外成本将在227,734美元至866,282美元之间(表V.30)。
As indicated in the table, the estimated total opportunity costs of time incurred as a result of the increased time burden for completing the forms over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034 would range from about $2.2 million to $8.4 million. Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated additional costs to complete Forms I-765 and I-765WS would range from $235,496 to $897,077 (Table V.30). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated additional costs to complete Forms I-765 and I-765WS would range from $227,734 to $866,282 (Table V.30).
四、税收
iv. Taxes
本拟议规则短期内可能减少向联邦政府缴纳的税款(一种转移支付)。在原本可由(或曾由)(c)(14)类外国劳工担任的职位(初次申请)或曾由其担任的职位(续期申请)处于空缺期间,联邦政府将无法征收相关税款。
This proposed rule could reduce taxes paid to the Federal Government (a transfer payment) in the short term. During the period of vacancy for a job that could be (initial) or formerly (renewal) held by the (c)(14) alien worker, the Federal Government would not be collecting taxes.
如果企业无法为不再具备工作许可资格的受影响外国人原本将占据的职位找到劳动力,那么未履行的劳动将导致雇主和雇员向各级政府缴纳的税款减少。因此,(c)(14)“收入”部分所述的收入损失将导致此类税收减少。
If businesses cannot find labor for the positions the affected aliens who are no longer eligible for work authorization would have occupied, then the unperformed labor would result in a reduction in taxes from employers and employees to governments. Accordingly, the lost earnings derived in the (c)(14) “Earnings” section will contribute to such a reduction in taxes paid.
就本分析而言,国土安全部(DHS)采用与(c)(18)“税收”部分所述相同的、对就业税(即医疗保险税和社会保障税)可能产生的潜在影响。国土安全部以未计负担的收入为基础估算税收影响。计算方法为:将稳定后的收入乘以15.3%的就业税率,再将所得结果除以1.45的福利负担倍数。[274]
DHS uses the same potential contributory effects on employment taxes, namely Medicare and Social Security, as described in the (c)(18) “Taxes” section for this analysis. DHS estimates the tax impacts on the unburdened earnings basis. We calculate this by multiplying the stabilized earnings by the employment tax rate of 15.3 percent and dividing the resulting product by the benefits burden multiple of 1.45.[274]
为估算就业税损失的范围,我们采用表V.26 G列和H列中按现行报酬率和平均报酬率计算的初次申请和续期申请预计申请人的预估收入损失,将每年数值乘以15.3%,再除以1.45。这些计算结果列于表V.29中。税收影响的实际数值,将取决于在没有本规则的情况下,企业原本能够轻易为多少受影响的就业许可文件(EAD)持有者找到合理劳动力替代者的数量。
To estimate the range of employment tax losses, we take the estimated lost earnings for the range of initial and renewal projected filers at the prevailing and average compensation rates from Table V.26, columns G and H, multiply each year by 15.3 percent, and divide by 1.45. These calculations are shown in Table V.29. The actual value of tax impacts would depend on the number of affected EAD holders that businesses would have been able to easily find reasonable labor substitutes for in the absence of this rule.
(印刷页 34447)
( printed page 34447)
(印刷页 34448)
( printed page 34448)
DHS估计,在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间,收入损失可能介于13亿美元至87亿美元之间,[275]这将导致就业税收或转移支付相应减少,减少幅度介于1.339亿美元至9.207亿美元之间。[276]按7%的贴现率年化计算,就业税收损失将介于约1430万美元至9840万美元之间(表V.30)。按3%的贴现率年化计算,就业税收的减少将介于约1380万美元至9480万美元之间(表V.30)。同样,根据雇员的具体情况,可能还存在未在此估算的额外联邦所得税损失。也可能存在州和地方所得税损失,其金额因司法管辖区而异,但国土安全部(DHS)无法对此进行量化。我们注意到,税收转移潜在减少的影响仅适用于薪酬影响,不适用于劳动力流动成本、与表格填写时间负担相关的成本,或E-Verify系统的实施和使用成本。
Lost earnings, which DHS estimates could range between $1.3 billion to $8.7 billion over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034,[275] would result in corresponding reduction in employment taxes or transfers ranging between $133.9 million and $920.7 million.[276] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, employment tax losses would range from approximately $14.3 million to $98.4 million (Table V.30). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the reduction in employment taxes would range from approximately $13.8 million to $94.8 million (Table V.30). Again, depending on the circumstances of the employee, there could be additional Federal income tax losses not estimated here. There may also be State and local income tax losses that would vary according to the jurisdiction, but that DHS is unable to quantify. We note that the potential decrease in tax transfers only applies to the compensation impacts, not to labor turnover costs, costs associated with form time burdens, or implementation and use of E-Verify.
v. 总计
v. Total
在前述(c)(14)“货币化影响分析”各节中,我们列出了拟议规则对丧失劳动收入、生物特征信息提交、填写表格所需时间负担增加以及以减税形式体现的转移支付等方面影响的估算。总影响是通过汇总表V.26至表V.29中最初和续期影响的最大值和最小值估算得出的。因此,表V.30显示了拟议规则估计货币化成本的范围,其中表V.30(A)列出了最大估算值,表V.30(B)列出了最小估算值。对于表格的每一小节,均提供了未贴现的10年期总值,以及按7%和3%贴现率计算的现值成本和年化成本。
In the previous (c)(14) “Monetized Impact Analysis” sections we presented estimates of the impacts of the proposed rule germane to lost labor earnings, biometrics submission, increased time burdens for completing forms, and transfer payments in the form of reduced taxes. The total impacts are aggregated by summing the total initial and renewal impacts from Tables V.26 through V.29 in terms of the maximum and minimum estimates. Therefore, Table V.30 shows the range of estimated monetized costs of the proposed rule, where Table V.30(A) presents the maximum estimates, and Table V.30(B) presents the minimum estimates. For each subsection of the table, the 10-year totals are provided in undiscounted 10-year total values, as well as the present value costs and annualized costs discounted at 7 percent and 3 percent.

如表V.30所示,在所采用的假设下,拟议规则在2025财年至2034财年期间预计的10年期未贴现货币化成本最高可达约88亿美元,最低成本估计为13亿美元。[277]如果公司无法为获得延期遣返行动的外国人所担任的职位找到合理的劳动力替代者,本规则的大部分成本将源于劳动收入的损失。国土安全部指出,上述估算中未反映出一些无法量化的成本。
As Table V.30 shows, the projected 10-year monetized undiscounted costs of the proposed rule for the period FY 2025 through FY 2034 could be as high as about $8.8 billion with a minimum cost estimate of $1.3 billion under the assumptions relied on.[277] The majority of the costs of this rule would result from lost labor earnings, if companies are unable to find reasonable labor substitutes for the positions that the aliens granted deferred action would have filled. DHS notes there are unquantified costs not reflected in the estimates above.
c. 获准假释进入美国的外国人的酌情就业授权((c)(11))
c. Discretionary Employment Authorization for Aliens Paroled Into the United States ((c)(11))
i. 收入
i. Earnings
国土安全部没有关于获得假释许可的外国人(无论是在最初阶段还是续期阶段)的工资或职业信息,因为这些外国劳工获得的是开放市场就业授权文件(EAD),不包含也不要求提供任何有关其就业情况的数据。由于许多申请(c)(11)类别的外国人与申请(c)(18)类别的外国人具有许多相同的特征,例如均为相对新进入劳动力市场者,且有效期均不超过一年等,国土安全部采用了与(c)(18)“收入”一节中所述相同的工资和收入估算方法。对于每名外国人,所使用的估计年收入下限为36,144美元,上限为81,440美元。
DHS has no information on wages or occupations of aliens who have been granted parole, at the initial or renewal stage, since these alien workers obtain an open-market EAD that does not include or require any data on their employment. Because many of the aliens applying for (c)(11) would have many of the same characteristics as aliens applying for (c)(18), such as being relatively new entrants to the labor force and having a validity period not exceeding one year, etc., DHS used the same wage and earnings estimates described in the (c)(18) “Earnings” section. For each alien, the estimated annual lower bound earnings used is $36,144 and the upper bound earnings is $81,440.
表V.31显示了针对获准假释的外国人,在两个工资估算范围下,最初批准和续期批准的两个估计人口范围,以及相应的潜在收入损失。关于本规则下估计的批准人数(列于A列和C列)以及无本规则情况下估计的基线申请人数(列于E列),对可能影响的评估依赖于这样一个假设,即所有在(c)(11)类别下获得就业授权批准的人都进入了劳动力市场。这一假设是合理的,因为除少数例外情况外,外国人如果不期望获得经济利益回报,通常不会为申请就业授权(对于需要缴纳申请费的相关就业授权类别而言)而承担直接申请费用和与时间相关的机会成本。然而,实际上,外国人可能由于与本行动不特别相关的其他多种原因而未能就业。
Table V.31 shows the two estimated population ranges for initial and renewal approvals for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens granted parole and the corresponding potential lost earnings. Regarding the estimated approvals under this rule reported in Columns A and C and the estimated baseline filers without this rule reported in Column E, the assessments of possible impacts rely on the assumption that everyone who was approved for employment authorization under the (c)(11) category entered the labor force. This assumption is justifiable because aliens, with few exceptions, generally would not have expended the direct filing (for the pertinent employment authorization categories in which there is a filing fee) and time-related opportunity costs associated with applying for employment authorization if the aliens did not expect to recoup an economic benefit. Realistically, however, aliens might not be employed for any number of other reasons not specifically relevant to this action.
截至2024年12月,全国失业率为4.1%。[278]即使在失业率较低的情况下,劳动力市场中仍存在持续且相当大的工作岗位流动。外国人可能因这种正常的岗位流动或因任何数量的具体案件因素和条件而失业。因此,我们认为按比例推算表V.20中的人口规模(即初次和续签基线申请量、本规则下初次和续签批准量的下限和上限)并将其列入表V.31的A、C、E栏,以反映当前失业情况,这是合理的做法,其计算方法是将就业率(即1减去0.041)纳入计算,得出0.959。[279]
The national unemployment rate as of December 2024 was 4.1 percent.[278] There is constant and considerable job turnover in the labor market even when the unemployment rate is low. Aliens could be unemployed due to this normal turnover or from any number of case-specific factors and conditions. As such, we believe it is reasonable to project scaled populations (from Table V.20: initial and renewal baseline receipts, lower bound initial and renewal approvals under this rule, and upper bound initial and renewal approvals under this rule) in Table V.31 Columns A, C, and E to account for current unemployment, which is conducted by integrating the employment rate, as unity minus 0.041, to arrive at 0.959.[279]
表V.31(A)显示了基于下限工资年收入36,144美元的初次就业授权批准量下限和上限范围的成本估算。根据(c)(11)“人口”部分所制定的预测,本规则下各类人群的总收入列于B、D、F栏。G栏和H栏显示了潜在收入损失,其计算方法是将本规则下人群的潜在收入(B栏和D栏)与当前基线(F栏)相减。同样,表V.31(B)根据上限(平均)工资年收入81,440美元,重复列出了初次就业授权批准量下限和上限范围的估算。表V.31(C)和表V.31(D)分别根据下限和上限工资年收入,重复列出了表V.31(A)和表V.31(B)中针对续签就业授权批准量下限和上限范围的估算。
Table V.31(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the lower bound wage annual earnings of $36,144. The total earnings for each population under the rule based on the projections developed in the (c)(11) “Population” section is reported in Columns B, D and F. Columns G and H present the potential lost earnings, by subtracting, the potential earnings from rule populations (Columns B and D) from the current baseline (Column F). Similarly, Table V.31(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial employment authorization approvals based on the upper bound (average) wage annual earnings of $81,440. Tables V.31(C) and V.31(D) repeat the estimates from Table V.31(A) and V.31(B) for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal employment authorization approvals based on the lower and upper bound wage annual earnings, respectively.
(印刷页34451)
( printed page 34451)
(印刷页34452)
( printed page 34452)
国土安全部(DHS)将获准假释入境的外国人的收入损失作为衡量取消(c)(11)类别就业授权资格总体影响的指标,无论是作为分配性影响(转移支付),还是作为企业生产力损失成本的替代指标。该指标不包括企业因利润损失和机会成本而产生的额外成本,也不包括对外国人支持网络中其他人员的分配性影响。然而,本分析将进一步讨论这些成本。如表V.31所示,潜在收入损失取决于获准假释且仍符合就业授权资格并继续工作的外国人数量,以及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间,总收入损失将在3.736亿美元至12亿美元之间。[280]按7%贴现率折算为年化值,(印刷页34453)初次和续签EAD持有者的收入损失将在4,200万美元至1.324亿美元之间(表V.35)。[281]按3%贴现率折算为年化值,初次和续签EAD持有者的收入损失将在3,940万美元至1.25亿美元之间(表V.35)。
DHS uses the lost compensation to aliens granted parole as a measure of the overall impact of removing eligibility for employment authorization under the (c)(11) category—either as distributional impacts (transfers) or as a proxy for costs to businesses for lost productivity. It does not include additional costs to businesses for lost profits and opportunity costs or the distributional impacts for those in an alien's support network. However, these costs will be discussed further in this analysis. As shown in Table V.31, the potential lost earnings depend on the number of aliens granted parole who remain eligible for employment authorization and continue to work, as well as their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034, the total lost earnings would range from $373.6 million to $1.2 billion.[280] Annualized at ( printed page 34453) a 7 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $42.0 million to $132.4 million (Table V.35).281 Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, lost earnings for initial and renewal EAD holders would range from $39.4 million to $125.0 million (Table V.35).
根据本规则拟议的资格标准,不再符合续签就业授权资格的EAD持有者将遭受收入损失。此外,国土安全部承认,如果申请续签的外国人的雇主目前未参加E-Verify计划且选择不参加该计划,则这些外国人可能遭受额外的收入损失。在这种情况下,申请续签的外国人如果无法找到参加E-Verify计划的雇主提供的工作,则可能蒙受收入损失。
EAD holders who would no longer be eligible to renew their employment authorization under the proposed eligibility criteria in this rule would incur lost earnings. Additionally, DHS acknowledges the potential for additional lost compensation to aliens applying for renewal if their employers are not currently enrolled in E-Verify and opt not to enroll in the E-Verify program. In such cases, aliens applying for renewal could lose earnings if they are unable to find employment with an employer who participates in E-Verify.
国土安全部认识到,在排除通货膨胀影响的情况下,收入通常会随时间推移而上升,EAD持有者未来的收入可能高于本分析中的估算值。此外,由于就业授权续签必然在初次就业授权批准之后进行,随着时间推移,续签所获得的工资收入及总薪酬可能更高。因此,这一因素可能使收入损失的估算值产生向下偏差。然而,我们目前尚未找到切实可行的方法将这种可能性纳入量化估算之中。
DHS recognizes that, excluding the effects of inflation, earnings generally rise over time and the earnings of EAD holders could be higher in the future than estimated in this analysis. Moreover, since employment authorization renewals necessarily follow initial employment authorization approvals, in time, wages earned and, hence total compensation, could be higher for renewals. Accordingly, this effect could have a downward bias in the estimate of earnings losses. However, we see no tractable way at present to incorporate this possibility into the quantified estimates.
除上述量化影响外，被准予假释的外国人如不再具备获得就业授权的资格，还可能产生定性影响。对于(c)(11)类人群中无法续签就业授权或无法获得初次就业授权的人员而言，很可能会因收入损失而受到影响，这可能造成经济困难。此类人群可能需要依靠其支持网络获得经济和社会援助，这可能包括但不限于家庭成员和朋友、宗教和慈善组织、私营非营利性服务提供者、州和地方政府以及非政府组织（NGO）。美国国土安全部（DHS）认为，本规则对外国人支持网络造成的直接间接影响，很可能不会显著超过该外国人在没有本规则情况下本应获得的工资和福利。
In addition to the above quantified impacts, there could be qualitative impacts for aliens granted parole who would no longer be eligible for employment authorization. For the (c)(11) population that would not be able to renew their employment authorization or obtain an initial employment authorization, there would likely be an impact in terms of lost income, which could pose economic hardships. Members of this population may need to rely on their support networks for financial and social assistance, which could involve, but may not be limited to, family members and friends, religious and charitable organizations, private non-profit providers, State and local governments, and NGOs. DHS believes that the immediate indirect impact of this rule to an alien's support network is likely not significantly more than the wages and benefits the alien would have earned without this rule.
二、生物识别信息
ii. Biometrics
美国国土安全部（DHS）现行法规赋予美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）一般性权力，可根据具体情况要求任何申请移民福利的外国人提交生物识别信息。当USCIS确定申请(c)(11)类别的外国人需要提交生物识别信息时，该外国人将收到USCIS发出的生物识别服务预约通知，前往申请支持中心（ASC）提交生物识别信息，以协助身份核实并促进(c)(11)类就业授权文件（EAD）卡的制作等。[282] DHS拟将提交生物识别信息的要求予以编纂为法规，要求所有以(c)(11)类别提交I-765表格的外国人，无论是初次申请还是续签申请，均须前往申请支持中心（ASC）提交生物识别信息。DHS拟利用申请(c)(11)类别的外国人提交的生物识别信息进行犯罪记录筛查。[283] 由于申请(c)(11)类别的外国人很可能面临与(c)(18)“生物识别信息”部分所述类似的费用，根据本分析所采用的较低和较高两种工资水平，每名外国人提交生物识别信息的费用分别为109.35美元和202.54美元。
Current DHS regulations provide general authority for USCIS to require the submission of biometrics from any alien filing for an immigration benefit on a case-by-case basis. When USCIS determines that an alien applying for (c)(11) is required to submit biometrics, they receive a biometrics services appointment notice from USCIS to submit biometrics at an ASC to assist in identity verification and facilitate (c)(11) EAD card production among other things.[282] DHS is proposing to codify the requirement to submit biometrics, where all aliens who file Form I-765 under the (c)(11) category—for both initial and renewal applications—would be required to appear at an ASC and submit biometrics. DHS proposes to use the biometrics submitted by aliens applying for (c)(11) to screen for criminal history.[283] Because aliens applying for (c)(11) would likely experience similar costs to those outlined in the (c)(18) “Biometrics” section, there is a per alien biometrics submission cost of $109.35 and $202.54 at the respective lower and upper wage rates for this analysis.
表V.32显示了在两种工资水平估算范围下，初次申请和续签申请的两个人群范围，以及提交生物识别信息的相应总费用。表V.32(A)显示了在较低提交费用109.35美元的情况下，初次提交I-765表格的下限和上限人数范围的费用估算。B栏和D栏中的下限和上限生物识别信息预计接收数，为来自(c)(11)“人群”部分表V.20中本规则下的下限和上限批准数。[284] C栏和E栏的总费用提供了下限估算下未折现费用的范围。同样，表V.32(B)基于较高提交费用202.54美元，重复了初次提交I-765表格的下限和上限范围的估算。表V.32(C)和表V.32(D)分别基于较低和较高提交费用，重复了续签I-765表格下限和上限范围的估算。
Table V.32 shows the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts for the two ranges of wage estimates for aliens granted parole and the corresponding total cost to submit biometrics. Table V.32(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts at the lower bound submission cost of $109.35. The lower and upper bound projected biometrics receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.20 in the (c)(11) “Population” section.[284] The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound. Similarly, Table V.32(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts based on the upper bound submission cost of $202.54. Tables V.32(C) and V.32(D) repeat these estimates for the lower and upper bound ranges of renewal Form I-765 receipts based on the lower and upper bound submission costs, respectively.

如表V.32所示，提交生物识别信息的费用取决于申请就业授权的被准予假释外国人数量及其工资水平。在2025财年至2034财年的10年期间内，提交生物识别信息的估计总费用预计在1,850万美元至3,520万美元之间。[285] 按7%的折现率年化计算，提交生物识别信息的估计费用预计在210万美元至390万美元之间（表V.35）。按3%的折现率年化计算，提交生物识别信息的估计费用预计在190万美元至370万美元之间（表V.35）。DHS认识到，所列估计数可能偏高，因为该人群中有一部分已收到生物识别服务预约通知并完成生物识别信息提交。根据本拟议规则的新要求，该部分人群将继续完成生物识别信息提交，因此不会产生新的费用。
As shown in Table V.32, the cost to submit biometrics depends on the number of aliens granted parole who apply for employment authorization and their wage rate. Over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034, the estimated total cost to submit biometrics would range from $18.5 million to $35.2 million.[285] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $2.1 million to $3.9 million (Table V.35). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated costs to submit biometrics would range from $1.9 million to $3.7 million (Table V.35). DHS recognizes that the estimates presented may be an overestimate, as a portion of the population already receives a biometrics services appointment notice and completes biometrics submission. Under the new requirement of this proposed rule, this portion of the population would continue to complete biometrics, and thus not incur new costs.
三、表格
iii. Forms
对于仍符合就业许可资格的外国人而言,拟议规则将增加申请就业许可的外国人群体的时间负担。本规则还建议为获得假释并寻求初次就业许可或续签就业许可的外国人增加申请程序和证据要求。拟议的新要求包括提交I-765WS表及相关证明文件[286],以证明该外国人存在就业的经济必要性,并且仅对申请续签的外国人而言,还需提供其在E-Verify系统中登记的美国雇主名称及该雇主的E-Verify公司识别号。
For aliens who remain eligible to be employment authorized, the proposed rule would increase the time burden on the population of aliens applying for employment authorization. This rule also proposes to add filing procedures and evidentiary requirements for aliens granted parole who are seeking initial employment authorization or renewing employment authorization. The proposed new requirements include submitting Form I-765WS along with supporting documentation [286] to establish the alien's economic necessity for employment and, for aliens applying for renewal only, the name of the alien's U.S. employer as listed in E-Verify and that employer's E-Verify Company Identification Number.
美国国土安全部(DHS)估计填写I-765表的时间负担为4.38小时。[287]对于本规则生效后(如最终定稿)申请就业许可的获假释外国人而言,本拟议规则将使I-765表的时间负担增加30分钟(0.5小时),总计达到4.88小时。[288]此时间负担增加是由于拟议的监管要求,即外国人必须受雇于在E-Verify系统中信誉良好的美国雇主,方可获得就业许可续签资格。根据有效最低小时薪酬计算,这一变化将使每份申请的时间机会成本增加约10.13美元(第34456页印刷版);根据所有职业的平均薪酬计算,则增加约22.83美元。[289]
DHS estimates the time burden for completing Form I-765 is 4.38 hours.[287] For aliens granted parole who apply for employment authorization after the effective date of this rule should it be finalized, this proposed rule would increase the time burden of Form I-765 by 30 minutes (0.5 hours) for a total of 4.88 hours.[288] The increased time burden is due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens be employed by a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify to be eligible for a renewal of employment authorization. This change would increase the opportunity cost of time for each application by approximately $10.13 based on the effective minimum hourly ( printed page 34456) compensation and by about $22.83 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[289]
本拟议规则还将规定,根据(c)(11)类别申请就业许可的外国人必须提交I-765WS表。目前,证明存在就业经济必要性仅作为可酌情考虑的因素列出,并非(c)(11)类别下的强制要求。在本拟议规则中,国土安全部将把这一要求设为强制性要求。国土安全部估计,目前填写I-765WS表的时间负担为30分钟(0.5小时)。[290]然而,由于拟议的监管要求规定外国人须就I-765WS表中申报的财务记录提供补充证明文件,该表格的时间负担将增加30分钟(0.5小时),使填写该表格的新时间负担达到1小时。对于本规则生效后(如最终定稿)继续符合资格并申请就业许可的获假释外国人而言,按有效最低小时薪酬计算,拟议规则将使每名外国人的时间机会成本增加20.26美元;按所有职业的平均薪酬计算,则增加45.65美元。[291]
This proposed rule would also make it a requirement to submit Form I-765WS for aliens applying for employment authorization under the (c)(11) category. Currently, proving the existence of economic necessity to be employed is listed as a discretionary factor for consideration, but it is not a requirement under the (c)(11) category. In this proposed rule, DHS would make this a mandatory requirement. DHS estimates the current time burden for completing Form I-765WS is 30 minutes (0.5 hours).[290] However, due to the proposed regulatory requirement that aliens provide supplementary documentation for their financial records reported in Form I-765WS, the time burden will increase 30 minutes (0.5 hours), making the new time burden to complete the form 1 hour. For aliens granted parole who continue to be eligible and apply for employment authorization after the effective date of the rule should it be finalized, the proposed rule would increase the opportunity cost of time for each alien by $20.26 based on the effective minimum hourly compensation and $45.65 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[291]
此外,本拟议规则还将影响I-131表的填报负担,该表格是目前根据(c)(11)类别申请就业许可的外国人须填写的表格。由于本规则拟议的监管要求规定外国人须受雇于在E-Verify系统中信誉良好的美国雇主方可获得就业许可续签资格,且须就I-765WS表中申报的财务记录提供补充证明文件,I-131表将相应更新以反映这些新要求。填写I-131表目前的时间负担为2.917小时,但在拟议的监管变更下将增加至3.167小时,相差15分钟(0.25小时)。由于这些变化,按有效最低小时薪酬计算,每名外国人的时间机会成本将增加5.07美元;按所有职业的平均薪酬计算,则增加11.41美元。[292]综合I-765表、I-765WS表和I-131表的新增成本,按较低和较高薪酬标准计算,每名外国人增加的时间负担成本分别为35.46美元和79.89美元。[293]
Additionally, this proposed rule would impact the reporting burden for Form I-131, a form aliens seeking employment authorization under the (c)(11) category are currently required to complete. Due to the proposed regulatory requirements in this rule that aliens be employed by a U.S. employer who is a participant in good standing in E-Verify to be eligible for a renewal of employment authorization and that aliens provide supplementary documentation for their financial records reported in Form I-765WS, Form I-131 would be updated to reflect these new requirements. The time burden to complete Form I-131 is currently 2.917 hours but would increase to 3.167 hours under the proposed regulatory changes, a 15-minute (0.25 hour) difference. The opportunity cost of time for each alien due to these changes would increase by $5.07 based on the effective minimum hourly compensation and $11.41 based on the average compensation for all occupations.[292] Combining the new costs of the Forms I-765, I-765WS, and I-131, the total per alien increased time burden would add costs of $35.46 and $79.89 at the respective lower and upper bound compensation rates.[293]
表 V.33 显示了针对初次受理和续期受理两个人口范围填写 I-765 表、I-765WS 表和 I-131 表所产生的额外时间机会成本。表 V.33(A) 显示了基于每小时 35.46 美元的下限额外时间机会成本,初次 I-765 表受理量下限和上限范围的成本估算。B 列和 D 列中的下限和上限预计表格受理量,是根据(c)(11)“人口”部分表 V.20 中本规则下的下限和上限批准量得出的。C 列和 E 列的总成本提供了下限补偿情形下未贴现成本的范围。同样,表 V.33(B) 基于每小时 79.89 美元的上限额外时间机会成本,重复列出了初次 EAD 受理量下限和上限范围的估算。表 V.33(C) 和表 V.33(D) 分别重复列出了下限和上限工资时间机会成本的估算。
Table V.33 shows the additional opportunity cost of time for filing Form I-765, Form I-765WS, and Form I-131 for the two population ranges for initial and renewal receipts. Table V.33(A) shows cost estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial Form I-765 receipts based on the lower bound additional opportunity cost of time of $35.46. The lower and upper bound projected forms receipts in Columns B and D are the lower and upper bound approvals under this rule from Table V.20 in the (c)(11) “Population” section. The total costs for Columns C and E provide the range of undiscounted costs for the lower bound compensation. Similarly, Table V.33(B) repeats the estimates for the lower and upper bound range of initial EAD receipts based on the upper bound additional opportunity cost of time of $79.89. Tables V.33(C) and V.33(D) repeat on the lower and upper bound wage opportunity cost of time, respectively.

如表中所示,在 2025 财年至 2034 财年这十年期间,因完成表格所需时间增加而产生的估计总时间机会成本约为 600 万美元至 1390 万美元不等。[294] 以 7% 的贴现率折算为年化值,完成 I-765 表、I-765WS 表和 I-131 表所需的估计额外成本将在 673,757 美元至 160 万美元之间(表 V.35)。以 3% 的贴现率折算为年化值,完成 I-765 表、I-765WS 表和 I-131 表所需的估计额外成本将在 632,291 美元至 150 万美元之间(表 V.35)。
As indicated in the table, the estimated total opportunity costs of time incurred as a result of the increased time burden for completing the forms over the 10-year period from FY 2025 to FY 2034 would range from about $6.0 million to $13.9 million.[294] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, the estimated additional costs to complete Form I-765, Form I-765WS, and Form I-131 would range from $673,757 to $1.6 million (Table V.35). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, the estimated additional costs to complete Form I-765, Form I-765WS, and Form I-131 would range from $632,291 to $1.5 million (Table V.35).
四、税收
iv. Taxes
本拟议规则短期内可能减少向联邦政府缴纳的税款(一种转移支付)。在原本可由(c)(11)外籍劳工担任的职位(初次)或曾经担任的职位(续期)处于空缺期间,联邦政府将无法征收相应税款。
This proposed rule could reduce taxes paid to the Federal Government (a transfer payment) in the short term. During the period of vacancy for a job that could be (initial) or formerly (renewal) held by the (c)(11) alien worker, the Federal Government would not be collecting taxes.
如果企业无法为那些因不再具备工作授权资格的受影响外国人原本将填补的职位找到替代劳动力,则未履行的劳动将导致雇主和雇员向各级政府缴纳的税款减少。因此,(c)(11)“收入”部分中得出的收入损失将导致此类税收减少。
If businesses cannot find labor for the positions the affected aliens who are no longer eligible for work authorization would have occupied, then the unperformed labor would result in a reduction in taxes from employers and employees to governments. Accordingly, the lost earnings derived in the (c)(11) “Earnings” section will contribute to such a reduction in taxes paid.
就本分析而言,国土安全部(DHS)采用与(c)(18)“税收”部分所述相同的、对就业税(即医疗保险税和社会保障税)可能产生的潜在影响。国土安全部基于未计负担的收入估算税收影响。具体计算方法为:将稳定后的收入乘以 15.3% 的就业税率,再将所得乘积除以 1.45 的福利负担倍数。[295]
DHS uses the same potential contributory effects on employment taxes, namely Medicare and Social Security, as described in the (c)(18) “Taxes” section for this analysis. DHS estimates the tax impacts on the unburdened earnings basis. We calculate this by multiplying the stabilized earnings by the employment tax rate of 15.3 percent and dividing the resulting product by the benefits burden multiple of 1.45.[295]
为估算就业税损失的范围,我们采用表 V.31 G 列和 H 列中按现行工资率和平均薪酬水平计算的初次及续期预计申请人的估计收入损失,将每年的数值乘以 15.3%,再除以 1.45。这些计算结果显示于表 V.34 中。税收影响的实际数值将取决于在没有本规则的情况下,企业能够轻易为多少受影响的 EAD 持有人找到合理替代劳动力。
To estimate the range of employment tax losses, we take the estimated lost earnings for the range of initial and renewal projected filers at the prevailing and average compensation rates from Table V.31, columns G and H, multiply each year by 15.3 percent, and divide by 1.45. These calculations are shown in Table V.34. The actual value of tax impacts would depend on the number of affected EAD holders that businesses would have been able to easily find reasonable labor substitutes for in the absence of this rule.
(印刷页第 34459 页)
( printed page 34459)
(印刷页第 34460 页)
( printed page 34460)
国土安全部估计,在 2025 财年至 2034 财年这十年期间,收入损失可能在 3.736 亿美元至 12 亿美元之间,[296] 由此导致的相应就业税损失或转移支付将在 3940 万美元至 1.259 亿美元之间。[297] 以 7% 的贴现率折算为年化值,就业税损失约在 440 万美元至 1400 万美元之间(表 V.35)。以 3% 的贴现率折算为年化值,就业税损失约在 420 万美元至 1320 万美元之间(表 V.35)。此外,根据雇员的具体情况不同,可能还存在此处未估算的额外联邦所得税损失。此外,还可能存在州和地方所得税损失,其数额因司法管辖区而异,但国土安全部无法对此进行量化。我们注意到,税收转移支付的潜在减少仅适用于薪酬影响,不适用于劳动力更替成本、与表格填写时间负担相关的成本,或 E-Verify 系统的实施和使用成本。
Lost earnings, which DHS estimates could range between $373.6 million to $1.2 billion [296] over the 10-year period from FY 2025 through FY 2034, would result in corresponding employment tax losses or transfers ranging between $39.4 million and $125.9 million.[297] Annualized at a 7 percent discount rate, employment tax losses would range from approximately $4.4 million to $14.0 million (Table V.35). Annualized at a 3 percent discount rate, employment tax losses would range from approximately $4.2 million to $13.2 million (Table V.35). Again, depending on the circumstances of the employee, there could be additional Federal income tax losses not estimated here. There may also be State and local income tax losses that would vary according to the jurisdiction but that DHS is unable to quantify. We note that the potential decrease in tax transfers only applies to the compensation impacts, not to labor turnover costs, costs associated with the form time burdens, or implementation and use of E-Verify.
五、总计
v. Total
在之前的(c)(11)“货币化影响分析”部分,我们提出了拟议规则在以下方面相关影响的估算:劳动收入损失、生物特征信息采集、填写表格所增加的时间负担,以及以减税形式体现的转移支付。总影响是通过汇总表V.31至表V.34中初次及续期影响的最大值和最小值估算得出的。因此,表V.35列示了拟议规则估算货币化成本的范围,其中表V.35(A)列示最大估算值,表V.35(B)列示最小估算值。对于该表的每个小节,均提供了未贴现的10年合计总值,以及按7%和3%贴现率计算的现值成本和年度化成本。

The total impacts are aggregated by summing the total initial and renewal impacts from Tables V.31 through V.34 in terms of the maximum and minimum estimates. Therefore, Table V.35 shows the range of estimated monetized costs of the proposed rule, where Table V.35(A) presents the maximum estimates, and Table V.35(B) presents the minimum estimates. For each subsection of the table, the 10-year totals are provided in undiscounted 10-year total values, as well as the present value costs and annualized costs discounted at 7 percent and 3 percent.

d. 总计
d. Grand Total
在之前的“货币化影响分析”各部分中,我们提出了拟议规则在以下方面相关影响的估算,这些影响与所讨论的酌情就业许可文件(EAD)有关:劳动收入损失、生物特征信息采集、填写表格所增加的时间负担,以及以减税形式体现的转移支付。总计影响是通过汇总表V.25、表V.30和表V.35中初次及续期影响的最大值和最小值估算得出的。因此,表V.36列示了拟议规则估算货币化成本的范围,其中表V.36(A)列示最大估算值,表V.36(B)列示最小估算值。对于该表的每个小节,均提供了未贴现的10年合计总值,以及按7%和3%贴现率计算的现值成本和年度化成本。

The grand total impacts are aggregated by summing the total initial and renewal impacts from Tables V.25, V.30, and V.35 in terms of the maximum and minimum estimates. Therefore, Table V.36 shows the range of estimated monetized costs of the proposed rule, where Table V.36(A) presents the maximum estimates, and Table V.36(B) presents the minimum estimates. For each subsection of the table, the 10-year totals are provided in undiscounted 10-year total values, as well as the present value costs and annualized costs discounted at 7 percent and 3 percent.
